  'Tanzania':       [1,1], // Developing economy, small forces
  'Uganda':         [1,2], // Active military (LRA, AMISOM)
  'Somalia':        [1,1], // Fragile state
  'Mozambique':     [1,1], // Low development
  'Zambia':         [1,1], // Low development
  'Zimbabwe':       [1,1], // Economic crisis, degraded forces
  'Angola':         [2,2], // Oil wealth, Soviet-legacy inventory
  'Cameroon':       [1,1], // Limited resources
  // ── South & Central Asia ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
  'India':          [5,6], // World 5th GDP, Brahmos, nuclear arsenal
  'Pakistan':       [3,5], // Nuclear power, large army, JF-17
  'Afghanistan':    [1,1], // Collapsed state since 2021
};

function applyStartingTechs(scenId, playerCivName){
  const isTno=_isTnoScenario(scenId);
  const scen=SCENARIOS[scenId];
  const histYear=scen?.historicalYear||(scenId==='ww1_1914'?1914:scenId==='ww1_1917'?1917:0);
  const isHistorical=!!histYear;
  if(scenId !== 'modern' && !isTno && !isHistorical) return;

  // TNO imports are MAPS, not progressed saves. WW1 uses a hand-picked 1914
  // subset of the existing tree so future-era concepts never leak into 1914.
  const techFor=(name,isPlayer)=>{
    if(isTno) return _buildTechSet(3,3);
    if(isHistorical) return histYear===1914?_ww1TechForName(name):_historicalTechSet(histYear,name);
    const level=MODERN_NATION_TECHS[name];
    const econT=level?level[0]:(isPlayer?4:3);
    const milT =level?level[1]:(isPlayer?4:3);
    return _buildTechSet(econT,milT);
  };

  // ── Player techs ────────────────────────────────────────────────
  if(playerCivId >= 0){
    playerTechs.clear();
    for(const id of techFor(playerCivName,true)) playerTechs.add(id);
    _recomputeTechBonuses();
    _syncPlayerTechsToCiv();
    _divCapCache = -1;
    _clearFoxSpriteCache();
  }

  // ── AI civ techs — each civ gets its own starting tech set ─────
  for(const civ of civs){
    if(civ.id === playerCivId) continue;
    civ.techs = new Set(techFor(civ.name,false));
    _recomputeBotTechBonus(civ);
  }
}


// ── STARTING ALLIANCES ───────────────────────────────────────────────
// Creates realistic real-world alliances for supported scenarios.
// Called from startGame when the "Starting Alliances" toggle is on.
function applyStartingAlliances(scenId){
  const civByName = {};
  for(const civ of civs){civByName[civ.name]=civ.id;if(civ.tnoCanonicalName)civByName[civ.tnoCanonicalName]=civ.id;}
  const allianceNameAliases = Object.freeze({
    'United States':'United States of America','United States of America':'United States',
    'North Macedonia':'Macedonia','Macedonia':'North Macedonia',
    'Czech Republic':'Czechia','Czechia':'Czech Republic'
  });
  const civIdFor = name => civByName[name] ?? civByName[allianceNameAliases[name]];

  function makeAlliance(name, color, leaderName, memberNames){
    const leaderId = civIdFor(leaderName);
    if(leaderId === undefined) return null;
    if(getAllianceOfCiv(leaderId)) return null; // already in one
    const al = {
      id: _nextAllianceId++,
      name,
      leaderId,
      memberIds: new Set([leaderId]),
      color
    };
    alliances.push(al);
    for(const mName of memberNames){
      const mid = civIdFor(mName);
      if(mid === undefined || mid === leaderId) continue;
      if(getAllianceOfCiv(mid)) continue; // skip if already claimed
      al.memberIds.add(mid);
    }
    _touchAllianceMembership();
    _allianceLabelDirty = true;
    _allianceBorderDirty = true;
    return al;
  }

  if(scenId==='ww1_1914'){
    // Operational blocs at the 4 Aug 1914 start. Italy and the Ottoman Empire
    // deliberately begin outside these blocs: Italy remained neutral in 1914,
    // while the Ottoman Empire did not enter the war until later that year.
    makeAlliance('Entente Powers', '#3f6fa8', 'French Republic', [
      'United Kingdom','Russian Empire','Kingdom of Belgium','Kingdom of Serbia','Kingdom of Montenegro',
      // Dependencies are attached to their wartime side so game war-propagation
      // behaves like imperial control rather than treating colonies as neutral.
      'French Algeria','French Protectorate of Tunisia','French Protectorate of Morocco','French West Africa','French Equatorial Africa',
      'Khedivate of Egypt (British Occupation)','Anglo-Egyptian Sudan'
    ]);
    makeAlliance('Central Powers', '#8a6540', 'German Empire', ['Austria-Hungary']);
    return;
  }


  if(scenId==='ww1_1917'){
    makeAlliance('Entente Powers','#3f6fa8','French Republic',['United Kingdom','Russian Republic','Kingdom of Italy','Kingdom of Romania','Kingdom of Greece','Kingdom of Belgium','French Algeria','French Protectorate of Tunisia','French Protectorate of Morocco','French West Africa','French Equatorial Africa','Khedivate of Egypt (British Occupation)','Anglo-Egyptian Sudan']);
    makeAlliance('Central Powers','#8a6540','German Empire',['Austria-Hungary','Ottoman Empire','Kingdom of Bulgaria','Kingdom of Poland (Central Powers)']);
    return;
  }
  if(scenId==='hist1933'||scenId==='hist1936'){
    makeAlliance('Little Entente','#537ca8','Czechoslovakia',['Kingdom of Romania','Kingdom of Yugoslavia']);
    return;
  }
  if(scenId==='hist1939'){
    makeAlliance('Allied Powers','#3f6fa8','United Kingdom',['French Republic','Second Polish Republic']);
    makeAlliance('Axis Powers','#77705b','German Reich',['Kingdom of Italy','Slovakia','Kingdom of Hungary']);
    return;
  }
  if(scenId==='hist1942'){
    makeAlliance('Allied Powers','#3f6fa8','United Kingdom',['Soviet Union']);
    makeAlliance('Axis Powers','#77705b','German Reich',['Kingdom of Italy','Kingdom of Hungary','Kingdom of Romania','Kingdom of Bulgaria','Slovakia','Independent State of Croatia']);
    return;
  }
  if(scenId==='hist1945'){
    makeAlliance('Allied Occupation Powers','#5c79a0','United States of America',['United Kingdom','Provisional Government of the French Republic','Soviet Union']);
    return;
  }
  if(scenId==='hist1954'){
    makeAlliance('NATO','#2e6fbf','United States of America',['United Kingdom','French Republic (Fourth Republic)','Italian Republic','Kingdom of Greece','Republic of Turkey','Belgium','Netherlands','Luxembourg','Denmark','Norway','Iceland','Portugal']);
    return;
  }
  if(scenId==='hist1967'){
    makeAlliance('NATO','#2e6fbf','United States of America',['United Kingdom','Federal Republic of Germany','French Republic (Fifth Republic)','Italian Republic','Kingdom of Greece (military junta)','Republic of Turkey','Belgium','Netherlands','Luxembourg','Denmark','Norway','Iceland','Portuguese Republic (Estado Novo)']);
    makeAlliance('Warsaw Pact','#a43e3e','Soviet Union',['German Democratic Republic','Polish People’s Republic','Czechoslovak Socialist Republic','Hungarian People’s Republic','Socialist Republic of Romania','People’s Republic of Bulgaria']);
    return;
  }
  if(scenId==='hist1989'){
    makeAlliance('NATO','#2e6fbf','United States of America',['Canada','United Kingdom','Federal Republic of Germany','French Republic','Italian Republic','Kingdom of Spain','Portuguese Republic','Hellenic Republic','Republic of Turkey','Belgium','Netherlands','Luxembourg','Denmark','Norway','Iceland']);
    makeAlliance('Warsaw Pact','#a43e3e','Soviet Union',['German Democratic Republic','Polish People’s Republic','Czechoslovak Socialist Republic','Hungarian People’s Republic','Socialist Republic of Romania','People’s Republic of Bulgaria']);
    return;
  }

  if(scenId==='tno1962'){
    // Native 1962 is built from current wiki state names. NEVER route it through the
    // obsolete regional TNO_CIV_TEMPLATE / numeric-ID mapper: that path can append old
    // states and rename unrelated full-world civs after the map has already loaded.
    makeAlliance('Organization of Free Nations','#3f617c','United States of America',[
      'Dominion of Canada'
    ]);
    makeAlliance('Einheitspakt','#55504a','Greater Germanic Reich',[
      'United Kingdom of Great Britain','Republic of Ireland','French State',
      'Reichskommissariat Ukraine','Reichskommissariat Moskowien','Reichskommissariat Ostland','Reichskommissariat Kaukasien',
      'Kingdom of Denmark','Norwegian State'
    ]);
    makeAlliance('Greater East Asia Co-Prosperity Sphere','#8b3e45','Empire of Japan',[
      'Empire of Manchuria','Republic of China','State of Guangdong','Republic of Thailand',
      'Empire of Vietnam','Government of Free India','Republic of Indonesia',
      'North Borneo Military Administration','Second Philippine Republic','Japanese Antarctic Sector'
    ]);
    makeAlliance('Triumvirate','#7b765e','Italian Empire',[
      'Iberian Union','Republic of Turkey','Hellenic State'
    ]);
    return;
  }

  if(_isTnoScenario(scenId)){
    // Hidden legacy snapshots keep their own legacy mapper for save compatibility.
    const defs=TNO_SCENARIO_DATA[scenId]?.alliances||[];
    if(!_tnoCivIdMap)_prepareTnoCivIdMap();
    for(const ad of defs){
      const leaderId=_tnoLiveCivId(ad.leaderId);
      if(leaderId<0||!civs[leaderId])continue;
      const members=(ad.memberIds||[]).map(_tnoLiveCivId).filter(id=>id>=0&&civs[id]);
      const al={
        id:_nextAllianceId++,
        name:ad.name||'Alliance',
        leaderId,
        memberIds:new Set(members.length?members:[leaderId]),
        color:ad.color||allianceColor(alliances.length)
      };
      al.memberIds.add(leaderId);
      alliances.push(al);
    }
    _touchAllianceMembership();
    _allianceLabelDirty=true;
    _allianceBorderDirty=true;
    return;
  }

  if(scenId === 'modern'){
    // ── NATO (North Atlantic Treaty Organization) ─────────────────
    // Full-world map: include the North American members as well.
    makeAlliance('NATO', '#2e6fbf', 'United States of America', [
      'Canada','United Kingdom','France','Germany','Italy','Spain','Portugal',
      'Netherlands','Belgium','Luxembourg','Denmark','Norway','Iceland',
      'Greece','Turkey','Poland','Czechia','Slovakia','Hungary',
      'Romania','Bulgaria','Estonia','Latvia','Lithuania',
      'Slovenia','Croatia','Albania','Montenegro','North Macedonia',
      'Finland','Sweden'
    ]);

    // ── CSTO (Collective Security Treaty Organization) ────────────
    // Russia-led Eurasian military alliance (founded 2002).
    makeAlliance('CSTO', '#b82828', 'Russia', [
      'Belarus','Kazakhstan','Kyrgyzstan','Tajikistan','Armenia'
    ]);

    // ── GCC (Gulf Cooperation Council) ───────────────────────────
    // Political-military bloc of Gulf Arab monarchies (founded 1981).
    makeAlliance('GCC', '#c8922a', 'Saudi Arabia', [
      'United Arab Emirates','Qatar','Kuwait','Bahrain','Oman'
    ]);
  }
  // ── Napoleonic / historical scenarios could get alliances here in future ──
}

function _normalizeFreshGameplayRenderState(){
  // Fresh games (including TNO) must always enter the ordinary nations gameplay renderer.
  // This only clears transient UI/compositor state; it does not alter provinces, armies,
  // wars, AI, resources, movement, combat, or scenario ownership.
  peaceConference=null;
  _peaceHoverPid=-1;
  document.body.classList.remove('pc-active');
  const pcRoot=document.getElementById('peaceConference');
  if(pcRoot){pcRoot.classList.remove('active');pcRoot.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');}

  viewMode='nations';
  document.getElementById('vm-nations')?.classList.add('active');
  document.getElementById('vm-alliances')?.classList.remove('active');

  // Remove any compositor transform left by a previous menu/treaty/mobile frame.
  if(typeof _resetLayerTransform==='function'){
    _resetLayerTransform(mapCanvas);_resetLayerTransform(labelCanvas);_resetLayerTransform(fxCanvas);
  }else{
    if(mapCanvas)mapCanvas.style.transform='';
    if(labelCanvas)labelCanvas.style.transform='';
    if(fxCanvas)fxCanvas.style.transform='';
  }

  // Force the normal map/label/dynamic layers to rebuild from the newly applied scenario.
  _mapLayerKey='';_mapLayerViewKey='';_mapLayerContentKey='';
  _mapLayerCamX=_mapLayerCamY=_mapLayerZoom=NaN;
  _labelLayerKey='';_labelLayerContentKey='';
  _labelLayerCamX=_labelLayerCamY=_labelLayerZoom=NaN;
  _baseLayerValid=false;_mapLayerValid=false;_labelLayerValid=false;
  _fxHadContent=false;_idleDynamicValid=false;
  _terrBorderDirty=true;_allianceBorderDirty=true;_allianceLabelDirty=true;_alOverlayDirty=true;_civLabelDirty=true;_hlDirty=true;
  _markWorldFullDirty();
}

function startGame(civId,mode){
  // Preserve scenario selection set by the picker before the full reset.
  // Scenario previews are disposable: after reset, rebuild the chosen scenario
  // from source so custom-nation IDs and territory are guaranteed to exist.
  const _savedScenario=activeScenario;
  const _savedCivIdentity=civId>=0?{name:civs[civId]?.name||'',countryId:civs[civId]?.countryId}:null;
  _fullReset();
  activeScenario=_savedScenario;
  if(activeScenario && activeScenario!=='modern'){
    try{applyScenario(activeScenario);}
    catch(err){console.error('Fresh scenario apply rolled back safely:',err);_fullReset();activeScenario='modern';alert('Scenario failed safely; the base map was restored: '+err.message);showMainMenu();return;}
  }
  // A scenario rebuild is allowed to recreate custom nations. Never trust a preview-era
  // numeric civ ID across that rebuild; resolve the selected nation by stable identity.
  if(_savedCivIdentity){
    const rebuilt=civs.find(c=>c&&c.name===_savedCivIdentity.name)
      ||civs.find(c=>c&&!c.customNation&&c.countryId===_savedCivIdentity.countryId);
    if(!rebuilt){console.error('Selected scenario nation disappeared during rebuild:',_savedCivIdentity);showMainMenu();return;}
    civId=rebuilt.id;
  }
  // Capital assignment depends on scenario-specific city ownership, so capture the
  // rebuilt starting world BEFORE centering the camera or any AI asks for a capital.
  _captureOriginalMapOwners(true);_captureCityOriginalOwners(true);_markCapitalsDirty();
  _scenarioAlreadyApplied=false;
  gameMode=mode;
  playerCivId=civId;
  if(civId>=0){
    const civ=civs[civId];
    civ.isPlayer=true;
    // No automatic starting divisions — player recruits manually
    const capPid=_capitalProvinceForCiv(civ.id);
    const cap=capPid>=0?provinces[capPid]:null;
    if(cap){
      zoom=0.30; // reset to a comfortable game zoom before centering
      camX=cap.centerTx-canvas.width/(2*TS*zoom);
      camY=cap.centerTy-canvas.height/(2*TS*zoom);
      clampCam();
    }
    document.getElementById('hud-dot').style.background=civ.color;
    document.getElementById('hud-civname').textContent=civ.name.toUpperCase();
    const scenLabel = SCENARIOS[activeScenario]?.label || '';
    document.getElementById('hud-mode').textContent=scenLabel;
  } else {
    const scenLabel = SCENARIOS[activeScenario]?.label || '';
    document.getElementById('hud-mode').textContent=mode==='spectator'?`SPECTATING${scenLabel?' · '+scenLabel:''}`:mode==='sandbox'?'SANDBOX':'';
    document.getElementById('hud-dot').style.background='transparent';
    document.getElementById('hud-civname').textContent='WORLD SANDBOX';
  }
  hideCountryBar();
  panelCivId = -1; selectedProv = -1; _hlDirty = true;
  document.getElementById('top-bar').classList.add('game-visible');
  document.getElementById('gen-bar').classList.add('game-visible');
  if(typeof _initGiveLandControls==='function') _initGiveLandControls();
  // Scenario was rebuilt immediately after _fullReset(), before player selection.
  _scenarioAlreadyApplied = false;
  // This is the final starting map for a fresh game: remember province/city home owners now.
  _captureOriginalMapOwners(true);
  _captureCityOriginalOwners(true);
  // Apply starting alliances if toggle is on
  if(startingAlliancesEnabled) applyStartingAlliances(activeScenario);
  // Apply starting techs — always run so AI civs get their proper starting tiers
  // even in spectator and sandbox modes (player name is '' when no player chosen)
  {
    const playerCivName = civId >= 0 ? (civs[civId]?.name || '') : '';
    applyStartingTechs(activeScenario, playerCivName);
  }
  // Set in-game date to the scenario's historical start date
  const scen = SCENARIOS[activeScenario];
  if(scen && scen.startDate){
    gameDay   = scen.startDate.day;
    gameMonth = scen.startDate.month;
    gameYear  = scen.startDate.year;
  } else {
    gameDay=1; gameMonth=1; gameYear=2024;
  }
  gameDayFrac=0;
  initBotPhases();
  // Final bot setup: guarantee every bot has techs, bonus cache, coins, and a
  // fresh _techCooldown so their first research fires within the first few frames.
  for(const civ of civs){
    if(civ.id === playerCivId) continue;
    // Safety net: assign techs if applyStartingTechs somehow missed this civ
    if(!civ.techs || !civ.techs.size){
      if(SCENARIOS[activeScenario]?.historicalYear||activeScenario==='ww1_1914'||activeScenario==='ww1_1917'){const y=SCENARIOS[activeScenario]?.historicalYear||(activeScenario==='ww1_1917'?1917:1914);civ.techs=new Set(y===1914?_ww1TechForName(civ.name):_historicalTechSet(y,civ.name));}
      else {
        const level = MODERN_NATION_TECHS[civ.name];
        const econT = level ? level[0] : 3;
        const milT  = level ? level[1] : 3;
        civ.techs = new Set(_buildTechSet(econT, milT));
      }
    }
    // Always recompute bonus cache after full setup
    _recomputeBotTechBonus(civ);
    // Modern gets its existing tier-sized coin head-start. TNO and WW1 keep
    // the fresh scenario resources already assigned by their map builders.
    const level = MODERN_NATION_TECHS[civ.name];
    const tier = level ? Math.max(level[0], level[1]) : 3;
    if(!_isTnoScenario(activeScenario) && activeScenario!=='ww1_1914')
      civ.res.coins = Math.max(civ.res.coins, 400 + tier * 250);
    // Stagger initial tech cooldown so they don't all research on frame 1,
    // but the first round fires within the first 60 frames
    civ._techCooldown = civ.id % 60;
  }
  _normalizeFreshGameplayRenderState();
  gameReady=true;
  document.body.classList.add('game-running');
  AudioEngine.init(); AudioEngine.syncFromSettings(); AudioEngine.confirm();
  setGameSpeed(0); // start paused — player unpauses manually
  setTimeout(()=>{ buildGenBar(); updateFlagBanner(); buildAllianceWidget(); updateMilBar(); }, 50);
}

// ── LOAD MAP ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function setLoad(pct,msg){document.getElementById('loadFill').style.width=pct+'%';document.getElementById('loadMsg').textContent=msg}

async function _fetchJsonFirst(urls,timeoutMs=12000){
  let lastErr=null;
  for(const url of urls){
    const controller=new AbortController();
    const timer=setTimeout(()=>controller.abort(),timeoutMs);
    try{
      const r=await fetch(url,{signal:controller.signal,cache:'force-cache'});
      if(!r.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${r.status} from ${url}`);
      return await r.json();
    }catch(err){lastErr=err;console.warn('[map source failed]',url,err);}
    finally{clearTimeout(timer);}
  }
  throw new Error('Could not load world-atlas data from any mirror: '+(lastErr?.message||'unknown network error'));
}

async function loadMap(){
  if(typeof d3==='undefined'||typeof topojson==='undefined') throw new Error('Map libraries failed to load (D3/TopoJSON). Check internet/CDN access and reload.');
  setLoad(5,'Fetching world atlas…');
  const topo=await _fetchJsonFirst([
    'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/world-atlas@2.0.2/countries-110m.json',
    'https://unpkg.com/world-atlas@2.0.2/countries-110m.json'
  ]);
  if(!topo?.objects?.countries) throw new Error('World-atlas response is missing countries topology.');
  const worldFC=topojson.feature(topo,topo.objects.countries);
  _worldGeoFeatures=worldFC.features;_ensureTnoCountryIdMaps();

  setLoad(10,'Ordering full-world geography…');
  // Keep the exact old regional country ordering first so legacy IDs remain
  // stable wherever possible; append the rest of the planet afterwards.
  const legacyFeats=worldFC.features.filter(inLegacyRegion);
  const legacySet=new Set(legacyFeats);
  const regionFeats=legacyFeats.concat(worldFC.features.filter(f=>!legacySet.has(f)));

  setLoad(14,'Projecting full world…');
  // Natural Earth is far less distorted at global scale than the old regional
  // Mercator while still supporting proj()/proj.invert() used everywhere else.
  proj=d3.geoNaturalEarth1().fitExtent([[8,8],[WORLD_W-8,WORLD_H-8]],WORLD_SPHERE);

  setLoad(16,'Building global validity mask…');
  const N=WORLD_W*WORLD_H;
  validTile=new Uint8Array(N);
  tileKindBuf=new Uint8Array(N);
  tileCountryBuf=new Int16Array(N);tileCountryBuf.fill(-1);
  _countryLandTiles=Array.from({length:regionFeats.length},()=>[]);

  const offC=(typeof OffscreenCanvas==='function')?new OffscreenCanvas(WORLD_W,WORLD_H):document.createElement('canvas');
  offC.width=WORLD_W;offC.height=WORLD_H;
  const oCtx=offC.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});
  const geoPath=d3.geoPath(proj,oCtx);
  // Native canvas rasterisation replaces 1.728M proj.invert() calls.
  oCtx.clearRect(0,0,WORLD_W,WORLD_H);oCtx.beginPath();geoPath(WORLD_SPHERE);oCtx.fillStyle='#fff';oCtx.fill();
  let sphereData=oCtx.getImageData(0,0,WORLD_W,WORLD_H).data;
  for(let i=0,si=3;i<N;i++,si+=4)if(sphereData[si]>0)validTile[i]=1;
  sphereData=null;

  setLoad(20,'Rasterising nations…');
  for(let idx=0;idx<regionFeats.length;idx++){
    const feat=regionFeats[idx];
    const name=feat.properties.name||('Region'+idx);
    const color=countryColor(name,idx);
    countries.push({id:idx,name,color,border:darken(color,.24),lite:lighten(color,.20),legacyRegion:legacySet.has(feat)});
    // Rasterise only this feature's projected bounding box. The old full-canvas
    // getImageData+scan repeated 1.7M pixels for every country and became a major
    // full-world startup bottleneck; this is pixel-equivalent inside the feature.
    const b=geoPath.bounds(feat);
    const x0=Math.max(0,Math.floor(b[0][0])-2),y0=Math.max(0,Math.floor(b[0][1])-2);
    const x1=Math.min(WORLD_W,Math.ceil(b[1][0])+2),y1=Math.min(WORLD_H,Math.ceil(b[1][1])+2);
    const bw=Math.max(0,x1-x0),bh=Math.max(0,y1-y0);
    if(bw&&bh){
      oCtx.clearRect(x0,y0,bw,bh);
      oCtx.beginPath();geoPath(feat);oCtx.fillStyle='#fff';oCtx.fill();
      const img=oCtx.getImageData(x0,y0,bw,bh).data;
      for(let py=0;py<bh;py++)for(let px=0;px<bw;px++){
        const si=(py*bw+px)*4;if(img[si]<=128)continue;
        const tx=x0+px,ty=y0+py,i=ty*WORLD_W+tx;
        if(validTile[i]&&tileKindBuf[i]===0){tileKindBuf[i]=1;tileCountryBuf[i]=idx;_countryLandTiles[idx].push(i);}
      }
    }
    const landTiles=_countryLandTiles[idx];
    if(Array.isArray(landTiles))_countryLandTiles[idx]=Uint32Array.from(landTiles);
    if(idx%4===0)setLoad(20+Math.round(idx/regionFeats.length*36),'Mapping '+name+'…');
    if(idx%12===0)await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,0));
  }
  // The 1800×960 raster backing was only needed to discover immutable geography.
  // Shrinking it before province partitioning releases its large backing store while
  // partition scratch/province arrays are being allocated.
  offC.width=1;offC.height=1;

  setLoad(56,'Indexing historical provinces…');
  buildHistTileCache(); // must run after proj is set, before loadProvinces

  setLoad(60,'Generating provinces…');
  await loadProvinces();

  setLoad(78,'Generating sea zones…');
  generateSeaProvinces();

  // Province geometry is immutable after generation. Compact millions of boxed JS
  // numbers into contiguous Uint32 storage before the long-lived game state begins.
  // All existing for/of and indexed callers keep identical semantics.
  for(const p of provinces)if(p&&Array.isArray(p.tiles))p.tiles=Uint32Array.from(p.tiles);

  validTile=null;
  setLoad(82,'Computing adjacency…');
  computeAdjacency();
  _buildProvinceRenderBounds(); // one-time province AABBs for ultra-fast unit viewport culling

  setLoad(87,'Baking colour cache…');
  buildColorBufs(); // tileCivBuf also initialised here (all -1)
  // Province generation/raster scratch is startup-only. Drop it before civ/scenario
  // setup so mobile WebKit never carries duplicate world-sized arrays into gameplay.
  _countryLandTiles=null;_partitionAssign=null;_partitionQueue=null;tileCountryBuf=null;

  setLoad(93,'Initialising civilisations…');
  initCivs();  // fills tileCivBuf with civIds for all owned tiles

  setLoad(100,'Ready…');
  await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,0));
  buildCityCache();
  buildBorderCache();       // pre-build static province border segments
  _terrBorderDirty=true;    // territory borders built on first draw
  _allianceBorderDirty=true; // alliance borders built on first draw
  _civLabelDirty=true;      // HOI4 labels built on first draw
  _markWorldFullDirty();      // world texture built on first draw
  _clearFoxSpriteCache();   // flush any stale civ-colour sprites
  document.getElementById('loadScreen').style.display='none';
  mapReady=true;
  // Center the world map correctly for the main-menu background
  centerMenuWorldMap();
  showMainMenu();
}

// ── FOX ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// MILITARY — Province BFS pathfinding + Division class
// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// Reachability cache for legal movement graphs. Impossible-route checks are a
// first-class fast path: once one component is explored for a civ/topology, every
// later target in/out of that component is answered in O(1) instead of re-running
// whole-world BFS for every division and candidate target.
let _routeTopologyVersion=1; // ownership/front-cache revision (cheap, global)
let _routeLegalVersion=new Uint32Array(0); // exact per-civ movement-graph revision
let _routeCompCache=new Map(); // LRU: key civ|sea -> {version,labels,nextComp}
const _ROUTE_COMP_CACHE_MAX=isMobile?48:128;
function _newRouteLabels(){return provinces.length<=65534?new Uint16Array(provinces.length):new Uint32Array(provinces.length);}
let _routeCompQueue=new Int32Array(0);
function _ensureRouteLegalVersions(){
  if(_routeLegalVersion.length>=civs.length)return;
  const oldLen=_routeLegalVersion.length;
  const a=new Uint32Array(Math.max(civs.length,Math.max(16,oldLen*2)));
  a.set(_routeLegalVersion);for(let i=oldLen;i<a.length;i++)a[i]=1;_routeLegalVersion=a;
}
function _routeLegalStamp(civId){_ensureRouteLegalVersions();return (civId>=0&&civId<_routeLegalVersion.length)?(_routeLegalVersion[civId]||1):1;}
function _bumpRouteLegal(civId){
  if(civId<0)return;_ensureRouteLegalVersions();let v=((_routeLegalVersion[civId]||1)+1)>>>0;
  if(v===0){v=1;_routeCompCache.delete(civId<<1);_routeCompCache.delete((civId<<1)|1);}
  _routeLegalVersion[civId]=v;
}
function _touchRouteTopology(invalidateLegal=true){
  _routeTopologyVersion=(_routeTopologyVersion+1)>>>0;if(_routeTopologyVersion===0)_routeTopologyVersion=1;
  if(invalidateLegal){_ensureRouteLegalVersions();for(let i=0;i<civs.length;i++)_bumpRouteLegal(i);}
}
function _touchRouteWarParticipants(ids){
  _touchRouteTopology(false);_ensureRouteLegalVersions();
  const seen=new Set();for(const id of ids){if(id>=0&&id<civs.length&&!seen.has(id)){seen.add(id);_bumpRouteLegal(id);}}
}
function _routeOwnerLegalFor(civId,owner){return owner<0||owner===civId||(owner>=0&&(areAtWar(civId,owner)||areAllied(civId,owner)));}
let _routeOwnershipBatchDepth=0;
const _routeOwnershipBatchPairs=new Map();
function _beginRouteOwnershipBatch(){_routeOwnershipBatchDepth++;}
function _endRouteOwnershipBatch(){
  if(_routeOwnershipBatchDepth<=0)return;
  if(--_routeOwnershipBatchDepth>0)return;
  if(!_routeOwnershipBatchPairs.size)return;
  _touchRouteTopology(false);_ensureRouteLegalVersions();
  for(let cid=0;cid<civs.length;cid++){
    for(const pair of _routeOwnershipBatchPairs.values())if(_routeOwnerLegalFor(cid,pair[0])!==_routeOwnerLegalFor(cid,pair[1])){_bumpRouteLegal(cid);break;}
  }
  _routeOwnershipBatchPairs.clear();
}
function _touchRouteOwnership(prevOwner,nextOwner){
  if(prevOwner===nextOwner)return;
  if(_routeOwnershipBatchDepth>0){_routeOwnershipBatchPairs.set(prevOwner+','+nextOwner,[prevOwner,nextOwner]);return;}
  // A wartime capture normally leaves passability unchanged for attacker,
  // defender and neutral third parties. Invalidate only civs whose legal graph
  // actually changes instead of rebuilding every country's world component.
  _touchRouteTopology(false);_ensureRouteLegalVersions();
  for(let cid=0;cid<civs.length;cid++)if(_routeOwnerLegalFor(cid,prevOwner)!==_routeOwnerLegalFor(cid,nextOwner))_bumpRouteLegal(cid);
}
function _ensureRouteCompQueue(){if(_routeCompQueue.length!==provinces.length)_routeCompQueue=new Int32Array(provinces.length);}
function _routeProvinceLegal(pid,civId,allowSea){
  if(pid<0||pid>=provinces.length)return false;
  const p=provinces[pid];if(!p)return false;if(p.isSea)return !!allowSea;
  return _routeOwnerLegalFor(civId,provOwner[pid]);
}
function _routeCompState(civId,allowSea){
  const key=(civId<<1)|(allowSea?1:0);let st=_routeCompCache.get(key);
  if(!st){st={version:0,labels:_newRouteLabels(),nextComp:1};_routeCompCache.set(key,st);while(_routeCompCache.size>_ROUTE_COMP_CACHE_MAX)_routeCompCache.delete(_routeCompCache.keys().next().value);}
  else{_routeCompCache.delete(key);_routeCompCache.set(key,st);}
  if(st.labels.length!==provinces.length){st.labels=_newRouteLabels();st.version=0;st.nextComp=1;}
  const legalVersion=_routeLegalStamp(civId);
  if(st.version!==legalVersion){st.labels.fill(0);st.version=legalVersion;st.nextComp=1;}
  return st;
}
function _routeComponentId(pid,civId,allowSea){
  if(!_routeProvinceLegal(pid,civId,allowSea))return -1;
  const st=_routeCompState(civId,allowSea),labels=st.labels;
  if(labels[pid])return labels[pid];
  _ensureRouteCompQueue();const comp=st.nextComp++;let qh=0,qt=0;
  labels[pid]=comp;_routeCompQueue[qt++]=pid;
  while(qh<qt){
    const cur=_routeCompQueue[qh++];
    for(let ai=_adjBegin(cur),ae=_adjEnd(cur);ai<ae;ai++){
      const v=_adjList[ai];if(labels[v]||!_routeProvinceLegal(v,civId,allowSea))continue;
      labels[v]=comp;_routeCompQueue[qt++]=v;
    }
  }
  return comp;
}
function _routeCanReach(startPid,endPid,civId,allowSea){
  if(startPid===endPid)return _routeProvinceLegal(startPid,civId,allowSea);
  if(!_routeProvinceLegal(startPid,civId,allowSea)||!_routeProvinceLegal(endPid,civId,allowSea))return false;
  return _routeComponentId(startPid,civId,allowSea)===_routeComponentId(endPid,civId,allowSea);
}

// BFS province-graph pathfinding. Returns array of province IDs start→end
function bfsProvPath(startPid,endPid){
  if(startPid===endPid)return [startPid];
  const n=provinces.length;if(startPid<0||endPid<0||startPid>=n||endPid>=n)return null;
  _ensureBotPathBuffers();const stamp=_botNextPathStamp();let qh=0,qt=0;
  _botPathQueue[qt++]=startPid;_botPathSeen[startPid]=stamp;_botPathPrev[startPid]=-1;
  while(qh<qt){
    const cur=_botPathQueue[qh++];if(cur===endPid)break;
    for(let ai=_adjBegin(cur),ae=_adjEnd(cur);ai<ae;ai++){
      const v=_adjList[ai];if(_botPathSeen[v]===stamp)continue;
      _botPathSeen[v]=stamp;_botPathPrev[v]=cur;_botPathQueue[qt++]=v;
      if(v===endPid){qh=qt;break;}
    }
  }
  if(_botPathSeen[endPid]!==stamp)return null;
  let len=1,x=endPid;while(x!==startPid){x=_botPathPrev[x];if(x<0)return null;len++;}
  const path=new Array(len);x=endPid;for(let i=len-1;i>=0;i--){path[i]=x;if(i)x=_botPathPrev[x];}
  return path;
}

// Restricted BFS: only traverses own territory, unclaimed land, or
// enemy territory (active war). Neutral nations are impassable.
// Sea provinces are used ONLY as last resort (land path tried first).
// Used for ALL autonomous bot movement.
function bfsProvPathRestricted(startPid,endPid,civId){
  if(startPid===endPid)return [startPid];
  _ensureBotPathBuffers();
  function bfs(allowSea){
    if(!_routeCanReach(startPid,endPid,civId,allowSea))return null;
    const stamp=_botNextPathStamp();let qh=0,qt=0;
    _botPathQueue[qt++]=startPid;_botPathSeen[startPid]=stamp;_botPathPrev[startPid]=-1;
    while(qh<qt){
      const cur=_botPathQueue[qh++];
      for(let ai=_adjBegin(cur),ae=_adjEnd(cur);ai<ae;ai++){
        const v=_adjList[ai];if(_botPathSeen[v]===stamp||!_routeProvinceLegal(v,civId,allowSea))continue;
        _botPathSeen[v]=stamp;_botPathPrev[v]=cur;
        if(v===endPid){let len=1,x=v;while(x!==startPid){x=_botPathPrev[x];if(x<0)return null;len++;}const path=new Array(len);x=v;for(let i=len-1;i>=0;i--){path[i]=x;if(i)x=_botPathPrev[x];}return path;}
        _botPathQueue[qt++]=v;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }
  // Never fall back to unrestricted whole-world routing through neutral nations.
  // If both legal components reject the target, it is genuinely unreachable now.
  return bfs(false)||bfs(true);
}

let _frontPathDist=new Float64Array(0),_frontPathPrev=new Int32Array(0),_frontPathSeen=new Uint32Array(0),_frontPathStamp=1;
let _frontPathHeapSeq=1;
function _ensureFrontPathBuffers(){
  const n=provinces.length;if(_frontPathDist.length===n)return;
  _frontPathDist=new Float64Array(n);_frontPathPrev=new Int32Array(n);_frontPathSeen=new Uint32Array(n);_frontPathStamp=1;
}
function _frontNextPathStamp(){
  _frontPathStamp=(_frontPathStamp+1)>>>0;if(_frontPathStamp===0){_frontPathSeen.fill(0);_frontPathStamp=1;}return _frontPathStamp;
}

// Weighted pathfinding for front-assault orders.
// Enemy-at-war provinces cost 0.72 vs 1.0 for own/neutral, so units
// naturally prefer routing through enemy land on the way to the front.
// The cost gap is intentionally small — units won't make huge detours;
// the front destination remains the primary objective.
function bfsProvPathFrontAssault(startPid, endPid, civId){
  if(startPid===endPid) return [startPid];
  // Reject impossible assaults before allocating/scanning the weighted frontier.
  if(!_routeCanReach(startPid,endPid,civId,true)) return null;

  // Reuse typed scratch buffers instead of allocating two Maps for every assault plan.
  // The priority queue and its insertion/tie order stay unchanged, preserving path choice.
  _ensureFrontPathBuffers();
  const stamp=_frontNextPathStamp(),dist=_frontPathDist,prev=_frontPathPrev,seen=_frontPathSeen;
  seen[startPid]=stamp;dist[startPid]=0;prev[startPid]=-1;
  // Stable binary min-heap: preserves deterministic insertion order for equal costs while
  // removing the old O(n) scan+splice from every Dijkstra pop. Large/world-view wars can
  // call this pathfinder thousands of times, so this changes the hot path to O(log n).
  const heap=[],pushHeap=(cost,pid)=>{
    const node=[cost,pid,_frontPathHeapSeq++];let i=heap.length;heap.push(node);
    while(i>0){const p=(i-1)>>1,par=heap[p];if(par[0]<cost||(par[0]===cost&&par[2]<node[2]))break;heap[i]=par;i=p;}heap[i]=node;
  },popHeap=()=>{
    const root=heap[0],last=heap.pop();if(heap.length&&last){let i=0;while(true){let l=i*2+1;if(l>=heap.length)break;let r=l+1,c=(r<heap.length&&(heap[r][0]<heap[l][0]||(heap[r][0]===heap[l][0]&&heap[r][2]<heap[l][2])))?r:l;const child=heap[c];if(child[0]>last[0]||(child[0]===last[0]&&child[2]>last[2]))break;heap[i]=child;i=c;}heap[i]=last;}return root;
  };
  pushHeap(0,startPid);

  while(heap.length>0){
    const [cost,cur]=popHeap();

    if(cur===endPid) break;
    if(seen[cur]!==stamp || cost>dist[cur]+1e-9) continue;

    const adj=provAdj.get(cur); if(!adj) continue;
    for(const n of adj){
      const owner=provOwner[n];
      // Hard block: neutral foreign territory — impassable (but allied land is free to cross)
      // Sea zones are always passable (open water)
      const isSea = provinces[n]&&provinces[n].isSea;
      if(!isSea && owner>=0 && owner!==civId && !areAtWar(civId,owner) && !areAllied(civId,owner)) continue;

      // Enemy territory gets a slight cost discount → units drift through it
      // Own / unclaimed / destination: cost 1.0
      // Enemy (at war):               cost 0.72
      // Sea zone: cost 9999 — absolute last resort, only used if no land path exists
      const edgeCost = isSea ? 9999 :
                      (owner>=0 && owner!==civId && areAtWar(civId,owner)) ? 0.72 : 1.0;

      const nc=cost+edgeCost;
      if(seen[n]!==stamp || nc<dist[n]){
        seen[n]=stamp;dist[n]=nc;prev[n]=cur;
        pushHeap(nc,n);
      }
    }
  }

  if(seen[endPid]!==stamp) return null; // no weighted route reached the destination

  // Reconstruct from the typed predecessor buffer. Build backwards, then reverse once;
  // avoids Array.unshift() shifting the whole path on every hop.
  const path=[];
  let n=endPid;
  while(n!==-1){path.push(n);n=prev[n];}
  path.reverse();
  return path;
}

let _divId=0;
let selectedDivs=new Set(); // Set of division IDs currently selected
let playerAutoMoveUniversal=false; // AUTO MOVE is a single global player-army mode

// ── AUTO-GROUP ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Once per render frame: merge any idle, non-selected player divisions
// that share a province and have been stationary for ≥120 frames.
// The 120-frame grace window lets the player give separate orders before
// divisions are silently combined.
// ── AUTO-GROUP ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Called once per render frame. Any idle (path=[], not selected) player
// divisions that share a province are merged immediately — no grace window.
function tickAutoGroup(){
  // NOTE: Not called for player divisions — player divs stay as individual objects.
  // Kept here only for potential future use.
  if(playerCivId<0||!gameReady) return;
}


// ── ASSIGN LINE (defense / front draw) ───────────────────────────
// When the player right-drags a front line, assign each selected division
// its own path from its CURRENT province to a target line province.
// Divisions are NEVER destroyed or teleported — they just get new movement orders.
// Distribution: round-robin across line provinces so they spread out.
function autoSplitForLine(selDivs, lineProvs){
  if(!lineProvs.length||!selDivs.length||playerCivId<0) return;

  // Sort line provinces by distance to "center of mass" of selected divisions
  // so nearby divisions get nearby line assignments
  const sorted=[...selDivs];

  for(let i=0;i<sorted.length;i++){
    const d=sorted[i];
    _disableAutoMoveFor(d);
    // Assign the closest unoccupied line province (greedy nearest-first)
    const targetPid=lineProvs[i%lineProvs.length];
    if(d.provId===targetPid){ d.path=[]; continue; }
    const path=bfsProvPathFrontAssault(d.provId,targetPid,playerCivId);
    if(path&&path.length>1) d.path=path.slice(1);
    else d.path=[];
  }

  selectedDivs.clear();
  updateMilBar();
}

// ── FOX SPRITE CACHE ──────────────────────────────────────────────
// Pre-renders each unique (civColor × animFrame) fox body to an offscreen
// canvas at S=1 (art unit = 1px). drawImage scales it at draw-time.
// 16 anim frames × N civs = tiny cache. Each fox draw becomes ONE drawImage
// instead of 50+ bezier/arc/fill calls.
//
// Sprite coordinate space (S=1):
//   Art origin (0,0) → sprite pixel (FOX_OX, FOX_OY)
//   Art bounds: x ∈ [-38, 50], y ∈ [-34, 18]  → canvas 90×54
const FOX_SW=90, FOX_SH=54, FOX_OX=38, FOX_OY=34;
const FOX_ANIM_N=16;           // discrete animation steps
const _foxSpriteCache=new Map(); // key: "base_animIdx" → ImageBitmap or HTMLCanvasElement
// Promote newly cached canvases to ImageBitmap asynchronously (GPU-resident, faster drawImage)
const _BMP_SUPPORTED = !isMobile && (typeof createImageBitmap === 'function');
const _FOX_SPRITE_CACHE_MAX=isMobile?512:2048;
const _foxBitmapQueue=[];let _foxBitmapScheduled=false;
function _scheduleFoxBitmapPump(){
  if(_foxBitmapScheduled||!_foxBitmapQueue.length)return;_foxBitmapScheduled=true;
  const run=(deadline)=>{
    _foxBitmapScheduled=false;let made=0;
    while(_foxBitmapQueue.length&&made<3&&(!deadline||deadline.timeRemaining()>2)){
      const [key,canvas]=_foxBitmapQueue.shift();made++;
      createImageBitmap(canvas).then(bmp=>{
        const cur=_foxSpriteCache.get(key);
        if(cur===canvas)_foxSpriteCache.set(key,bmp);else if(bmp.close)bmp.close();
      }).catch(()=>{});
    }
    if(_foxBitmapQueue.length)_scheduleFoxBitmapPump();
  };
  if(typeof requestIdleCallback==='function')requestIdleCallback(run,{timeout:220});else setTimeout(()=>run(null),18);
}
function _promoteToBitmap(key,canvas){if(!_BMP_SUPPORTED)return;_foxBitmapQueue.push([key,canvas]);_scheduleFoxBitmapPump();}

function _foxAnimParams(animIdx){
  // Reconstruct animation parameters from quantised frame index
  const frame=animIdx*(25.13/FOX_ANIM_N); // spread evenly over one bounce period
  const trot=frame*0.25;
  return{
    bounce:Math.sin(trot)*1.5,
    lp1:Math.sin(trot),
    lp2:Math.sin(trot+Math.PI),
    ts:Math.sin(frame*0.12)*0.35
  };
}

function _foxLegOffscreen(c2, bx, phase, bounce, bootTier){
  const lb=Math.abs(phase)*1.5, px=phase*2;
  const saved=c2.fillStyle;
  // Upper leg
  c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(bx-2,2+bounce); c2.lineTo(bx-2.5,7+bounce); c2.lineTo(bx+2.5,7+bounce); c2.lineTo(bx+2,2+bounce); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
  // Lower leg
  c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(bx-2.5,7+bounce); c2.lineTo(bx-3+px,13+lb); c2.lineTo(bx+3+px,13+lb); c2.lineTo(bx+2.5,7+bounce); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
  // Boot / paw by tier
  const fx=bx+px, fy=14+lb;
  if(!bootTier||bootTier<=0){
    // Bare paw
    c2.fillStyle='#1a1a1a'; c2.beginPath(); c2.ellipse(fx,fy,2.5,1.8,0,0,Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
  } else if(bootTier===1){
    // Leather boot — warm brown oval
    c2.fillStyle='#8B5E3C'; c2.beginPath(); c2.ellipse(fx,fy,3.2,2,0,0,Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
    c2.fillStyle='#5a3a1a'; c2.beginPath(); c2.ellipse(fx,fy+1,3.2,0.9,0,0,Math.PI); c2.fill();
  } else if(bootTier===2){
    // Riding boot — tall dark brown box with rounded top
    c2.fillStyle='#3d2310';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(fx-3,fy-3); c2.lineTo(fx-3,fy+2); c2.lineTo(fx+3,fy+2); c2.lineTo(fx+3,fy-3); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    c2.fillStyle='#5a3520'; c2.beginPath(); c2.ellipse(fx,fy+2,3,1,0,0,Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
    // tiny cuff line
    c2.strokeStyle='#5a3520'; c2.lineWidth=0.6;
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(fx-2.5,fy-2); c2.lineTo(fx+2.5,fy-2); c2.stroke();
  } else if(bootTier===3){
    // Combat boot — black boxy with visible sole
    c2.fillStyle='#1a1a1a';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(fx-3,fy-3.5); c2.lineTo(fx-3,fy+1.5); c2.lineTo(fx+3,fy+1.5); c2.lineTo(fx+3,fy-3.5); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    c2.fillStyle='#333'; c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(fx-3,fy+0.5); c2.lineTo(fx+3,fy+0.5); c2.lineTo(fx+3,fy+2); c2.lineTo(fx-3,fy+2); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
  } else if(bootTier===4){
    // Motorised boot — black with small white lace dot
    c2.fillStyle='#111';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(fx-3,fy-3.5); c2.lineTo(fx-3,fy+1.5); c2.lineTo(fx+3,fy+1.5); c2.lineTo(fx+3,fy-3.5); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    c2.fillStyle='#444'; c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(fx-3,fy+0.5); c2.lineTo(fx+3,fy+0.5); c2.lineTo(fx+3,fy+2); c2.lineTo(fx-3,fy+2); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,0.5)'; c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(fx,fy-1.5,0.8,0,Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
  } else if(bootTier===5){
    // Flight boot — dark gray-blue
    c2.fillStyle='#222233';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(fx-3,fy-4); c2.lineTo(fx-3,fy+1.5); c2.lineTo(fx+3,fy+1.5); c2.lineTo(fx+3,fy-4); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    c2.fillStyle='#3a3a55'; c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(fx-3,fy+0.5); c2.lineTo(fx+3,fy+0.5); c2.lineTo(fx+3,fy+2); c2.lineTo(fx-3,fy+2); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
  } else if(bootTier===6){
    // Tier 6 exo boot — near-black with cyan stripe
    c2.fillStyle='#0a0a12';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(fx-3,fy-4); c2.lineTo(fx-3,fy+1.5); c2.lineTo(fx+3,fy+1.5); c2.lineTo(fx+3,fy-4); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    c2.fillStyle='#00aacc'; c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(fx-3,fy+1); c2.lineTo(fx+3,fy+1); c2.lineTo(fx+3,fy+2); c2.lineTo(fx-3,fy+2); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
  } else if(bootTier===7){
    // Orbital drop pod boot — gunmetal with orange heat marks
    c2.fillStyle='#2a2a2a';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(fx-3.5,fy-5); c2.lineTo(fx-3.5,fy+2); c2.lineTo(fx+3.5,fy+2); c2.lineTo(fx+3.5,fy-5); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(255,100,0,0.7)'; c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(fx-3.5,fy+0.5); c2.lineTo(fx+3.5,fy+0.5); c2.lineTo(fx+3.5,fy+2); c2.lineTo(fx-3.5,fy+2); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(255,200,0,0.5)'; c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(fx,fy-3,0.8,0,Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
  } else if(bootTier===8){
    // Teleport boot — translucent blue-white shimmer
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(20,40,80,0.85)';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(fx-3.5,fy-5); c2.lineTo(fx-3.5,fy+2); c2.lineTo(fx+3.5,fy+2); c2.lineTo(fx+3.5,fy-5); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    c2.strokeStyle='rgba(100,200,255,0.9)'; c2.lineWidth=0.8;
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(fx-3.5,fy-5); c2.lineTo(fx-3.5,fy+2); c2.lineTo(fx+3.5,fy+2); c2.lineTo(fx+3.5,fy-5); c2.closePath(); c2.stroke();
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(140,230,255,0.4)'; c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(fx,fy-2,1.2,0,Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
  } else {
    // Tier 9 temporal boot — void-dark with purple time-distortion aura
    c2.fillStyle='#050508';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(fx-4,fy-6); c2.lineTo(fx-4,fy+2); c2.lineTo(fx+4,fy+2); c2.lineTo(fx+4,fy-6); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    c2.strokeStyle='rgba(180,0,255,0.8)'; c2.lineWidth=0.8;
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(fx-4,fy-6); c2.lineTo(fx-4,fy+2); c2.lineTo(fx+4,fy+2); c2.lineTo(fx+4,fy-6); c2.closePath(); c2.stroke();
    c2.strokeStyle='rgba(255,100,255,0.5)'; c2.lineWidth=0.5;
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(fx-3,fy-4); c2.lineTo(fx+3,fy); c2.stroke();
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(200,80,255,0.9)'; c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(fx,fy-3,1,0,Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
  }
  c2.fillStyle=saved;
}

// ── Body-armour overlay helper ──────────────────────────────────────
function _drawBodyArmor(c2, tier, bounce){
  if(tier<=0) return;
  const by=-8+bounce;
  if(tier===1){
    // Leather chest strap — warm brown, centred on torso (x:-9→11, y:by to by+9)
    c2.fillStyle='#7a4a20';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(-9,by+2); c2.bezierCurveTo(-5,by-1,6,by-1,11,by+2);
    c2.lineTo(11,by+9); c2.bezierCurveTo(5,by+7,-4,by+7,-9,by+9); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    // Buckle detail in the centre-front area
    c2.fillStyle='#c08030';
    c2.fillRect(3,by+3,5,3.5);
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(255,200,80,0.7)'; c2.fillRect(4,by+3.5,3,2.5);
  } else if(tier===2){
    // Chainmail torso — mid-gray with subtle links
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(155,155,155,0.8)';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(-9,by+2); c2.bezierCurveTo(-5,by-1,6,by-1,11,by+2);
    c2.lineTo(11,by+8); c2.bezierCurveTo(5,by+6,-4,by+6,-9,by+8); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    c2.strokeStyle='rgba(90,90,90,0.55)'; c2.lineWidth=0.5;
    for(let i=1;i<4;i++){c2.beginPath();c2.moveTo(-9,by+2+i*1.8);c2.lineTo(11,by+2+i*1.8);c2.stroke();}
  } else if(tier===3){
    // Steel breastplate — silver with centre ridge
    c2.fillStyle='#aaaaaa';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(-7,by+1); c2.bezierCurveTo(-3,by-3,6,by-3,10,by+1);
    c2.lineTo(10,by+8); c2.bezierCurveTo(4,by+5,-3,by+5,-7,by+8); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,0.3)';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(-4,by+2); c2.lineTo(4,by+2); c2.lineTo(3,by+7); c2.lineTo(-3,by+7); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    c2.strokeStyle='#888'; c2.lineWidth=0.7;
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(1.5,by+1); c2.lineTo(1.5,by+8); c2.stroke();
  } else if(tier===4){
    // Kevlar vest — olive with MOLLE straps
    c2.fillStyle='#5a6a3a';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(-9,by); c2.bezierCurveTo(-5,by-3,6,by-3,11,by);
    c2.lineTo(11,by+9); c2.bezierCurveTo(5,by+6,-4,by+6,-9,by+9); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    c2.fillStyle='#4a5a2a';
    // Pockets shifted toward chest/front (x centre-right on torso)
    for(let i=0;i<3;i++){c2.fillRect(-2+i*4,by+5,3,2.5);}
  } else if(tier===5){
    // Composite vest — near-black with plate segments
    c2.fillStyle='#1e2028';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(-9,by-1); c2.bezierCurveTo(-5,by-4,7,by-4,11,by-1);
    c2.lineTo(11,by+9); c2.bezierCurveTo(5,by+6,-4,by+6,-9,by+9); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(70,85,105,0.85)';
    // Plates shifted to cover chest (front/right of torso)
    c2.fillRect(-3,by+1,10,3.5); c2.fillRect(-3,by+5.5,10,3);
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,0.07)'; c2.fillRect(-3,by+1,10,0.8);
  } else if(tier===6){
    // Exoskeleton frame — near-black chassis with cyan glow lines
    // Body x: -14→12, armor safely x: -10→11, y: by-2→by+9
    c2.fillStyle='#111118';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(-10,by-2); c2.bezierCurveTo(-5,by-5,7,by-5,11,by-2);
    c2.lineTo(11,by+9); c2.bezierCurveTo(5,by+7,-4,by+7,-10,by+9); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    c2.strokeStyle='rgba(0,200,255,0.75)'; c2.lineWidth=0.8;
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(-8,by+2); c2.lineTo(9,by+2); c2.stroke();
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(-8,by+6); c2.lineTo(9,by+6); c2.stroke();
    c2.strokeStyle='rgba(0,200,255,0.4)'; c2.lineWidth=0.6;
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(1,by-1); c2.lineTo(1,by+8); c2.stroke();
  } else if(tier===7){
    // Combat Android Frame — polished chrome with orange power nodes
    // Stays within body: x -10→11, y by-2→by+9
    c2.fillStyle='#c0c0d0';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(-10,by-2); c2.bezierCurveTo(-5,by-5,7,by-5,11,by-2);
    c2.lineTo(11,by+9); c2.bezierCurveTo(5,by+7,-4,by+7,-10,by+9); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    // Chrome highlight
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,0.25)';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(-9,by-1); c2.bezierCurveTo(-5,by-4,6,by-4,10,by-1);
    c2.lineTo(10,by+2); c2.bezierCurveTo(4,by+0,-4,by+0,-9,by+2); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    // Orange power nodes on the chest
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(255,120,0,0.9)';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(-3,by+3,1.8,0,Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(4,by+3,1.8,0,Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(0.5,by+7,2,0,Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
    c2.strokeStyle='rgba(255,160,0,0.6)'; c2.lineWidth=0.7;
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(-3,by+3); c2.lineTo(0.5,by+7); c2.lineTo(4,by+3); c2.stroke();
  } else if(tier===8){
    // Quantum Phase Shield — dark chassis + translucent blue force-field bubble
    // Inner chassis: x -10→11, outer shield: x -11→12 (just 1px larger each side)
    c2.fillStyle='#0a0a1e';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(-10,by-2); c2.bezierCurveTo(-5,by-5,7,by-5,11,by-2);
    c2.lineTo(11,by+9); c2.bezierCurveTo(5,by+7,-4,by+7,-10,by+9); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    // Outer phase bubble (slightly larger, semi-transparent)
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(80,160,255,0.12)';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(-11,by-3); c2.bezierCurveTo(-5,by-7,8,by-7,12,by-3);
    c2.lineTo(12,by+11); c2.bezierCurveTo(5,by+9,-4,by+9,-11,by+11); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    c2.strokeStyle='rgba(100,210,255,0.85)'; c2.lineWidth=1.0;
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(-11,by-3); c2.bezierCurveTo(-5,by-7,8,by-7,12,by-3);
    c2.lineTo(12,by+11); c2.bezierCurveTo(5,by+9,-4,by+9,-11,by+11); c2.closePath(); c2.stroke();
    // Inner energy ripples
    c2.strokeStyle='rgba(160,230,255,0.4)'; c2.lineWidth=0.5;
    for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
      c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(-8,by+1+i*3); c2.lineTo(10,by+1+i*3); c2.stroke();
    }
  } else {
    // Tier 9+ Reality Anchor — void-black with purple spacetime distortion
    // Body suit: x -10→11. Aura: 1px bleed each side (x -11→12).
    c2.fillStyle='#050508';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(-10,by-2); c2.bezierCurveTo(-5,by-5,7,by-5,11,by-2);
    c2.lineTo(11,by+9); c2.bezierCurveTo(5,by+7,-4,by+7,-10,by+9); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    // Purple void aura — slightly wider than the suit
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(130,0,255,0.15)';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(-11,by-3); c2.bezierCurveTo(-5,by-7,8,by-7,12,by-3);
    c2.lineTo(12,by+10); c2.bezierCurveTo(5,by+8,-4,by+8,-11,by+10); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    // Distortion arcs centred on torso (r=6 and r=8 to stay inside body)
    c2.strokeStyle='rgba(180,0,255,0.8)'; c2.lineWidth=1.0;
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(0.5,by+3,6,Math.PI*0.05,Math.PI*0.95); c2.stroke();
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(0.5,by+3,8,Math.PI*1.05,Math.PI*1.95); c2.stroke();
    c2.strokeStyle='rgba(240,100,255,0.5)'; c2.lineWidth=0.5;
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(-8,by+1); c2.lineTo(10,by+6); c2.stroke();
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(10,by+1); c2.lineTo(-8,by+6); c2.stroke();
    // Bright singularity node
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(220,80,255,0.95)';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(0.5,by+3,1.5,0,Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
  }
}

// ── Military greatcoat overlay ──────────────────────────────────────
// Drawn OVER the body fur, UNDER the body armour.
// Gives the fox a WWI/WWII/HOI4-style soldier silhouette.
function _drawMilitaryCoat(c2, base, baseD, helmD, lite, bounce, ts){
  const coat   = baseD;           // main coat body (darker nation shade)
  const coatL  = base;            // collar / cuffs / epaulettes (lighter)
  const btn    = '#14100a';       // dark buttons
  const bkl    = '#c8a040';       // brass buckle / medal
  const by     = bounce;
  // ── COAT TAILS — drawn before body so legs poke out front ───────
  // Back panel (animates gently with ts)
  const sway = ts * 0.18;
  c2.fillStyle = coat;
  c2.beginPath();
  c2.moveTo(-4+sway,   3+by);
  c2.bezierCurveTo(-7+sway*1.2, 7+by, -9+sway*1.6, 11+by, -8+sway*2, 17+by);
  c2.lineTo(-2+sway*2, 17+by);
  c2.bezierCurveTo(-1+sway,     9+by, -1, 5+by, 0, 3+by);
  c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
  // Coat-tail lining accent (slightly lighter strip at hem)
  c2.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.08)';
  c2.beginPath();
  c2.moveTo(-8+sway*2,15+by); c2.lineTo(-2+sway*2,15+by);
  c2.lineTo(-2+sway*2,17+by); c2.lineTo(-8+sway*2,17+by);
  c2.closePath(); c2.fill();

  // Front panel
  c2.fillStyle = coat;
  c2.beginPath();
  c2.moveTo(3, 3+by);
  c2.bezierCurveTo(5, 7+by, 7-sway*0.6, 11+by, 5-sway, 17+by);
  c2.lineTo(9-sway, 17+by);
  c2.bezierCurveTo(10-sway*0.5, 10+by, 9, 6+by, 7, 3+by);
  c2.closePath(); c2.fill();

  // ── COAT BODY (large torso panel) ───────────────────────────────
  c2.fillStyle = coat;
  c2.beginPath();
  c2.moveTo(12, -2+by);
  c2.bezierCurveTo(10,-11+by, 3,-14+by, -3,-12+by);
  c2.bezierCurveTo(-9,-12+by, -14,-9+by, -15,-3+by);
  c2.bezierCurveTo(-16,  1+by, -13,  6+by,  -7,  7+by);
  c2.bezierCurveTo( -1,  8+by,   8,  7+by,  13,  5+by);
  c2.bezierCurveTo( 15,  2+by,  14, -0+by,  12, -2+by);
  c2.closePath(); c2.fill();

  // Coat body subtle highlight (top edge catch-light)
  c2.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.09)';
  c2.beginPath();
  c2.moveTo(-3,-12+by); c2.bezierCurveTo(3,-14+by, 9,-12+by, 12,-2+by);
  c2.bezierCurveTo(9,-4+by, 2,-5+by, -3,-4+by);
  c2.bezierCurveTo(-8,-5+by, -12,-5+by, -14,-4+by);
  c2.bezierCurveTo(-12,-9+by, -7,-12+by, -3,-12+by);
  c2.closePath(); c2.fill();

  // ── FRONT OPENING (lapel centre strip) ──────────────────────────
  // Slightly lighter vertical strip to suggest open lapels
  c2.fillStyle = helmD;
  c2.beginPath();
  c2.moveTo( 1, -9+by); c2.bezierCurveTo( 3, -5+by,  4, 2+by,  3,  8+by);
  c2.lineTo(-1,  8+by); c2.bezierCurveTo(-2,  2+by, -1, -5+by,  0, -9+by);
  c2.closePath(); c2.fill();

  // ── BUTTONS (4 down the centre, with brass highlight) ───────────
  c2.fillStyle = btn;
  for(let i=0;i<4;i++){
    const byi = -7+by + i*3.8;
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(1.2, byi, 1.3, 0, Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
    c2.fillStyle = bkl;
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(0.9, byi-0.35, 0.55, 0, Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
    c2.fillStyle = btn;
  }

  // ── BELT WITH BUCKLE ────────────────────────────────────────────
  c2.fillStyle = '#22180c';
  c2.beginPath();
  c2.moveTo(-14, 0.5+by); c2.lineTo(13, 0.5+by);
  c2.lineTo(13, 3.2+by);  c2.lineTo(-14, 3.2+by);
  c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
  // Buckle body
  c2.fillStyle = bkl;
  c2.fillRect(-1.2, 0.2+by, 5, 3.2);
  // Buckle hole
  c2.fillStyle = '#22180c';
  c2.fillRect(0, 0.8+by, 2.8, 1.5);
  // Belt stitch line
  c2.strokeStyle='rgba(255,200,80,0.18)'; c2.lineWidth=0.4;
  c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(-14,1.8+by); c2.lineTo(-1.2,1.8+by); c2.stroke();
  c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(3.8,1.8+by); c2.lineTo(13,1.8+by); c2.stroke();

  // ── EPAULETTES (shoulder tabs) ───────────────────────────────────
  // Left shoulder (rear side)
  c2.fillStyle = coatL;
  c2.beginPath();
  c2.moveTo(-14,-5+by); c2.lineTo(-9,-5+by); c2.lineTo(-8,-9+by); c2.lineTo(-13,-9+by);
  c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
  c2.strokeStyle='rgba(200,180,80,0.5)'; c2.lineWidth=0.5;
  for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
    c2.beginPath();
    c2.moveTo(-12.5+i*1.5,-5+by); c2.lineTo(-12.2+i*1.5,-8+by); c2.stroke();
  }
  // Right shoulder (face side)
  c2.fillStyle = coatL;
  c2.beginPath();
  c2.moveTo(8,-8+by); c2.lineTo(13,-8+by); c2.lineTo(12,-12+by); c2.lineTo(7,-12+by);
  c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
  for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
    c2.beginPath();
    c2.moveTo(8.5+i*1.5,-8+by); c2.lineTo(8.2+i*1.5,-11+by); c2.stroke();
  }

  // ── HIGH MILITARY COLLAR ─────────────────────────────────────────
  // Left lapel
  c2.fillStyle = coatL;
  c2.beginPath();
  c2.moveTo(2,-9+by); c2.lineTo(7,-6+by); c2.lineTo(5,-5+by); c2.lineTo(1,-8+by);
  c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
  // Right lapel (face side, pointing toward head)
  c2.fillStyle = coatL;
  c2.beginPath();
  c2.moveTo(7,-9+by); c2.lineTo(14,-7+by); c2.lineTo(12,-5+by); c2.lineTo(5,-7+by);
  c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
  // Collar piping
  c2.strokeStyle = lite; c2.lineWidth = 0.55;
  c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(2,-9+by); c2.lineTo(7,-6+by); c2.stroke();
  c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(7,-9+by); c2.lineTo(14,-7+by); c2.stroke();

  // ── MEDAL / CAMPAIGN RIBBON on left chest ───────────────────────
  c2.fillStyle = bkl;
  c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(-5, -4+by, 2.0, 0, Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
  c2.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.35)';
  c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(-5.6,-4.6+by, 0.75, 0, Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
  c2.fillStyle = '#8a6020';
  c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(-5,-4+by, 0.9, 0, Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
  // Ribbon bar above medal
  c2.fillStyle = '#a02020'; c2.fillRect(-6.5, -7+by, 3, 1.4);
  c2.fillStyle = '#2020a0'; c2.fillRect(-5.0, -7+by, 1.5, 1.4);
}

// ── Tier-based rifle drawn in slope-arms carry position ─────────────
// Slope arms: barrel upper-left, stock lower-right, paw at midpoint.
function _drawRifle(c2, armorTier, bounce, armSwing){
  const gt = armorTier; // gun tier mirrors armor tier
  if(gt <= 0) return;   // tier 0 = no weapon

  // Slope-arms anchor points (in fox sprite coordinate space)
  const bx = 4,  by2= -27+bounce; // barrel end (upper left)
  const sx = 21, sy2=   8+bounce; // stock end  (lower right)

  // Direction & perpendicular unit vectors
  const rdx=bx-sx, rdy=by2-sy2;
  const rlen=Math.hypot(rdx,rdy);
  const unx=rdx/rlen, uny=rdy/rlen;   // along rifle (stock→barrel)
  const rnx=-uny,    rny=unx;          // perpendicular (right-hand side)

  // Helper: point at fraction t along rifle, offset w perp
  const R=(t,w)=>([sx+rdx*t+rnx*w, sy2+rdy*t+rny*w]);

  if(gt===1){
    // ── Flintlock Musket (1680s–1800s) ──────────────────────────
    // Long one-piece hardwood stock, round metal barrel, S-curve trigger guard

    // Full wooden stock (bottom 50%)
    c2.fillStyle='#9B6E3C';
    let [x0,y0]=R(0,2.8), [x1,y1]=R(0,-2), [x2,y2]=R(0.48,1.6), [x3,y3]=R(0.48,-1.4);
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(x0,y0); c2.lineTo(x2,y2); c2.lineTo(x3,y3); c2.lineTo(x1,y1); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    // Stock wood grain lines
    c2.strokeStyle='rgba(60,30,10,0.3)'; c2.lineWidth=0.5;
    for(let i=1;i<4;i++){let [a,b]=R(i*0.1,0.5), [c3,d]=R(i*0.1+0.08,2);c2.beginPath();c2.moveTo(a,b);c2.lineTo(c3,d);c2.stroke();}

    // Metal lock plate & pan
    c2.fillStyle='#888';
    let [lx,ly]=R(0.38,2.8);
    c2.beginPath(); c2.ellipse(lx,ly, 3,2, Math.atan2(uny,unx)+Math.PI/2, 0, Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
    // Pan cover (cocked position)
    c2.fillStyle='#aaa';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.ellipse(lx,ly, 2,1.2, Math.atan2(uny,unx), 0, Math.PI*2); c2.fill();

    // Metal barrel (upper 52%)
    c2.fillStyle='#707070';
    let [m0,m1]=R(0.48,1.2), [m2,m3]=R(0.48,-1.1), [b0,b1]=R(1,0.8), [b2,b3]=R(1,-0.8);
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(m0,m1); c2.lineTo(b0,b1); c2.lineTo(b2,b3); c2.lineTo(m2,m3); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    // Barrel highlight (light reflection along top edge)
    c2.strokeStyle='#bbb'; c2.lineWidth=0.7;
    let [h0,h1]=R(0.5,-0.9), [h2,h3]=R(0.96,-0.9);
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(h0,h1); c2.lineTo(h2,h3); c2.stroke();
    // Muzzle flare guard (flared end)
    c2.fillStyle='#999';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(bx,by2, 1.8, 0, Math.PI*2); c2.fill();

    // Trigger guard (curved line under grip)
    c2.strokeStyle='#777'; c2.lineWidth=1.2; c2.lineCap='round';
    let [tg0,tg1]=R(0.30,3.5), [tg2,tg3]=R(0.44,3.5);
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(tg0,tg1);
    c2.bezierCurveTo(tg0+rnx,tg1+rny+2, tg2+rnx,tg3+rny+2, tg2,tg3);
    c2.stroke();

  } else if(gt===2){
    // ── Bolt-action Rifle (Mosin-Nagant / Springfield 1903, WWI) ──
    // Long barrel, visible bolt handle, wooden furniture with metal receiver

    // Wooden stock (lower 42%)
    c2.fillStyle='#7a5028';
    let [s0,s1]=R(0,3), [s2,s3]=R(0,-2.2), [m0,m1]=R(0.42,1.8), [m2,m3]=R(0.42,-1.5);
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(s0,s1); c2.lineTo(m0,m1); c2.lineTo(m2,m3); c2.lineTo(s2,s3); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    // Wood detail — cheek piece swell

    c2.beginPath(); c2.ellipse(sl0,sl1,2.5,1.4,Math.atan2(uny,unx)+Math.PI/2,0,Math.PI*2); c2.fill();

    // Short barrel + muzzle brake
    c2.fillStyle='#3a3a3a';
    let [ba0,ba1]=R(0.50,1.8), [ba2,ba3]=R(0.50,-1.8), [be0,be1]=R(0.92,1.4), [be2,be3]=R(0.92,-1.4);
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(ba0,ba1); c2.lineTo(be0,be1); c2.lineTo(be2,be3); c2.lineTo(ba2,ba3); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    c2.fillStyle='#555';
    let [mb0,mb1]=R(0.92,1.8), [mb2,mb3]=R(0.92,-1.8), [me0,me1]=R(1,1.8), [me2,me3]=R(1,-1.8);
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(mb0,mb1); c2.lineTo(me0,me1); c2.lineTo(me2,me3); c2.lineTo(mb2,mb3); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    // Muzzle ports
    c2.strokeStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.5)'; c2.lineWidth=0.5;
    for(let i=0;i<3;i++){let [p0,p1]=R(0.94,-1+i*0.6),[p2,p3]=R(0.98,-1+i*0.6);c2.beginPath();c2.moveTo(p0,p1);c2.lineTo(p2,p3);c2.stroke();}

  } else if(gt===7){
    // ── Energy Rifle (Sci-fi plasma bolt, near-future) ────────────
    // Sleek angular chassis, energy cell instead of magazine, glowing emitter

    // Main body (angular polymer/composite chassis)
    c2.fillStyle='#1a1a28';
    let [b0,b1]=R(0,2.8), [b2,b3]=R(0,-2.5), [bm0,bm1]=R(0.72,2.2), [bm2,bm3]=R(0.72,-2.2);
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(b0,b1); c2.lineTo(bm0,bm1); c2.lineTo(bm2,bm3); c2.lineTo(b2,b3); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    // Chassis highlight
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(100,150,255,0.12)';
    let [hl0,hl1]=R(0.02,-2.5), [hl2,hl3]=R(0.70,-2.5), [hl4,hl5]=R(0.70,-1.2), [hl6,hl7]=R(0.02,-1.2);
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(hl0,hl1); c2.lineTo(hl2,hl3); c2.lineTo(hl4,hl5); c2.lineTo(hl6,hl7); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();

    // Energy cell (glowing crystal magazine at the base)
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(50,100,255,0.3)';
    let [ec0,ec1]=R(0.18,2.2);
    c2.save(); c2.translate(ec0+rnx*3,ec1+rny*3);
    c2.rotate(Math.atan2(rdy,rdx));
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(-2,0); c2.lineTo(-2,6); c2.lineTo(2,6); c2.lineTo(2,0); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    // Crystal glow interior
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(80,160,255,0.8)';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(-1,1); c2.lineTo(-1,5); c2.lineTo(1,5); c2.lineTo(1,1); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    c2.restore();

    // Receiver glow lines (tech detail)
    c2.strokeStyle='rgba(80,130,255,0.6)'; c2.lineWidth=0.7;
    let [gl0,gl1]=R(0.08,1), [gl2,gl3]=R(0.60,1);
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(gl0,gl1); c2.lineTo(gl2,gl3); c2.stroke();
    let [gl4,gl5]=R(0.08,-1), [gl6,gl7]=R(0.60,-1);
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(gl4,gl5); c2.lineTo(gl6,gl7); c2.stroke();

    // Grip (ergonomic, with thumb rest)
    c2.fillStyle='#222230';
    let [gp0,gp1]=R(0.44,2.2);
    c2.save(); c2.translate(gp0+rnx*1.5,gp1+rny*1.5); c2.rotate(Math.atan2(rdy,rdx)+0.2);
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(-1.5,0); c2.bezierCurveTo(-2,2,-2,4,-1,5); c2.lineTo(1,5); c2.bezierCurveTo(2,4,2,2,1.5,0); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    c2.restore();

    // Barrel (upper 28%, thin and precise)
    c2.fillStyle='#303040';
    let [ba0,ba1]=R(0.72,1.6), [ba2,ba3]=R(0.72,-1.6), [be0,be1]=R(1,0.9), [be2,be3]=R(1,-0.9);
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(ba0,ba1); c2.lineTo(be0,be1); c2.lineTo(be2,be3); c2.lineTo(ba2,ba3); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    // Emitter coils
    c2.strokeStyle='rgba(100,160,255,0.8)'; c2.lineWidth=1.0;
    for(let i=0;i<4;i++){let [co0,co1]=R(0.75+i*0.055,-1),[co2,co3]=R(0.75+i*0.055,1);c2.beginPath();c2.moveTo(co0,co1);c2.lineTo(co2,co3);c2.stroke();}
    // Muzzle emitter (glowing cyan ring)
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(0,200,255,0.25)';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(bx,by2, 4, 0, Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(60,220,255,0.8)';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(bx,by2, 1.8, 0, Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(200,240,255,0.95)';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(bx,by2, 0.9, 0, Math.PI*2); c2.fill();

    // Holographic sight
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(10,10,30,0.85)'; c2.strokeStyle='rgba(80,130,255,0.7)'; c2.lineWidth=0.7;
    let [hs0,hs1]=R(0.22,-2.2), [hs2,hs3]=R(0.44,-2.2), [hs4,hs5]=R(0.44,-3.2), [hs6,hs7]=R(0.22,-3.2);
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(hs0,hs1); c2.lineTo(hs2,hs3); c2.lineTo(hs4,hs5); c2.lineTo(hs6,hs7); c2.closePath(); c2.fill(); c2.stroke();
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(0,220,255,0.4)';
    let [hc0,hc1]=R(0.33,-3.1);
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(hc0,hc1, 1.2, 0, Math.PI*2); c2.fill();

  } else if(gt===8){
    // ── Plasma Carbine (far future, compact, powerful) ─────────────
    // Round housing, plasma vents, heat dissipation fins

    // Main plasma housing (round, compact)
    c2.fillStyle='#0d0d18';
    let [h0,h1]=R(0.15,2.8), [h2,h3]=R(0.15,-2.8), [he0,he1]=R(0.80,2.2), [he2,he3]=R(0.80,-2.2);
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(h0,h1); c2.bezierCurveTo(h0,h1, he0,he1, he0,he1);
    c2.lineTo(he2,he3); c2.bezierCurveTo(he2,he3, h2,h3, h2,h3); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();

    // Stock (folds or is part of housing)
    c2.fillStyle='#111120';
    let [s0,s1]=R(0,2.5), [s2,s3]=R(0,-2.5), [sm0,sm1]=R(0.15,2.2), [sm2,sm3]=R(0.15,-2.2);
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(s0,s1); c2.lineTo(sm0,sm1); c2.lineTo(sm2,sm3); c2.lineTo(s2,s3); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();

    // Plasma chamber (glowing core visible through housing gaps)
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(255,100,0,0.2)';
    let [pc0,pc1]=R(0.35,2);
    c2.save(); c2.translate(pc0+rnx*2,pc1+rny*2);
    c2.rotate(Math.atan2(rdy,rdx));
    c2.beginPath(); c2.ellipse(0,0,5,2,0,0,Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(255,150,30,0.8)';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.ellipse(0,0,3,1.2,0,0,Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
    c2.restore();

    // Fins / vents (heat dissipation)
    c2.strokeStyle='rgba(255,120,30,0.6)'; c2.lineWidth=0.8;
    for(let i=0;i<5;i++){
      let [v0,v1]=R(0.20+i*0.10,-2.2),[v2,v3]=R(0.20+i*0.10,-3.2);
      c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(v0,v1); c2.lineTo(v2,v3); c2.stroke();
    }

    // Barrel / emitter cone
    c2.fillStyle='#222232';
    let [ba0,ba1]=R(0.80,2.0), [ba2,ba3]=R(0.80,-2.0), [bt0,bt1]=R(1,1.2), [bt2,bt3]=R(1,-1.2);
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(ba0,ba1); c2.lineTo(bt0,bt1); c2.lineTo(bt2,bt3); c2.lineTo(ba2,ba3); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();

    // Plasma burst at muzzle
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(255,80,0,0.3)';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(bx,by2, 5.5, 0, Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(255,140,30,0.7)';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(bx,by2, 3.2, 0, Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(255,220,100,0.95)';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(bx,by2, 1.5, 0, Math.PI*2); c2.fill();

  } else {
    // ── Void Disruptor (Tier 9+, reality-warping weapon) ──────────
    // Black crystalline structure, purple void energy, distorts space

    // Void crystal housing (spiky, angular, unsettling)
    c2.fillStyle='#050508';
    let [a0,a1]=R(0,2.5), [a2,a3]=R(0,-2.5), [am,amY]=R(0.65,2.0), [am2,am3]=R(0.65,-2.0);
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(a0,a1); c2.lineTo(am,amY); c2.lineTo(am2,am3); c2.lineTo(a2,a3); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();

    // Crystal spikes along the body
    c2.fillStyle='#100010';
    for(let i=0;i<5;i++){
      let [sp0,sp1]=R(0.08+i*0.11, 2.5),[sp2,sp3]=R(0.13+i*0.11,2.5);
      let [spt0,spt1]=R(0.105+i*0.11, 4.5);
      c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(sp0,sp1); c2.lineTo(spt0,spt1); c2.lineTo(sp2,sp3); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    }

    // Void core (pulsing void energy — animated via armSwing)
    const voidPulse = 0.5 + 0.5*Math.sin(armSwing*8);
    c2.fillStyle=`rgba(100,0,200,${0.15+voidPulse*0.12})`;
    let [vc0,vc1]=R(0.32,2.5);
    c2.save(); c2.translate(vc0+rnx*2,vc1+rny*2); c2.rotate(Math.atan2(rdy,rdx));
    c2.beginPath(); c2.ellipse(0,0,5,2.5,0,0,Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
    c2.fillStyle=`rgba(160,0,255,${0.6+voidPulse*0.3})`;
    c2.beginPath(); c2.ellipse(0,0,2.5,1.2,0,0,Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
    c2.restore();

    // Void distortion lines
    c2.strokeStyle=`rgba(180,0,255,${0.5+voidPulse*0.4})`; c2.lineWidth=0.7;
    for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
      let [vl0,vl1]=R(0.10+i*0.15,-2),[vl2,vl3]=R(0.15+i*0.15,2);
      c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(vl0,vl1); c2.lineTo(vl2,vl3); c2.stroke();
    }

    // Barrel (barely visible, reality distorted)
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(30,0,50,0.8)';
    let [vb0,vb1]=R(0.65,1.5), [vb2,vb3]=R(0.65,-1.5), [vbe0,vbe1]=R(1,0.8), [vbe2,vbe3]=R(1,-0.8);
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(vb0,vb1); c2.lineTo(vbe0,vbe1); c2.lineTo(vbe2,vbe3); c2.lineTo(vb2,vb3); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();

    // Muzzle void vortex
    c2.fillStyle=`rgba(80,0,160,${0.25+voidPulse*0.2})`;
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(bx,by2, 7, 0, Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
    c2.fillStyle=`rgba(150,0,255,${0.6+voidPulse*0.3})`;
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(bx,by2, 4, 0, Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
    c2.fillStyle=`rgba(220,80,255,${0.85+voidPulse*0.15})`;
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(bx,by2, 2, 0, Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(255,200,255,0.98)';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(bx,by2, 0.9, 0, Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
  }
}

function _renderFoxSprite(cd, animIdx, tierInfo){
  const armorTier = (tierInfo && tierInfo.armorTier) || 0;
  const bootTier  = (tierInfo && tierInfo.bootTier)  || 0;
  const {bounce,lp1,lp2,ts}=_foxAnimParams(animIdx);
  const base=cd.base, baseD=cd.baseD, helmD=cd.helmD, wh='#fff8f0', bk='#1a1a1a', ns='#2a2a2a', ey='#ffcc66', ep='#e8a0a0';


  const oc=document.createElement('canvas');
  oc.width=FOX_SW; oc.height=FOX_SH;
  const c2=oc.getContext('2d',{alpha:true,willReadFrequently:false});
  c2.translate(FOX_OX, FOX_OY);

  // Shadow
  c2.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.18)';
  c2.beginPath(); c2.ellipse(2,14,18,4,0,0,Math.PI*2); c2.fill();

  // Tail
  c2.save(); c2.translate(-14,-2+bounce); c2.rotate(-0.25+ts);
  c2.fillStyle=base; c2.beginPath();
  c2.moveTo(4,0); c2.bezierCurveTo(0,-4,-12,-6,-26,0);
  c2.bezierCurveTo(-28,4,-24,8,-18,8); c2.bezierCurveTo(-10,10,0,6,4,4);
  c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
  c2.fillStyle=wh; c2.beginPath();
  c2.moveTo(-18,2); c2.bezierCurveTo(-22,4,-26,0,-24,6);
  c2.bezierCurveTo(-22,8,-16,6,-14,4); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
  c2.restore();

  // Back legs
  c2.fillStyle=baseD;
  _foxLegOffscreen(c2,-5,lp2,bounce,bootTier); _foxLegOffscreen(c2,8,lp1,bounce,bootTier);

  // ── COAT TAILS (drawn before body so legs protrude in front) ────
  _drawMilitaryCoat(c2, base, baseD, helmD, cd.lite, bounce, ts);

  // Body (fur, mostly hidden by coat)
  c2.fillStyle=base; c2.beginPath();
  c2.moveTo(12,-4+bounce); c2.bezierCurveTo(10,-10+bounce,4,-12+bounce,-2,-10+bounce);
  c2.bezierCurveTo(-8,-10+bounce,-12,-8+bounce,-14,-4+bounce);
  c2.bezierCurveTo(-15,0+bounce,-12,4+bounce,-8,4+bounce);
  c2.bezierCurveTo(-2,5+bounce,6,4+bounce,12,3+bounce);
  c2.bezierCurveTo(14,1+bounce,14,-2+bounce,12,-4+bounce);
  c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
  c2.fillStyle=wh; c2.beginPath();
  c2.moveTo(10,2+bounce); c2.bezierCurveTo(4,5+bounce,-4,5+bounce,-10,4+bounce);
  c2.bezierCurveTo(-8,2+bounce,-2,3+bounce,4,3+bounce); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();

  // Front legs
  c2.fillStyle=base;
  _foxLegOffscreen(c2,-2,lp1,bounce,bootTier); _foxLegOffscreen(c2,10,lp2,bounce,bootTier);

  // Body armour (drawn over coat, under neck/head)
  _drawBodyArmor(c2, armorTier, bounce);

  // ── RIFLE ARM ── right/front arm gripping the rifle at slope arms
  // Arm animates with lp1 (same phase as front-left leg) so it swings naturally
  const armSwing = lp1 * 0.8;
  const armGripX = 15 + armSwing * 0.5;
  const armGripY = -7 + bounce + armSwing * 0.3;

  // Upper arm (shoulder to elbow)
  c2.strokeStyle = base; c2.lineWidth = 3.5; c2.lineCap = 'round';
  c2.beginPath();
  c2.moveTo(11, -7+bounce);     // shoulder
  c2.lineTo(armGripX, armGripY); // grip
  c2.stroke();
  // Coat sleeve over arm
  c2.strokeStyle = baseD; c2.lineWidth = 3.0;
  c2.beginPath();
  c2.moveTo(10.5, -6+bounce);
  c2.lineTo(armGripX+0.5, armGripY+0.5);
  c2.stroke();
  // Cuff detail (slightly lighter)
  c2.strokeStyle = base; c2.lineWidth = 1.5;
  c2.beginPath();
  c2.arc(armGripX, armGripY, 2.0, 0, Math.PI*2);
  c2.stroke();
  // Paw
  c2.fillStyle = base;
  c2.beginPath(); c2.ellipse(armGripX, armGripY, 2.5, 2.0, 0.3, 0, Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
  c2.fillStyle = '#2a2a2a';
  // Claw hints
  for(let ci=0;ci<3;ci++){
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(armGripX+ci*0.8-0.8, armGripY+1.8, 0.6, 0, Math.PI); c2.fill();
  }

  // ── RIFLE (slope-arms position) ──────────────────────────────────
  _drawRifle(c2, armorTier, bounce, armSwing);

  // Muzzle stub (neck connection)
  c2.fillStyle=base; c2.beginPath();
  c2.moveTo(10,-6+bounce); c2.bezierCurveTo(14,-10+bounce,18,-12+bounce,22,-10+bounce);
  c2.lineTo(20,-4+bounce); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();

  // Head
  c2.fillStyle=base; c2.beginPath();
  c2.moveTo(14,-8+bounce); c2.bezierCurveTo(16,-18+bounce,22,-20+bounce,28,-16+bounce);
  c2.bezierCurveTo(32,-15+bounce,38,-11+bounce,42,-7+bounce);
  c2.bezierCurveTo(40,-5+bounce,36,-3+bounce,30,-3+bounce);
  c2.bezierCurveTo(24,-4+bounce,18,-5+bounce,14,-6+bounce); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
  c2.fillStyle=wh; c2.beginPath();
  c2.moveTo(18,-5+bounce); c2.bezierCurveTo(24,-3+bounce,30,-3+bounce,34,-5+bounce);
  c2.bezierCurveTo(32,-2+bounce,26,0+bounce,20,-1+bounce); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
  c2.fillStyle='#ffeedd'; c2.beginPath();
  c2.moveTo(34,-5+bounce); c2.bezierCurveTo(38,-6+bounce,40,-7+bounce,42,-7+bounce);
  c2.bezierCurveTo(40,-5+bounce,36,-4+bounce,34,-5+bounce); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();

  // Ears
  c2.fillStyle=baseD;
  c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(18,-14+bounce); c2.lineTo(16,-26+bounce); c2.lineTo(24,-16+bounce); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
  c2.fillStyle=base;
  c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(22,-16+bounce); c2.lineTo(20,-28+bounce); c2.lineTo(30,-18+bounce); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
  c2.fillStyle=ep;
  c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(23,-17+bounce); c2.lineTo(21.5,-24+bounce); c2.lineTo(27,-19+bounce); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();



  // Muzzle stub
  c2.fillStyle=base; c2.beginPath();
  c2.moveTo(10,-6+bounce); c2.bezierCurveTo(14,-10+bounce,18,-12+bounce,22,-10+bounce);
  c2.lineTo(20,-4+bounce); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();

  // Head
  c2.fillStyle=base; c2.beginPath();
  c2.moveTo(14,-8+bounce); c2.bezierCurveTo(16,-18+bounce,22,-20+bounce,28,-16+bounce);
  c2.bezierCurveTo(32,-15+bounce,38,-11+bounce,42,-7+bounce);
  c2.bezierCurveTo(40,-5+bounce,36,-3+bounce,30,-3+bounce);
  c2.bezierCurveTo(24,-4+bounce,18,-5+bounce,14,-6+bounce); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
  c2.fillStyle=wh; c2.beginPath();
  c2.moveTo(18,-5+bounce); c2.bezierCurveTo(24,-3+bounce,30,-3+bounce,34,-5+bounce);
  c2.bezierCurveTo(32,-2+bounce,26,0+bounce,20,-1+bounce); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
  c2.fillStyle='#ffeedd'; c2.beginPath();
  c2.moveTo(34,-5+bounce); c2.bezierCurveTo(38,-6+bounce,40,-7+bounce,42,-7+bounce);
  c2.bezierCurveTo(40,-5+bounce,36,-4+bounce,34,-5+bounce); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();

  // Ears (drawn before helmet so helmet overlaps them naturally)
  c2.fillStyle=baseD;
  c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(18,-14+bounce); c2.lineTo(16,-26+bounce); c2.lineTo(24,-16+bounce); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
  c2.fillStyle=base;
  c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(22,-16+bounce); c2.lineTo(20,-28+bounce); c2.lineTo(30,-18+bounce); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
  c2.fillStyle=ep;
  c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(23,-17+bounce); c2.lineTo(21.5,-24+bounce); c2.lineTo(27,-19+bounce); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();

  // ── HELMET by armorTier ────────────────────────────────────────
  const hcx=28, hcy=-14+bounce, hr=11;
  if(armorTier===0){
    // No armour — bare head, no helmet drawn
  } else if(armorTier===1){
    // Leather cap — full dome sitting at hcy (head widest point), plus earflap
    // Dome: PI→0 gives chord at y=hcy=-14, exactly matching head width (hr=11 each side)
    c2.fillStyle='#8B5E3C';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(hcx,hcy,hr*1.02,Math.PI,0); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    c2.strokeStyle='#5a3a1a'; c2.lineWidth=0.7; c2.stroke();
    // Brim band along the base chord
    c2.fillStyle='#5a3a1a';
    c2.fillRect(hcx-hr*1.02,hcy-0.5,hr*2.04,2.5);
    // Centre seam running from brim to top of dome
    c2.strokeStyle='#6a3a18'; c2.lineWidth=0.6;
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(hcx,hcy-hr*0.9); c2.lineTo(hcx,hcy-0.5); c2.stroke();
  } else if(armorTier===2){
    // Chainmail coif — full dome (PI→0) so base sits at hcy, plus brim and rings
    c2.fillStyle='#8c8c8c';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(hcx,hcy,hr*1.12,Math.PI,0); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    c2.strokeStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.3)'; c2.lineWidth=0.6; c2.stroke();
    // Brim at base chord
    c2.fillStyle='#6a6a6a'; c2.fillRect(hcx-hr*1.25,hcy-0.5,hr*2.5,hr*0.36);
    // Ring-link hints drawn just below the brim, at eye level (hcy+2)
    c2.strokeStyle='rgba(100,100,100,0.7)'; c2.lineWidth=0.7;
    for(let i=0;i<3;i++){c2.beginPath();c2.arc(hcx-3+i*4,hcy+2,1.8,0,Math.PI*2);c2.stroke();}
  } else if(armorTier===3){
    // Plate bucket helmet — dome + face plate + T-visor all connected
    c2.fillStyle='#4a4a4a';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(hcx,hcy,hr*1.2,Math.PI,0); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    c2.strokeStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.5)'; c2.lineWidth=0.8; c2.stroke();
    // Wide brim at base
    c2.fillStyle='#333'; c2.fillRect(hcx-hr*1.45,hcy-0.5,hr*2.9,hr*0.42);
    // Face plate on right side — connects dome base down to chin level
    c2.fillStyle='#3a3a3a';
    c2.fillRect(hcx+2,hcy,hr*1.1,8);
    // T-visor at eye level (hcy+2, eye is at hcy+3)
    c2.fillStyle='#1a1a1a';
    c2.fillRect(hcx+3,hcy+2,9,3);     // horizontal bar
    c2.fillRect(hcx+8,hcy+2,3.5,6);   // vertical slot
  } else if(armorTier===4){
    // Modern ACH — full dome (PI→0), brim, front face plate
    c2.fillStyle='#5c6e40';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(hcx,hcy,hr*1.15,Math.PI,0); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    c2.strokeStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.35)'; c2.lineWidth=0.7; c2.stroke();
    // Brim at base
    c2.fillStyle='#4a5a32'; c2.fillRect(hcx-hr*1.28,hcy-0.5,hr*2.56,hr*0.38);
    // Front face plate connecting dome to brow (x=hcx to hcx+hr on face side)
    c2.fillStyle='#4a5832';
    c2.fillRect(hcx+3,hcy,hr*0.9,6);
    // Front lip (upswept visor brow)
    c2.fillStyle='#3a4a28';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(hcx+3,hcy); c2.lineTo(hcx+hr*1.3,hcy); c2.lineTo(hcx+hr*1.1,hcy+3); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
  } else if(armorTier===5){
    // Composite tactical — dark dome, face plate, visor strip at eye level
    c2.fillStyle='#1e2028';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(hcx,hcy,hr*1.18,Math.PI,0); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    c2.strokeStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.5)'; c2.lineWidth=0.8; c2.stroke();
    // Brim
    c2.fillStyle='#15161c'; c2.fillRect(hcx-hr*1.4,hcy-0.5,hr*2.8,hr*0.38);
    // Face plate on right (face) side
    c2.fillStyle='#18181f';
    c2.fillRect(hcx+3,hcy,hr,7);
    // Visor strip at eye level (hcy+2, eye centre at hcy+3)
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(80,130,170,0.75)'; c2.fillRect(hcx+4,hcy+2,hr-1,2.5);
    // Side rail on RIGHT side of dome (face side, x=hcx+hr)
    c2.strokeStyle='#333'; c2.lineWidth=1;
