    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(hcx+hr,hcy-3); c2.lineTo(hcx+hr*1.12,hcy); c2.stroke();
  } else if(armorTier===6){
    // Exo-powered helmet — near-black dome + face plate + cyan glow visor
    c2.fillStyle='#12121a';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(hcx,hcy,hr*1.2,Math.PI,0); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    c2.strokeStyle='rgba(0,200,255,0.45)'; c2.lineWidth=0.9; c2.stroke();
    // Brim
    c2.fillStyle='#0d0d14'; c2.fillRect(hcx-hr*1.4,hcy-0.5,hr*2.8,hr*0.38);
    // Face plate (right/face side)
    c2.fillStyle='#0d0d1a';
    c2.fillRect(hcx+3,hcy,hr,7);
    // Cyan glow visor at eye level inside face plate
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(0,220,255,0.8)'; c2.fillRect(hcx+4,hcy+2,hr-2,3);
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(0,240,255,0.2)'; c2.fillRect(hcx+3,hcy+1,hr,5);
  } else if(armorTier===7){
    // Android Combat Helmet — polished chrome dome, full face plate, orange visor
    // Chrome skull cap
    c2.fillStyle='#b8b8cc';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(hcx,hcy,hr*1.25,Math.PI,0); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    // Highlight stripe
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,0.3)';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(hcx-2,hcy-2,hr*0.7,Math.PI+0.4,-0.4); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    // Brim
    c2.fillStyle='#8888a0'; c2.fillRect(hcx-hr*1.35,hcy-0.5,hr*2.7,hr*0.35);
    // Face plate (right side where the face is)
    c2.fillStyle='#9090a8';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(hcx+2,hcy-0.5); c2.lineTo(hcx+hr*1.2,hcy-0.5);
    c2.lineTo(hcx+hr*1.2,hcy+5); c2.lineTo(hcx+2,hcy+5); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    // Orange visor slit at eye level
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(255,120,0,0.9)'; c2.fillRect(hcx+3,hcy+2,10,2.5);
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(255,180,0,0.25)'; c2.fillRect(hcx+2,hcy+1,12,4.5);
  } else if(armorTier===8){
    // Quantum Phase Helm — dark blue-black dome, cyan halo ring, blue visor
    // Deep space dome
    c2.fillStyle='#060618';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(hcx,hcy,hr*1.28,Math.PI,0); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    // Phase-field halo ring around dome
    c2.strokeStyle='rgba(80,200,255,0.75)'; c2.lineWidth=1.4;
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(hcx,hcy,hr*1.38,Math.PI+0.2,-0.2); c2.stroke();
    // Second fainter ring
    c2.strokeStyle='rgba(80,200,255,0.3)'; c2.lineWidth=0.6;
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(hcx,hcy,hr*1.5,Math.PI+0.35,-0.35); c2.stroke();
    // Brim
    c2.fillStyle='#04041a'; c2.fillRect(hcx-hr*1.4,hcy-0.5,hr*2.8,hr*0.35);
    // Cyan visor at eye level
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(60,210,255,0.85)'; c2.fillRect(hcx+3,hcy+2,10,2.5);
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(100,230,255,0.2)'; c2.fillRect(hcx+1,hcy+1,13,4.5);
    // Centre glow dot on visor
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(180,245,255,0.9)';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(hcx+9,hcy+3.25,1.2,0,Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
  } else {
    // Tier 9+ Void Helm — absolute black, purple distortion rings, bright core eye
    // Void-black skull
    c2.fillStyle='#020204';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(hcx,hcy,hr*1.3,Math.PI,0); c2.closePath(); c2.fill();
    // Purple distortion arcs — concentric, only top half
    c2.strokeStyle='rgba(180,0,255,0.7)'; c2.lineWidth=1.2;
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(hcx,hcy,hr*1.4,Math.PI+0.25,-0.25); c2.stroke();
    c2.strokeStyle='rgba(220,50,255,0.35)'; c2.lineWidth=0.7;
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(hcx,hcy,hr*1.55,Math.PI+0.45,-0.45); c2.stroke();
    // Brim
    c2.fillStyle='#080008'; c2.fillRect(hcx-hr*1.45,hcy-0.5,hr*2.9,hr*0.35);
    // Purple visor slit at eye level
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(200,0,255,0.85)'; c2.fillRect(hcx+3,hcy+2,10,2.5);
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(180,0,255,0.2)'; c2.fillRect(hcx+1,hcy+1,13,4.5);
    // Singularity core — bright purple-white dot at visor centre
    c2.fillStyle='rgba(250,150,255,0.95)';
    c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(hcx+8,hcy+3.25,1.4,0,Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
  }

  // Nose
  c2.fillStyle=ns; c2.beginPath(); c2.ellipse(42,-7+bounce,2.5,2,0.1,0,Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
  // Nose highlight
  c2.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,0.3)'; c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(41.2,-7.8+bounce,0.9,0,Math.PI*2); c2.fill();

  // Eye
  c2.fillStyle=ey; c2.beginPath(); c2.ellipse(30,-11+bounce,3.5,3,0.2,0,Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
  c2.fillStyle=bk; c2.beginPath(); c2.ellipse(30.5,-10.5+bounce,2.5,2.8,0,0,Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
  c2.fillStyle='#fff'; c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(32,-12+bounce,1.5,0,Math.PI*2); c2.fill();
  c2.beginPath(); c2.arc(29,-9+bounce,0.7,0,Math.PI*2); c2.fill();

  // Whiskers (three per side, fine lines)
  c2.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,0.5)'; c2.lineWidth=0.5; c2.lineCap='round';
  // Upper whiskers
  for(let w=0;w<3;w++){
    const wy=-5.5+w*1.2+bounce, wlen=6+w*0.8;
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(38,wy); c2.lineTo(38+wlen,wy-1-w*0.4); c2.stroke();
    c2.beginPath(); c2.moveTo(38,wy); c2.lineTo(38-wlen*0.6,wy-0.5); c2.stroke();
  }

  return oc;
}

function _foxSpriteKey(cd,animIdx,dir,tierInfo){
  const at=(tierInfo&&tierInfo.armorTier)||0,bt=(tierInfo&&tierInfo.bootTier)||0;
  return cd.base+'_'+animIdx+(dir<0?'_r':'')+'_a'+at+'_b'+bt;
}
function _getFoxSprite(cd, animIdx, dir, tierInfo, suppressPromotion=false){
  // Cache key includes tier so each upgrade level gets its own sprite set.
  const key=_foxSpriteKey(cd,animIdx,dir,tierInfo);
  let spr=_foxSpriteCache.get(key);
  // LRU touch bounds GPU/Canvas memory while keeping hot visible variants resident.
  if(spr){_foxSpriteCache.delete(key);_foxSpriteCache.set(key,spr);}
  if(!spr){
    const base=_renderFoxSprite(cd,animIdx,tierInfo);
    if(dir<0){
      const f=document.createElement('canvas');
      f.width=FOX_SW; f.height=FOX_SH;
      const fc=f.getContext('2d',{alpha:true,willReadFrequently:false});
      fc.translate(FOX_SW,0); fc.scale(-1,1);
      fc.drawImage(base,0,0);
      spr=f;
    } else {
      spr=base;
    }
    _foxSpriteCache.set(key,spr);
    while(_foxSpriteCache.size>_FOX_SPRITE_CACHE_MAX){const k=_foxSpriteCache.keys().next().value,old=_foxSpriteCache.get(k);if(typeof ImageBitmap!=='undefined'&&old instanceof ImageBitmap)old.close?.();_foxSpriteCache.delete(k);}
    if(!suppressPromotion)_promoteToBitmap(key, spr); // desktop-only generic promotion; mobile stays single-copy Canvas2D
  }
  return spr;
}

// Call on scenario restart to flush stale sprites
let _foxSpriteVersion=1;
function _clearFoxSpriteCache(){_foxSpriteVersion++;for(const spr of _foxSpriteCache.values())if(typeof ImageBitmap!=='undefined'&&spr instanceof ImageBitmap&&spr.close)spr.close();_foxSpriteCache.clear();_foxBitmapQueue.length=0;if(typeof _invalidateBotSpriteLayers==='function')_invalidateBotSpriteLayers();if(typeof _resetBotLayerWorkerSpriteCache==='function')_resetBotLayerWorkerSpriteCache();if(typeof _resetUnitGLAtlas==='function')_resetUnitGLAtlas();}

// Returns {armorTier, bootTier} for any tech Set (player or bot).
// Pass the civ's techs Set directly.
function _foxTierInfoFromSet(techs){
  if(!techs || !techs.size) return {armorTier:0, bootTier:0};
  const armorIds=['arm1','arm2','arm3','arm4','arm5','arm6','arm7','arm8','arm9','arm10','arm11','arm12'];
  const bootIds =['bot1','bot2','bot3','bot4','bot5','bot6','bot7','bot8','bot9','bot10','bot11','bot12'];
  let at=0, bt=0;
  for(let i=armorIds.length-1;i>=0;i--){ if(techs.has(armorIds[i])){ at=i+1; break; } }
  for(let i=bootIds.length-1; i>=0;i--){ if(techs.has(bootIds[i])) { bt=i+1; break; } }
  return {armorTier:at, bootTier:bt};
}
// Convenience wrapper — works for player (uses playerTechs) and bots (uses civ.techs).
// Tier state changes only when the sprite/tech generation changes. Cache the result per civ so
// rendering 50-200 visible countries does not repeat up to 24 Set.has() probes per country per frame.
const _EMPTY_FOX_TIER={armorTier:0,bootTier:0};
let _playerFoxTierVersion=0,_playerFoxTierCache=_EMPTY_FOX_TIER;
function _foxTierInfo(civId){
  if(civId===playerCivId){
    if(_playerFoxTierVersion!==_foxSpriteVersion){_playerFoxTierCache=_foxTierInfoFromSet(playerTechs);_playerFoxTierVersion=_foxSpriteVersion;}
    return _playerFoxTierCache;
  }
  const civ=civs[civId];if(!civ)return _EMPTY_FOX_TIER;
  if(civ._foxTierVersion!==_foxSpriteVersion){civ._foxTierCache=_foxTierInfoFromSet(civ.techs);civ._foxTierVersion=_foxSpriteVersion;}
  return civ._foxTierCache||_EMPTY_FOX_TIER;
}
// Player AUTO MOVE pathfinding uses reusable typed buffers and a multi-target BFS.
// This avoids allocating Map/Array state for every target province on every army plan tick.
let _playerPlanPrev=new Int32Array(0),_playerPlanQueue=new Int32Array(0);
let _playerPlanSeen=new Uint32Array(0),_playerPlanTargets=new Uint32Array(0);
let _playerPlanSeenStamp=0,_playerPlanTargetStamp=0;
function _ensurePlayerPlanBuffers(){
  const n=provinces.length;
  if(_playerPlanPrev.length===n)return;
  _playerPlanPrev=new Int32Array(n);
  _playerPlanQueue=new Int32Array(n);
  _playerPlanSeen=new Uint32Array(n);
  _playerPlanTargets=new Uint32Array(n);
  _playerPlanSeenStamp=0;_playerPlanTargetStamp=0;
}
function _nextPlayerPlanSeenStamp(){
  _playerPlanSeenStamp=(_playerPlanSeenStamp+1)>>>0;
  if(_playerPlanSeenStamp===0){_playerPlanSeen.fill(0);_playerPlanSeenStamp=1;}
  return _playerPlanSeenStamp;
}
function _nextPlayerPlanTargetStamp(){
  _playerPlanTargetStamp=(_playerPlanTargetStamp+1)>>>0;
  if(_playerPlanTargetStamp===0){_playerPlanTargets.fill(0);_playerPlanTargetStamp=1;}
  return _playerPlanTargetStamp;
}
function _playerPlanPathToAny(startPid,targetPids,mode='war'){
  if(startPid<0||!targetPids||!targetPids.length||!provinces[startPid])return null;
  _ensurePlayerPlanBuffers();
  const targetStamp=_nextPlayerPlanTargetStamp();
  let targetCount=0;
  for(const raw of targetPids){
    const pid=raw|0;
    if(pid<0||pid>=provinces.length||!provinces[pid])continue;
    if(_playerPlanTargets[pid]!==targetStamp){_playerPlanTargets[pid]=targetStamp;targetCount++;}
  }
  if(!targetCount)return null;
  if(_playerPlanTargets[startPid]===targetStamp)return [startPid];
  const friendlyOnly=mode==='friendly';
  function bfs(allowSea){
    // War-plan AUTO MOVE can reject a completely unreachable target set from the
    // same cached legal-component graph used by bots. This prevents repeated
    // full-world BFS scans when a player front/enclave has no possible route.
    if(!friendlyOnly){
      const startComp=_routeComponentId(startPid,playerCivId,allowSea);if(startComp<0)return null;
      let anyReachable=false;
      for(const raw of targetPids){const pid=raw|0;if(pid<0||pid>=provinces.length||!provinces[pid])continue;if(_routeComponentId(pid,playerCivId,allowSea)===startComp){anyReachable=true;break;}}
      if(!anyReachable)return null;
    }
    const seenStamp=_nextPlayerPlanSeenStamp();
    let head=0,tail=0;
    _playerPlanQueue[tail++]=startPid;_playerPlanSeen[startPid]=seenStamp;_playerPlanPrev[startPid]=-1;
    while(head<tail){
      const cur=_playerPlanQueue[head++];
      for(let _ai=_adjBegin(cur),_ae=_adjEnd(cur);_ai<_ae;_ai++){const n=_adjList[_ai];
        if(n<0||n>=provinces.length||_playerPlanSeen[n]===seenStamp)continue;
        const p=provinces[n];if(!p)continue;
        if(p.isSea&&!allowSea)continue;
        const owner=provOwner[n];
        const legal=friendlyOnly
          ? (p.isSea||owner===playerCivId||(owner>=0&&areAllied(playerCivId,owner)))
          : (p.isSea||owner<0||owner===playerCivId||(owner>=0&&areAllied(playerCivId,owner))||(owner>=0&&areAtWar(playerCivId,owner)));
        if(!legal)continue;
        _playerPlanSeen[n]=seenStamp;_playerPlanPrev[n]=cur;
        if(_playerPlanTargets[n]===targetStamp){
          let len=1,x=n;while(x!==startPid){x=_playerPlanPrev[x];if(x<0)return null;len++;}
          const path=new Array(len);x=n;
          for(let i=len-1;i>=0;i--){path[i]=x;if(i)x=_playerPlanPrev[x];}
          return path;
        }
        _playerPlanQueue[tail++]=n;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }
  // Prefer land routes for ordinary fronts; only open sea lanes when needed.
  return bfs(false)||bfs(true);
}
let _frontTargetMark=new Uint32Array(0),_frontSeenMark=new Uint32Array(0),_frontQueue=new Int32Array(0),_frontMarkStamp=1;
function _frontComponents(targets){
  const n=provinces.length;if(_frontTargetMark.length<n){_frontTargetMark=new Uint32Array(n);_frontSeenMark=new Uint32Array(n);_frontQueue=new Int32Array(n);_frontMarkStamp=1;}
  let stamp=(++_frontMarkStamp)>>>0;if(stamp===0){_frontTargetMark.fill(0);_frontSeenMark.fill(0);_frontMarkStamp=stamp=1;}
  for(let i=0;i<targets.length;i++){const pid=targets[i];if(pid>=0&&pid<n)_frontTargetMark[pid]=stamp;}
  const lines=[];
  for(let si=0;si<targets.length;si++){const start=targets[si];if(start<0||start>=n||_frontSeenMark[start]===stamp)continue;
    const line=[];let qh=0,qt=0;_frontQueue[qt++]=start;_frontSeenMark[start]=stamp;
    while(qh<qt){const cur=_frontQueue[qh++];line.push(cur);for(let ai=_adjBegin(cur),ae=_adjEnd(cur);ai<ae;ai++){const v=_adjList[ai];if(_frontTargetMark[v]===stamp&&_frontSeenMark[v]!==stamp){_frontSeenMark[v]=stamp;_frontQueue[qt++]=v;}}}
    lines.push(line);
  }
  return lines;
}

function _playerWarFrontLines(){
  if(playerCivId<0)return [];
  const enemies=_warEnemiesFor(playerCivId).filter(id=>civs[id]?.ownedProvs?.size>0);
  if(!enemies.length)return [];

  // Treat every disconnected enemy landmass as an independent front objective.
  // A landmass touching our/allied land targets its contact line; an island or
  // overseas enclave targets its coast, so AUTO MOVE assigns actual amphibious
  // forces instead of ignoring islands whenever a continental front exists.
  const friendlyOwners=new Set([playerCivId]);
  const alliance=getAllianceOfCiv(playerCivId);
  if(alliance)for(const cid of alliance.memberIds)friendlyOwners.add(cid);
  const lines=[];
  for(const eid of enemies){
    const enemyLand=new Set();
    for(const pid of civs[eid].ownedProvs){const p=provinces[pid];if(p&&!p.isSea)enemyLand.add(pid);}
    const seen=new Set();
    for(const start of enemyLand){
      if(seen.has(start))continue;
      const component=[],contact=[],coastal=[],q=[start];seen.add(start);
      for(let qi=0;qi<q.length;qi++){
        const pid=q[qi],p=provinces[pid];component.push(pid);
        let isContact=false,isCoast=false;
        for(let _ai=_adjBegin(pid),_ae=_adjEnd(pid);_ai<_ae;_ai++){const n=_adjList[_ai];
          const np=provinces[n];if(!np)continue;
          if(np.isSea){isCoast=true;continue;}
          const owner=provOwner[n];
          if(friendlyOwners.has(owner))isContact=true;
          if(enemyLand.has(n)&&!seen.has(n)){seen.add(n);q.push(n);}
        }
        if(isContact)contact.push(pid);
        if(isCoast)coastal.push(pid);
      }
      // Direct front first. Remote islands/exclaves get coastal landing objectives.
      // Landlocked remote pockets fall back to the whole component.
      lines.push(..._frontComponents(contact.length?contact:(coastal.length?coastal:component)));
    }
  }
  return lines;
}

function _playerBorderGuardLines(mode,targetIds=[]){
  if(playerCivId<0||!civs[playerCivId]) return [];
  const wanted=new Set(targetIds||[]),guards=[];
  for(const pid of civs[playerCivId].ownedProvs){
    const p=provinces[pid]; if(!p||p.isSea) continue;
    for(let _ai=_adjBegin(pid),_ae=_adjEnd(pid);_ai<_ae;_ai++){const n=_adjList[_ai];
      const np=provinces[n]; if(!np) continue;
      const owner=provOwner[n];
      // "Own Country Border" means the whole national perimeter: foreign/
      // unclaimed LAND borders plus every COASTLINE touching a navigable sea
      // province. Armies still stand on the player's coastal land province;
      // sea provinces remain uncaptureable and are never used as guard posts.
      const hit = mode==='own'
        ? (np.isSea || owner!==playerCivId)
        : (!np.isSea && owner>=0 && owner!==playerCivId && wanted.has(owner));
      if(hit){guards.push(pid);break;}
    }
  }
  return _frontComponents([...new Set(guards)]);
}

function _provPlanDist(a,b){
  const pa=provinces[a],pb=provinces[b];if(!pa||!pb)return 1e12;
  const dx=(pa.centerTx||0)-(pb.centerTx||0),dy=(pa.centerTy||0)-(pb.centerTy||0);return dx*dx+dy*dy;
}

function _proportionalLineQuotas(lines,count){
  const q=new Int32Array(lines.length);if(count<=0||!lines.length)return q;
  const usable=lines.map((line,i)=>({i,n:line.length})).filter(x=>x.n>0);if(!usable.length)return q;
  let used=0;
  // Keep every disconnected front alive when manpower permits.
  if(count>=usable.length){for(const x of usable){q[x.i]=1;used++;}}
  else{usable.sort((a,b)=>b.n-a.n||a.i-b.i);for(let k=0;k<count;k++){q[usable[k].i]=1;used++;}}
  // Fill by worst troops-per-border-slot density, not equal units per landmass.
  const totalSlots=usable.reduce((z,x)=>z+x.n,0);
  while(used<count){let bi=-1,bs=-1;for(const x of usable){const i=x.i;if(q[i]>=x.n&&count<=totalSlots)continue;const score=x.n/(q[i]+1);if(score>bs){bs=score;bi=i;}}if(bi<0)break;q[bi]++;used++;}
  return q;
}

let _playerPlanRouteBudget=null;
function _assignBalancedPlan(divs,lines,pathToAnyFn,kind,budget=null){
  if(!divs.length)return false;
  const validLines=lines.filter(l=>l&&l.length),validTargets=new Set();
  for(const line of validLines)for(const pid of line)validTargets.add(pid);
  if(!validLines.length){
    for(const d of divs){d._planTargetPid=-1;if(d.path.length){d.path=[];d.moveProgress=0;}}
    return false;
  }

  const lineOf=new Map();validLines.forEach((line,i)=>line.forEach(pid=>lineOf.set(pid,i)));
  const lineLoad=new Int32Array(validLines.length),targetLoad=new Map();
  for(const pid of validTargets)targetLoad.set(pid,0);
  for(const d of divs){
    if(!validTargets.has(d._planTargetPid)){
      if(d._planTargetPid>=0){d.path=[];d.moveProgress=0;}d._planTargetPid=-1;continue;
    }
    const li=lineOf.get(d._planTargetPid);lineLoad[li]++;targetLoad.set(d._planTargetPid,(targetLoad.get(d._planTargetPid)||0)+1);
  }

  // Front topology can change after captures. Build balanced per-line quotas that
  // differ by at most one. Give the few extra slots to already-busy lines to minimize
  // churn, then release only true surplus armies. This is what guarantees a newly
  // exposed island/front actually receives troops even when every old target remains valid.
  const quota=_proportionalLineQuotas(validLines,divs.length);
  for(let li=0;li<validLines.length;li++){
    let excess=lineLoad[li]-quota[li];if(excess<=0)continue;
    // Never reverse a unit that is already travelling toward a still-valid target.
    // Rebalancing may move settled surplus troops; in-flight orders remain stable until
    // arrival or until topology makes their target invalid. This removes AUTO rubberbanding.
    const assigned=divs.filter(d=>d._planTargetPid>=0&&lineOf.get(d._planTargetPid)===li&&d.provId===d._planTargetPid&&d.path.length===0);
    assigned.sort((a,b)=>_provPlanDist(b.provId,b._planTargetPid)-_provPlanDist(a.provId,a._planTargetPid));
    for(let i=0;i<assigned.length&&excess>0;i++,excess--){
      const d=assigned[i],old=d._planTargetPid;
      lineLoad[li]=Math.max(0,lineLoad[li]-1);
      targetLoad.set(old,Math.max(0,(targetLoad.get(old)||1)-1));
      d._planTargetPid=-1;d.path=[];d.moveProgress=0;
    }
  }

  const need=divs.filter(d=>d._planTargetPid<0||(d.path.length===0&&d.provId!==d._planTargetPid));
  let pending=false;
  for(const d of need){
    if(budget&&budget.left<=0){pending=true;continue;}
    if(d._planTargetPid>=0&&d.provId===d._planTargetPid&&kind==='border')continue;
    if(d._planTargetPid>=0){
      const li=lineOf.get(d._planTargetPid);if(li!==undefined)lineLoad[li]=Math.max(0,lineLoad[li]-1);
      targetLoad.set(d._planTargetPid,Math.max(0,(targetLoad.get(d._planTargetPid)||1)-1));d._planTargetPid=-1;
    }

    // Lowest-loaded disconnected fronts first; distance is only a tie-breaker.
    const lineOrder=validLines.map((line,i)=>{
      let near=1e12;for(const pid of line)near=Math.min(near,_provPlanDist(d.provId,pid));
      const deficit=(quota[i]||0)-lineLoad[i];
      return{i,deficit,density:lineLoad[i]/Math.max(1,line.length),near};
    }).filter(x=>(quota[x.i]||0)>0).sort((a,b)=>b.deficit-a.deficit||a.density-b.density||a.near-b.near||a.i-b.i);

    let chosen=-1,chosenPath=null,chosenLine=-1;
    for(const lo of lineOrder){
      // Within a front, fill the least-used target provinces first. One multi-target
      // BFS chooses the nearest reachable member of that load tier.
      const byLoad=new Map();
      for(const pid of validLines[lo.i]){
        const load=targetLoad.get(pid)||0;
        let arr=byLoad.get(load);if(!arr){arr=[];byLoad.set(load,arr);}arr.push(pid);
      }
      const loads=[...byLoad.keys()].sort((a,b)=>a-b);
      for(const load of loads){
        const candidates=byLoad.get(load);
        if(budget)budget.left--;
        const path=pathToAnyFn(d,candidates);
        if(path&&path.length){chosen=path[path.length-1];chosenPath=path;chosenLine=lo.i;break;}
      }
      if(chosen>=0)break;
    }
    if(chosen<0)continue;
    d._planTargetPid=chosen;d._vGroupId=null;
    d.moveProgress=0;d.path=chosenPath&&chosenPath.length>1?chosenPath.slice(1):[];
    lineLoad[chosenLine]++;targetLoad.set(chosen,(targetLoad.get(chosen)||0)+1);
  }
  return pending;
}

let _playerArmyPlanDirty=true;

// ── 20K UNIVERSAL AUTO-MOVE FLOW FIELD ──────────────────────────────────────
// Universal AUTO MOVE never computes one route search per fox. A small number of
// reverse BFS "lanes" are built for the whole map. Every automated division reads
// one next-province integer from its lane. Cost therefore scales with map topology,
// not army count. Lanes use disjoint target subsets so a mass army fans across a
// front instead of drilling one straight corridor.
const _PLAYER_FLOW_LANES=32;
let _playerFlowLand=[],_playerFlowSea=[],_playerFlowMode='',_playerFlowSig='',_playerFlowDirty=true,_playerAutoFullKick=true,_playerFlowLaneCount=_PLAYER_FLOW_LANES;
let _playerFlowLegalStamp=0,_playerFlowCivId=-1;
let _playerFlowQ=new Int32Array(0),_playerFlowSeen=new Uint32Array(0),_playerFlowSeenStamp=1;
const _playerFlowFallbackTarget=new Int32Array(_PLAYER_FLOW_LANES);_playerFlowFallbackTarget.fill(-1);

// Stable flow-lane partitioning -------------------------------------------------
// Lane anchors persist while their target remains valid. New/removed frontline provinces
// therefore affect only the minimum number of lanes instead of re-indexing the whole army.
// When a front is wider than the lane count, every additional frontline province is still
// inserted into one deterministic lane, so stability never comes at the cost of coverage.
function _stableFlowHash32(x,salt=0){
  let h=((x|0)+1)^salt;h=Math.imul(h^h>>>16,0x7feb352d);h=Math.imul(h^h>>>15,0x846ca68b);return (h^h>>>16)>>>0;
}
function _stableFlowSeedLanes(targets,laneCount,salt,fallbackTargets){
  const lanes=Array.from({length:laneCount},()=>[]),uniq=[],seen=new Set();
  for(const raw of targets||[]){const pid=raw|0;if(pid<0||pid>=provinces.length||seen.has(pid))continue;seen.add(pid);uniq.push(pid);}
  uniq.sort((a,b)=>a-b);if(!uniq.length)return lanes;

  // Preserve every still-valid lane anchor. Only invalid lanes (or the minimum number
  // required to cover a newly-added target) are reassigned. This is consistent hashing
  // with explicit load balancing, so distribution stays even without path churn.
  const load=new Map();for(const pid of uniq)load.set(pid,0);
  const anchors=new Int32Array(laneCount);anchors.fill(-1);
  if(fallbackTargets)for(let l=0;l<laneCount;l++){
    const pid=fallbackTargets[l]|0;if(seen.has(pid)){anchors[l]=pid;load.set(pid,(load.get(pid)||0)+1);}
  }
  const pickLeastLoaded=(lane)=>{
    let bestPid=-1,bestLoad=Infinity,bestScore=-1;
    for(const pid of uniq){const ld=load.get(pid)||0,score=_stableFlowHash32(pid,salt^Math.imul(lane+1,0x9e3779b1));if(ld<bestLoad||(ld===bestLoad&&score>bestScore)){bestLoad=ld;bestScore=score;bestPid=pid;}}
    return bestPid;
  };
  for(let l=0;l<laneCount;l++)if(anchors[l]<0){const pid=pickLeastLoaded(l);anchors[l]=pid;load.set(pid,(load.get(pid)||0)+1);}

  // If there are at most as many targets as lanes, every target must own at least the
  // floor share of lanes. New frontline sectors therefore receive forces immediately,
  // but only the necessary lanes move away from an overloaded old sector.
  if(uniq.length<=laneCount){
    const low=Math.floor(laneCount/uniq.length),high=Math.ceil(laneCount/uniq.length);
    for(const want of uniq){
      while((load.get(want)||0)<low){
        let donor=-1,donorLoad=-1,bestScore=-1;
        for(let l=0;l<laneCount;l++){const cur=anchors[l],ld=load.get(cur)||0;if(cur===want||ld<=low)continue;const score=_stableFlowHash32(l,salt^Math.imul(want+1,0x85ebca6b));if(ld>donorLoad||(ld===donorLoad&&score>bestScore)){donor=l;donorLoad=ld;bestScore=score;}}
        if(donor<0)break;const old=anchors[donor];load.set(old,(load.get(old)||1)-1);anchors[donor]=want;load.set(want,(load.get(want)||0)+1);
      }
    }
  }
  const anchored=new Set();
  for(let l=0;l<laneCount;l++){const pid=anchors[l];if(pid>=0){lanes[l].push(pid);anchored.add(pid);if(fallbackTargets)fallbackTargets[l]=pid;}}
  // Wide fronts can contain more provinces than flow lanes. Never drop those extra front
  // slots: attach each unanchored target to a deterministic lane. Adding/removing one such
  // province then changes only that lane's reverse-BFS field, while every front province
  // remains represented and available to advancing units.
  if(uniq.length>laneCount)for(const pid of uniq){if(anchored.has(pid))continue;const lane=_stableFlowHash32(pid,salt)%laneCount;lanes[lane].push(pid);}
  for(const lane of lanes)lane.sort((a,b)=>a-b);
  return lanes;
}
function _traceCommittedFlowRoute(flow,startPid){
  if(!flow||startPid<0||startPid>=provinces.length)return null;
  let cur=startPid;const path=[];const max=provinces.length+1;
  for(let steps=0;steps<max;steps++){
    const next=flow[cur]??-1;if(next<0)return null;if(next===cur)return{path,target:cur};
    path.push(next);cur=next;
  }
  return null; // defensive cycle guard; reverse-BFS fields should never cycle
}
function _markPlayerArmyPlanDirty(full=false){_playerArmyPlanDirty=true;_playerFlowDirty=true;if(full)_playerAutoFullKick=true;}
function _ensurePlayerFlowBuffers(){
  const n=provinces.length;if(_playerFlowQ.length!==n){_playerFlowQ=new Int32Array(n);_playerFlowSeen=new Uint32Array(n);_playerFlowSeenStamp=1;}
  while(_playerFlowLand.length<_PLAYER_FLOW_LANES)_playerFlowLand.push(new Int32Array(n));
  while(_playerFlowSea.length<_PLAYER_FLOW_LANES)_playerFlowSea.push(new Int32Array(n));
  for(let i=0;i<_PLAYER_FLOW_LANES;i++){
    if(_playerFlowLand[i].length!==n)_playerFlowLand[i]=new Int32Array(n);
    if(_playerFlowSea[i].length!==n)_playerFlowSea[i]=new Int32Array(n);
  }
}
function _playerFlowNodeLegal(pid,mode,allowSea){
  const p=provinces[pid];if(!p)return false;if(p.isSea)return !!allowSea;
  const owner=provOwner[pid];
  if(mode==='friendly')return owner===playerCivId||(owner>=0&&areAllied(playerCivId,owner));
  return owner<0||owner===playerCivId||(owner>=0&&areAllied(playerCivId,owner))||(owner>=0&&areAtWar(playerCivId,owner));
}
function _buildOnePlayerFlow(out,seeds,mode,allowSea,distOut=null,targetOut=null){
  out.fill(-1);if(distOut)distOut.fill(distOut.BYTES_PER_ELEMENT===2?65535:0xffffffff);if(targetOut)targetOut.fill(-1);if(!seeds.length)return;
  let st=(++_playerFlowSeenStamp)>>>0;if(st===0){_playerFlowSeen.fill(0);_playerFlowSeenStamp=st=1;}
  let h=0,t=0;
  for(let i=0;i<seeds.length;i++){
    const pid=seeds[i]|0;if(pid<0||pid>=provinces.length||!_playerFlowNodeLegal(pid,mode,allowSea)||_playerFlowSeen[pid]===st)continue;
    _playerFlowSeen[pid]=st;out[pid]=pid;if(distOut)distOut[pid]=0;if(targetOut)targetOut[pid]=pid;_playerFlowQ[t++]=pid;
  }
  while(h<t){const cur=_playerFlowQ[h++];for(let ai=_adjBegin(cur),ae=_adjEnd(cur);ai<ae;ai++){
    const n=_adjList[ai];if(n<0||n>=provinces.length||_playerFlowSeen[n]===st||!_playerFlowNodeLegal(n,mode,allowSea))continue;
    _playerFlowSeen[n]=st;out[n]=cur;if(distOut)distOut[n]=distOut[cur]+1;if(targetOut)targetOut[n]=targetOut[cur];_playerFlowQ[t++]=n;
  }}
}
// Per-division reservations, independent of cosmetic groups. A lane may contain
// several front provinces; once one has its share, remove that seed from the lane
// so its less convenient neighbours also receive orders. Routes are shared by
// origin/lane, not searched afresh for every division.
function _newAutoCoverage(civId,mode,targets,lanes,land,sea,build,divs){
  const field=civId===playerCivId?'_planTargetPid':'_botPlanTargetPid';
  if(divs.length>0&&divs.length<targets.length){
    // With too few units to fill every province, cover separated fronts first
    // and spread the remaining posts geographically, retaining valid old posts.
    const lines=_frontComponents(targets),quota=_proportionalLineQuotas(lines,divs.length),chosen=new Set();
    const old=new Set();for(let i=0;i<divs.length;i++){const d=divs[i];if(d&&d.hp>0)old.add(d[field]);}
    for(let i=0;i<lines.length;i++){
      const count=quota[i];if(!count)continue;const line=lines[i],selected=[];
      for(const pid of line)if(old.has(pid)&&selected.length<count)selected.push(pid);
      if(selected.length<count)for(const pid of _evenCoverageTargets([line],count))if(!selected.includes(pid)&&selected.length<count)selected.push(pid);
      for(const pid of selected)chosen.add(pid);
    }
    targets=[...chosen];lanes=lanes.map(line=>line.filter(pid=>chosen.has(pid))).filter(line=>line.length);
    land=land.slice(0,lanes.length);sea=sea.slice(0,lanes.length);
  }
  const targetSet=new Set(targets),loads=new Map(),capacity=new Map();
  const state={civId,mode,targets:targetSet,loads,lanes,land,sea,build,
    limit:Math.max(1,Math.ceil(divs.length/Math.max(1,targets.length))),capacity,
    landDirty:new Uint8Array(lanes.length),seaDirty:new Uint8Array(lanes.length),activeSeeds:new Array(lanes.length),
    landDist:new Array(lanes.length),landTarget:new Array(lanes.length),landSingle:new Int32Array(lanes.length),
    targetLanes:new Map(),fallback:new Map(),revision:0};
  state.landSingle.fill(-1);
  state.landDirty.fill(1);state.seaDirty.fill(1);
  for(let l=0;l<lanes.length;l++)for(const pid of lanes[l]){let a=state.targetLanes.get(pid);if(!a){a=[];state.targetLanes.set(pid,a);}a.push(l);}
  const base=Math.max(1,Math.floor(divs.length/Math.max(1,targets.length)));
  const extra=Math.max(0,divs.length-base*targets.length);
  for(let i=0;i<targets.length;i++){const pid=targets[i];loads.set(pid,0);capacity.set(pid,base+(i<extra?1:0));}
  // Reserve settled posts first. Never evacuate an occupied border post merely
  // because another unit in the same visual stack happens to have a lower ID.
  for(let i=0;i<divs.length;i++){
    const d=divs[i];if(!d)continue;d._autoCoverageState=null;d._autoCoveragePid=-1;
    if(d.hp<=0)continue;
    if(mode==='friendly'&&!d.path.length&&targetSet.has(d.provId)&&(loads.get(d.provId)||0)<_autoCoverageCapacity(state,d.provId)){
      d[field]=d.provId;_reserveAutoCoverage(state,d,d.provId);
    }
  }
  for(let i=0;i<divs.length;i++){
    const d=divs[i];if(!d||d.hp<=0||d._autoCoverageState===state)continue;
    const target=d[field];
    const sameRole=civId===playerCivId?(d._autoWarRole===(mode==='war'?'front':'guard')):
      (d._botPlanRole===(mode==='war'?'attack':'border'));
    if(sameRole&&d.path.length&&d.path[d.path.length-1]===target&&targetSet.has(target)&&
      (loads.get(target)||0)<_autoCoverageCapacity(state,target))_reserveAutoCoverage(state,d,target);
  }
  return state;
}
function _autoCoverageCapacity(state,pid){return state.limit===Infinity?Infinity:(state.capacity.get(pid)||1);}
function _reserveAutoCoverage(state,div,pid){
  if(div._autoCoverageState===state&&div._autoCoveragePid===pid)return;
  _releaseAutoCoverage(div);
  div._autoCoverageState=state;div._autoCoveragePid=pid;
  const next=(state.loads.get(pid)||0)+1;state.loads.set(pid,next);
  if(next===_autoCoverageCapacity(state,pid))_markAutoCoverageTargetDirty(state,pid);
}
function _releaseAutoCoverage(div){
  const state=div?._autoCoverageState,pid=div?._autoCoveragePid;
  if(!state)return;
  const old=state.loads.get(pid)||0;state.loads.set(pid,Math.max(0,old-1));
  if(old===_autoCoverageCapacity(state,pid))_markAutoCoverageTargetDirty(state,pid);
  div._autoCoverageState=null;div._autoCoveragePid=-1;
}
function _markAutoCoverageTargetDirty(state,pid){
  const ls=state?.targetLanes?.get(pid);if(!ls)return;
  for(let i=0;i<ls.length;i++){const l=ls[i];state.landDirty[l]=1;state.seaDirty[l]=1;state.activeSeeds[l]=null;}
  state.revision=(state.revision+1)>>>0;state.fallback.clear();
}
function _autoCoverageSeeds(state,l){
  let seeds=state.activeSeeds[l];if(seeds)return seeds;
  const src=state.lanes[l],out=[];
  for(let i=0;i<src.length;i++){const pid=src[i];if((state.loads.get(pid)||0)<_autoCoverageCapacity(state,pid))out.push(pid);}
  state.activeSeeds[l]=out;return out;
}
function _ensureAutoCoverageLane(state,l,allowSea){
  if(!state||l<0||l>=state.lanes.length)return null;
  if(allowSea){
    if(state.seaDirty[l]){const seeds=_autoCoverageSeeds(state,l);state.build(state.sea[l],seeds,true,null,null);state.seaDirty[l]=0;}
    return state.sea[l];
  }
  if(state.landDirty[l]){
    const seeds=_autoCoverageSeeds(state,l),n=provinces.length;
    let dist=state.landDist[l];if(!dist||dist.length!==n)dist=state.landDist[l]=n<65535?new Uint16Array(n):new Uint32Array(n);
    let target=null;state.landSingle[l]=seeds.length===1?seeds[0]:-1;
    if(seeds.length>1){target=state.landTarget[l];if(!target||target.length!==n)target=state.landTarget[l]=new Int32Array(n);}else state.landTarget[l]=null;
    state.build(state.land[l],seeds,false,dist,target);state.landDirty[l]=0;
  }
  return state.land[l];
}
function _autoCoverageRouteForLane(state,start,l){
  const land=_ensureAutoCoverageLane(state,l,false);let r=_traceCommittedFlowRoute(land,start);if(r)return r;
  const sea=_ensureAutoCoverageLane(state,l,true);return _traceCommittedFlowRoute(sea,start);
}
let _autoFallbackLand=new Int32Array(0),_autoFallbackSea=new Int32Array(0);
function _ensureAutoFallbackBuffers(){const n=provinces.length;if(_autoFallbackLand.length!==n){_autoFallbackLand=new Int32Array(n);_autoFallbackSea=new Int32Array(n);}}
function _autoCoverageFallbackRoute(state,start,startLane=0){
  _ensureAutoFallbackBuffers();const n=state.lanes.length;if(!n)return null;
  for(let k=0;k<n;k++){
    const l=(startLane+k)%n,seeds=state.lanes[l];if(!seeds.length)continue;
    state.build(_autoFallbackLand,seeds,false);let r=_traceCommittedFlowRoute(_autoFallbackLand,start);if(r)return r;
    state.build(_autoFallbackSea,seeds,true);r=_traceCommittedFlowRoute(_autoFallbackSea,start);if(r)return r;
  }
  return null;
}
function _autoCoverageCandidates(state,start){
  // Compatibility/debug helper. The hot path no longer materialises routes for every
  // lane: _pickAutoCoverageRoute uses compact BFS metadata, then materialises only the winning route.
  const out=new Array(state?.lanes?.length||0);if(!state)return out;
  for(let l=0;l<out.length;l++)out[l]=_autoCoverageRouteForLane(state,start,l);
  return out;
}
function _pickAutoCoverageRoute(state,div,start=div.provId){
  if(!state||!state.targets.size||!state.lanes.length)return null;
  let bestLane=-1,bestTarget=-1,bestLoad=Infinity,bestDist=Infinity;
  // Exact baseline decision, without baseline cost: reverse BFS now records the reached
  // seed + distance. We scan 32 tiny integers, then materialise ONE route instead of
  // tracing/copying up to 32 complete province paths for every division.
  for(let l=0;l<state.lanes.length;l++){
    const flow=_ensureAutoCoverageLane(state,l,false);if(!flow||flow[start]===-1)continue;
    const ta=state.landTarget[l],target=ta?ta[start]:state.landSingle[l];if(target<0)continue;
    const dist=state.landDist[l]?.[start]??Infinity,load=state.loads.get(target)||0;
    if(load<bestLoad||(load===bestLoad&&(dist<bestDist||(dist===bestDist&&l<bestLane)))){bestLane=l;bestTarget=target;bestLoad=load;bestDist=dist;}
  }
  if(bestLane>=0){const r=_traceCommittedFlowRoute(state.land[bestLane],start);if(r)return r;}

  // Land-unreachable fronts (islands/overseas enclaves) keep the exact old sea fallback.
  // Sea fields are built only here, so continental wars no longer pay for them at all.
  let best=null;bestLoad=Infinity;
  for(let l=0;l<state.lanes.length;l++){
    const sea=_ensureAutoCoverageLane(state,l,true),r=_traceCommittedFlowRoute(sea,start);if(!r)continue;
    const load=state.loads.get(r.target)||0;if(load<bestLoad||(load===bestLoad&&(!best||r.path.length<best.path.length))){best=r;bestLoad=load;}
  }
  if(best)return best;

  // Preserve the old surplus rule: if this component cannot reach any target that still
  // has nominal capacity, allow it to reinforce a reachable target instead of parking.
  let cached=state.fallback.get(start);if(cached)return{path:cached.path.slice(),target:cached.target};
  const r=_autoCoverageFallbackRoute(state,start,0);
  if(r){state.fallback.set(start,{path:r.path.slice(),target:r.target});if(state.fallback.size>512)state.fallback.delete(state.fallback.keys().next().value);}
  return r;
}
function _retargetAutoCoverage(div,state){
  const player=div.civId===playerCivId,field=player?'_planTargetPid':'_botPlanTargetPid';
  const role=state.mode==='war'?(player?'front':'attack'):(player?'guard':'border');
  const roleField=player?'_autoWarRole':'_botPlanRole';
  if(div._autoCoverageState===state&&state.targets.has(div[field]))return false;
  _releaseAutoCoverage(div);
  // Finish only the current legal edge, preserving fractional movement and its
  // scheduled arrival. Replace the obsolete tail immediately, not on old-target arrival.
  const next=div.path[0],progress=div.moveProgress;
  const keepEdge=next!==undefined&&progress>0&&_autoCoverageNodeLegal(next,state);
  const start=keepEdge?next:div.provId;
  const route=_pickAutoCoverageRoute(state,div,start);
  const path=route?(keepEdge?[next,...route.path]:route.path):(keepEdge?[next]:[]);
  div[field]=route?route.target:-1;div[roleField]=role;div._vGroupId=null;
  if(route)_reserveAutoCoverage(state,div,route.target);
  if(keepEdge){
    // The first edge is unchanged, so its safe-move registration, blocked state,
    // facing and visual interpolation remain correct. Only the tail changes.
    div._path=path;
  }else{if(div.path.length)div.path=[];div.moveProgress=0;div.path=path;}
  return !!path.length;
}
function _autoCoverageNodeLegal(pid,state){
  const p=provinces[pid];if(!p)return false;if(p.isSea)return true;
  const owner=provOwner[pid];
  return owner===state.civId||(owner>=0&&areAllied(state.civId,owner))||
    (state.mode==='war'&&((owner>=0&&areAtWar(state.civId,owner))||(owner<0&&state.civId===playerCivId)));
}
let _playerAutoCoverage=null;
function _playerAutoObjectives(){
  // AUTO MOVE has an explicit two-state contract:
  //   PEACE -> guard the player's own national perimeter.
  //   WAR   -> advance toward active enemy fronts.
  // Do not infer peacetime from whether a cached/front calculation happened to be empty;
  // use the live war graph so ending the final war always switches straight back to guard mode.
  if(!_playerHasActiveWars())return{mode:'friendly',lines:_playerBorderGuardLines('own',[])};
  const warLines=_playerWarFrontLines();
  return warLines.length?{mode:'war',lines:warLines}:{mode:'friendly',lines:_playerBorderGuardLines('own',[])};
}
function _buildPlayerAutoFlow(){
  if(playerCivId<0||!civs[playerCivId])return false;_ensurePlayerFlowBuffers();
  const obj=_playerAutoObjectives(),targets=[];const seen=new Set();
  for(const line of obj.lines||[])for(const raw of line||[]){const pid=raw|0;if(pid>=0&&pid<provinces.length&&!seen.has(pid)){seen.add(pid);targets.push(pid);}}
  targets.sort((a,b)=>a-b);
  const sig=obj.mode+'|'+targets.join(',');
  if(!_playerFlowDirty&&sig===_playerFlowSig)return targets.length>0;
  _playerFlowDirty=false;_playerFlowSig=sig;_playerFlowMode=obj.mode;
  _playerFlowLegalStamp=_routeLegalStamp(playerCivId);_playerFlowCivId=playerCivId;
  if(!targets.length){_playerFlowLaneCount=0;_playerAutoCoverage=null;return false;}

  // Repeating one target across 32 lanes produced identical reverse-BFS fields. Use at
  // most one lane per target until the front is wider than the 32-lane cap: same coverage,
  // dramatically less graph work and memory traffic on small/medium fronts.
  const laneCount=Math.min(_PLAYER_FLOW_LANES,targets.length);_playerFlowLaneCount=laneCount;
  const seedLanes=_stableFlowSeedLanes(targets,laneCount,0x51f15e1d,_playerFlowFallbackTarget);
  const all=civs[playerCivId].foxes,divs=[];for(let i=0;i<all.length;i++){const d=all[i];if(d.hp>0&&d.autoMove)divs.push(d);}
  _playerAutoCoverage=_newAutoCoverage(playerCivId,obj.mode,targets,seedLanes,_playerFlowLand.slice(0,laneCount),_playerFlowSea.slice(0,laneCount),
    (out,seeds,sea,dist,target)=>_buildOnePlayerFlow(out,seeds,obj.mode,sea,dist,target),divs);
  return true;
}
function _playerCommittedAutoRoute(div){
  if(!div||playerCivId<0||!_playerAutoCoverage)return null;
  if(_playerFlowMode==='friendly'&&div._autoCoverageState===_playerAutoCoverage&&
    div._autoCoveragePid===div.provId&&_playerAutoCoverage.targets.has(div.provId))return null;
  _releaseAutoCoverage(div);
  return _pickAutoCoverageRoute(_playerAutoCoverage,div);
}
function _playerAutoNextPid(div){const r=_playerCommittedAutoRoute(div);return r?.path?.[0]??-1;}
function _playerAutoKick(div){
  if(!div||div.hp<=0||!playerAutoMoveUniversal||!div.autoMove||div.civId!==playerCivId||!gameReady)return false;
  if(div.path.length||div._blockedParked||div.combatCooldown>0)return false;
  if(_playerFlowDirty){_playerAutoFullKick=true;return false;}
  const route=_playerCommittedAutoRoute(div);if(!route||!route.path.length)return false;
  _reserveAutoCoverage(_playerAutoCoverage,div,route.target);
  div._planTargetPid=route.target;div._autoWarRole=_playerFlowMode==='war'?'front':'guard';div._vGroupId=null;div.moveProgress=0;div.path=route.path;
  // Sparse simulation only ticks active divisions. A stationary division receiving a fresh
  // AUTO order must be inserted immediately or it can own a valid path while never moving.
  _syncDivActivity(div);
  return true;
}
function _retaskPlayerAutoToPeaceBorder(){
  if(!playerAutoMoveUniversal||playerCivId<0||_playerFlowMode!=='friendly')return;
  const all=civs[playerCivId]?.foxes||[];
  for(let i=0;i<all.length;i++){
    const d=all[i];if(!d||!d.autoMove||d.hp<=0)continue;
    // War movement can be represented either as an ordinary path, a scheduled safe hop,
    // or a parked blocked hop. Clear every representation before assigning the peace guard.
    if(d._safeMoveDue>0)_cancelSafeMove(d,false,false);
    if(d._blockedParked)_unparkBlockedDiv(d,false);
    d.path=[];d.moveProgress=0;d._planTargetPid=-1;d._autoWarRole='';d._vGroupId=null;
    _syncDivActivity(d);
  }
}
function tickPlayerArmyPlans(force=false){
  if(playerCivId<0||!gameReady)return;
  // Universal AUTO MOVE is event-driven: this function is O(1) on ordinary sim ticks.
  // Rebuild at territory/war topology events, then each arriving fox reads its next step
  // directly from the shared flow field. No 20,000-army scan per simulation tick.
  if(playerAutoMoveUniversal){
    if(_playerFlowCivId!==playerCivId||_playerFlowLegalStamp!==_routeLegalStamp(playerCivId))_markPlayerArmyPlanDirty(true);
    const rebuilt=_playerArmyPlanDirty||_playerFlowDirty;_playerArmyPlanDirty=false;
    const previousMode=_playerFlowMode;
    if(rebuilt)_buildPlayerAutoFlow();
    // The moment the final player war ends, stale attack paths must not keep marching.
    // Re-route every automated unit from its CURRENT province to the own-country border.
    if(rebuilt&&_playerFlowMode==='friendly'&&previousMode!=='friendly'){
      _retaskPlayerAutoToPeaceBorder();_playerFlowDirty=true;_buildPlayerAutoFlow();
    }
    if(force||rebuilt||_playerAutoFullKick){
      _playerAutoFullKick=false;
      const all=civs[playerCivId]?.foxes||[];
      for(let i=0;i<all.length;i++){
        const d=all[i];if(!d.autoMove||d.hp<=0)continue;
        if(_playerAutoCoverage&&d._autoCoverageState!==_playerAutoCoverage)_retargetAutoCoverage(d,_playerAutoCoverage);
        else if(!d.path.length)_playerAutoKick(d);
      }
    }
  }

  // Manual BORDER orders retain the precise balanced planner. They are not polled every
  // logical tick; only explicit/topology events or a slow fallback pulse revisit them.
  if(!force&&!_playerArmyPlanDirty&&time%48!==0)return;
  _playerArmyPlanDirty=false;
  const divs=playerDivs(),groups=new Map();
  for(const d of divs){
    if(!d.borderMode)continue;
    const ids=[...(d.borderTargets||[])].sort((a,b)=>a-b),key=d.borderMode+'|'+ids.join(',');
    let g=groups.get(key);if(!g){g={mode:d.borderMode,ids,divs:[]};groups.set(key,g);}g.divs.push(d);
  }
  for(const g of groups.values())_assignBalancedPlan(g.divs,_playerBorderGuardLines(g.mode,g.ids),(d,pids)=>_playerPlanPathToAny(d.provId,pids,'friendly'),'border');
}
function _refreshUniversalAutoMovePlan(){
  if(!playerAutoMoveUniversal||playerCivId<0)return;
  for(const d of playerDivs()){d.autoMove=true;d.borderMode='';d.borderTargets=[];d._planTargetPid=-1;d._autoRepathAt=0;d._autoWarRole='';} _playerAutoFullKick=true;_markPlayerArmyPlanDirty(true);
  if(gameReady)tickPlayerArmyPlans(true);
  updateMilBar();
}
function _disableAutoMoveFor(div){
  if(!div) return;
  // AUTO MOVE is universal. Manual route-drawing can temporarily give one army
  // a path, but it must not silently create a mixed ON/OFF auto state.
  div.autoMove=!!playerAutoMoveUniversal;
  div.borderMode='';
  div.borderTargets=[];
  div._planTargetPid=-1;
  div._autoRepathAt=0;
  div._autoWarRole='';
  _markPlayerArmyPlanDirty();
}

// ── INSTANCED FOX BODY GPU RENDERER ───────────────────────────────────────────
// At very large army counts Canvas2D spends most of its frame issuing thousands of
// drawImage calls. WebGL2 renders the *same cached fox sprite canvases* from a small
// texture atlas with one instanced draw call. All selection rings, HP bars, badges,
// march markers, anchors and routes remain on the normal Canvas2D path.
const _unitGLCanvas=document.createElement('canvas');
let _unitGL=null,_unitGLProgram=null,_unitGLVAO=null,_unitGLCornerBuf=null,_unitGLInstanceBuf=null,_unitGLTex=null;
let _unitGLRectLoc=-1,_unitGLUvLoc=-1,_unitGLViewportLoc=null;
let _unitGLReady=0; // 0 unknown, 1 ready, -1 unavailable
let _unitGLInstances=new Float32Array(8*1024);
const _unitGLAtlasMap=new Map();
const _unitGLNeeded=new Map(); // sprite key -> packed cid/animation/direction descriptor
const _unitGLVariantCache=[];  // cid -> exact sprite identity + keys/UVs
function _unitGLFrameVariants(cid,tier){
  let v=_unitGLVariantCache[cid];
  if(!v)v=_unitGLVariantCache[cid]={version:-1,base:null,armor:-1,boot:-1,keys:new Array(FOX_ANIM_N*2).fill(null),uvs:new Array(FOX_ANIM_N*2).fill(null)};
  const version=_foxSpriteVersion,base=civs[cid]?._cd?.base||'',armor=tier?.armorTier||0,boot=tier?.bootTier||0;
  if(v.version!==version||v.base!==base||v.armor!==armor||v.boot!==boot){v.version=version;v.base=base;v.armor=armor;v.boot=boot;v.keys.fill(null);v.uvs.fill(null);}
  return v;
}

let _unitGLAtlasNext=0,_unitGLAtlasSize=0,_unitGLAtlasCols=0,_unitGLAtlasRows=0,_unitGLAtlasCapacity=0;
let _unitGLBodyCacheValid=false,_unitGLBodyBotHash=0,_unitGLBodyPlayerHash=0,_unitGLBodyTierPhaseHash=0,_unitGLBodyCamX=NaN,_unitGLBodyCamY=NaN,_unitGLBodyZoom=NaN,_unitGLBodyW=0,_unitGLBodyH=0,_unitGLBodyCount=-1;
function _unitGLTierPhaseHash(){let h=_foxSpriteVersion>>>0;for(const cid of _renderCivIds){const t=_foxTierRenderCache[cid];h=Math.imul(h^((cid+1)*131+(t?.armorTier||0)*17+(t?.bootTier||0)*31),16777619)>>>0;}for(let p=0;p<8;p++)h=Math.imul(h^((_BOT_PHASE_FRAMES[p]+1)*(p+3)),16777619)>>>0;return h>>>0;}
function _invalidateUnitGLBodyCache(){_unitGLBodyCacheValid=false;}
function _compileUnitGLShader(gl,type,src){const sh=gl.createShader(type);gl.shaderSource(sh,src);gl.compileShader(sh);if(!gl.getShaderParameter(sh,gl.COMPILE_STATUS)){gl.deleteShader(sh);return null;}return sh;}
function _initUnitGL(){
  if(_unitGLReady)return _unitGLReady>0;
  try{
    const gl=_unitGLCanvas.getContext('webgl2',{alpha:true,premultipliedAlpha:true,antialias:false,depth:false,stencil:false,preserveDrawingBuffer:!isMobile,desynchronized:!isMobile});
    if(!gl){_unitGLReady=-1;return false;}
    const vs=_compileUnitGLShader(gl,gl.VERTEX_SHADER,`#version 300 es\nprecision highp float;layout(location=0) in vec2 aCorner;layout(location=1) in vec4 aRect;layout(location=2) in vec4 aUV;uniform vec2 uViewport;out vec2 vUV;void main(){vec2 p=aRect.xy+aCorner*aRect.zw;vec2 clip=vec2(p.x/uViewport.x*2.0-1.0,1.0-p.y/uViewport.y*2.0);gl_Position=vec4(clip,0.0,1.0);vUV=mix(aUV.xy,aUV.zw,aCorner);}`);
    const fs=_compileUnitGLShader(gl,gl.FRAGMENT_SHADER,`#version 300 es\nprecision mediump float;in vec2 vUV;uniform sampler2D uTex;out vec4 outColor;void main(){outColor=texture(uTex,vUV);}`);
    if(!vs||!fs){_unitGLReady=-1;return false;}
    const pr=gl.createProgram();gl.attachShader(pr,vs);gl.attachShader(pr,fs);gl.linkProgram(pr);gl.deleteShader(vs);gl.deleteShader(fs);
    if(!gl.getProgramParameter(pr,gl.LINK_STATUS)){_unitGLReady=-1;return false;}
    const vao=gl.createVertexArray();gl.bindVertexArray(vao);
    const cb=gl.createBuffer();gl.bindBuffer(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER,cb);gl.bufferData(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER,new Float32Array([0,0,1,0,0,1,0,1,1,0,1,1]),gl.STATIC_DRAW);gl.enableVertexAttribArray(0);gl.vertexAttribPointer(0,2,gl.FLOAT,false,0,0);
    const ib=gl.createBuffer();gl.bindBuffer(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER,ib);gl.bufferData(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER,_unitGLInstances.byteLength,gl.DYNAMIC_DRAW);
    gl.enableVertexAttribArray(1);gl.vertexAttribPointer(1,4,gl.FLOAT,false,32,0);gl.vertexAttribDivisor(1,1);
    gl.enableVertexAttribArray(2);gl.vertexAttribPointer(2,4,gl.FLOAT,false,32,16);gl.vertexAttribDivisor(2,1);
    const maxTex=gl.getParameter(gl.MAX_TEXTURE_SIZE)||2048;_unitGLAtlasSize=Math.min(isMobile?1536:2048,maxTex);
    _unitGLAtlasCols=Math.floor(_unitGLAtlasSize/FOX_SW);_unitGLAtlasRows=Math.floor(_unitGLAtlasSize/FOX_SH);_unitGLAtlasCapacity=_unitGLAtlasCols*_unitGLAtlasRows;
    if(_unitGLAtlasCapacity<128){_unitGLReady=-1;return false;}
    const tex=gl.createTexture();gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D,tex);gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D,gl.TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER,gl.NEAREST);gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D,gl.TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER,gl.NEAREST);gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D,gl.TEXTURE_WRAP_S,gl.CLAMP_TO_EDGE);gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D,gl.TEXTURE_WRAP_T,gl.CLAMP_TO_EDGE);gl.texImage2D(gl.TEXTURE_2D,0,gl.RGBA,_unitGLAtlasSize,_unitGLAtlasSize,0,gl.RGBA,gl.UNSIGNED_BYTE,null);
    gl.pixelStorei(gl.UNPACK_PREMULTIPLY_ALPHA_WEBGL,true);gl.pixelStorei(gl.UNPACK_FLIP_Y_WEBGL,false);
    gl.enable(gl.BLEND);gl.blendFunc(gl.ONE,gl.ONE_MINUS_SRC_ALPHA);gl.disable(gl.DEPTH_TEST);gl.disable(gl.CULL_FACE);
    gl.useProgram(pr);gl.uniform1i(gl.getUniformLocation(pr,'uTex'),0);
    _unitGL=gl;_unitGLProgram=pr;_unitGLVAO=vao;_unitGLCornerBuf=cb;_unitGLInstanceBuf=ib;_unitGLTex=tex;_unitGLViewportLoc=gl.getUniformLocation(pr,'uViewport');_unitGLReady=1;
    return true;
  }catch(_){_unitGLReady=-1;return false;}
}
function _resetUnitGLAtlas(){
  _unitGLAtlasMap.clear();_unitGLAtlasNext=0;_unitGLVariantCache.length=0;_invalidateUnitGLBodyCache();
  // Do not reallocate the entire atlas texture on a logical cache reset. Every reused
  // slot is fully overwritten before its UV is published; retaining the allocation
  // avoids repeated multi-megabyte GPU allocations, especially important on iOS WebKit.
}
function _ensureUnitGLInstanceCapacity(n){
  const need=n*8;if(_unitGLInstances.length>=need)return;
  let cap=_unitGLInstances.length;while(cap<need)cap*=2;_unitGLInstances=new Float32Array(cap);
  if(_unitGLReady>0){const gl=_unitGL;gl.bindBuffer(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER,_unitGLInstanceBuf);gl.bufferData(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER,_unitGLInstances.byteLength,gl.DYNAMIC_DRAW);}
}
let _unitGLUploadCanvas=null;
function _unitGLUploadKey(key,spr){
  const gl=_unitGL,slot=_unitGLAtlasNext++;if(slot>=_unitGLAtlasCapacity)return null;
  const col=slot%_unitGLAtlasCols,row=(slot/_unitGLAtlasCols)|0,x=col*FOX_SW,y=row*FOX_SH;
  gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D,_unitGLTex);
  // Atlas UVs run top-to-bottom, like Canvas2D. Normalize bitmap uploads to
  // canvas so WebGL unpack orientation and premultiplication apply consistently.
  if(typeof ImageBitmap!=='undefined'&&spr instanceof ImageBitmap){
    if(!_unitGLUploadCanvas){_unitGLUploadCanvas=document.createElement('canvas');_unitGLUploadCanvas.width=FOX_SW;_unitGLUploadCanvas.height=FOX_SH;}
    const uploadCtx=_unitGLUploadCanvas.getContext('2d');
    uploadCtx.clearRect(0,0,FOX_SW,FOX_SH);uploadCtx.drawImage(spr,0,0);spr=_unitGLUploadCanvas;
  }
  gl.pixelStorei(gl.UNPACK_FLIP_Y_WEBGL,false);
  gl.pixelStorei(gl.UNPACK_PREMULTIPLY_ALPHA_WEBGL,true);
  try{gl.texSubImage2D(gl.TEXTURE_2D,0,x,y,gl.RGBA,gl.UNSIGNED_BYTE,spr);}catch(_){return null;}
  const uv=[x/_unitGLAtlasSize,y/_unitGLAtlasSize,(x+FOX_SW)/_unitGLAtlasSize,(y+FOX_SH)/_unitGLAtlasSize];_unitGLAtlasMap.set(key,uv);return uv;
}
function _renderFoxBodiesGL(S){
  const count=_renderDivs.length;if(count<220||!_initUnitGL())return false;
  const w=canvas.width,h=canvas.height,tierPhaseHash=isMobile?0:_unitGLTierPhaseHash();
  if(!_renderPlayerMoving&&_unitGLBodyCacheValid&&_unitGLBodyW===w&&_unitGLBodyH===h&&_unitGLBodyCount===count&&_unitGLBodyBotHash===_renderBotHash&&_unitGLBodyPlayerHash===_renderPlayerHash&&_unitGLBodyTierPhaseHash===tierPhaseHash&&_unitGLBodyCamX===camX&&_unitGLBodyCamY===camY&&_unitGLBodyZoom===zoom){ctx.drawImage(_unitGLCanvas,0,0);return true;}

  _ensureUnitGLInstanceCapacity(count);
  let k=0,warm=true;
  // Hot path: after the atlas has been primed, persistent per-civ variant UVs let us
  // animate + pack each visible unit exactly once. No discovery pass is needed.
  warmScan: for(const cid of _renderCivIds){
    const arr=_renderByCiv[cid];if(!arr)continue;const civ=civs[cid];if(!civ)continue;const tier=_foxTierRenderCache[cid]||null;
    const variants=_unitGLFrameVariants(cid,tier);
    for(const d of arr){
      const dir=d.dir||1,anim=(cid===playerCivId)?((((_simTick+(d._animSeed||0))>>2)>>>0)%FOX_ANIM_N):_BOT_PHASE_FRAMES[d._botPhaseSlot||0],vi=(anim<<1)|(dir<0?1:0),uv=variants.uvs[vi];
      d._renderVariant=vi;if(!uv){warm=false;break warmScan;}
      const sx=d._renderSx,sy=d._renderSy,x=dir===1?sx-FOX_OX*S:sx-(FOX_SW-FOX_OX)*S,y=sy-FOX_OY*S,dw=FOX_SW*S,dh=FOX_SH*S;
      _unitGLInstances[k++]=x;_unitGLInstances[k++]=y;_unitGLInstances[k++]=dw;_unitGLInstances[k++]=dh;_unitGLInstances[k++]=uv[0];_unitGLInstances[k++]=uv[1];_unitGLInstances[k++]=uv[2];_unitGLInstances[k++]=uv[3];
    }
  }

  if(!warm){
    // Cold/changed-art path: discover only the unique variants, upload anything missing,
    // then repack. This is the existing correctness path and runs only when required.
    _unitGLNeeded.clear();
    for(const cid of _renderCivIds){
      const arr=_renderByCiv[cid];if(!arr)continue;const civ=civs[cid];if(!civ)continue;const tier=_foxTierRenderCache[cid]||null;
      const variants=_unitGLFrameVariants(cid,tier);let mask=0;
      for(const d of arr){
        const dir=d.dir||1,anim=(cid===playerCivId)?((((_simTick+(d._animSeed||0))>>2)>>>0)%FOX_ANIM_N):_BOT_PHASE_FRAMES[d._botPhaseSlot||0],vi=(anim<<1)|(dir<0?1:0);
        d._renderVariant=vi;mask|=1<<vi;
      }
      for(let vi=0;vi<FOX_ANIM_N*2;vi++){
        if(!(mask&(1<<vi)))continue;
        const anim=vi>>1,dir=(vi&1)?-1:1,key=_foxSpriteKey(civ._cd,anim,dir,tier);variants.keys[vi]=key;
        if(!_unitGLNeeded.has(key))_unitGLNeeded.set(key,(cid<<6)|vi);
      }
    }
    let missing=0;for(const key of _unitGLNeeded.keys())if(!_unitGLAtlasMap.has(key))missing++;
    if(_unitGLNeeded.size>_unitGLAtlasCapacity)return false;
    if(_unitGLAtlasMap.size+missing>_unitGLAtlasCapacity)_resetUnitGLAtlas();
    for(const [key,packed] of _unitGLNeeded){
      if(_unitGLAtlasMap.has(key))continue;
      const cid=packed>>6,anim=(packed>>1)&31,dir=(packed&1)?-1:1,civ=civs[cid],tier=_foxTierRenderCache[cid]||null;if(!civ)return false;
      const spr=_getFoxSprite(civ._cd,anim,dir,tier);if(!_unitGLUploadKey(key,spr))return false;
    }
    k=0;
    for(const cid of _renderCivIds){
      const arr=_renderByCiv[cid];if(!arr)continue;const civ=civs[cid],tier=_foxTierRenderCache[cid]||null;
      const variants=_unitGLFrameVariants(cid,tier);
      for(const d of arr){
        const dir=d.dir||1,vi=d._renderVariant;
        let key=variants.keys[vi];if(key===null){key=_foxSpriteKey(civ._cd,vi>>1,dir,tier);variants.keys[vi]=key;}
        let uv=variants.uvs[vi];if(uv===null){uv=_unitGLAtlasMap.get(key);variants.uvs[vi]=uv;}if(!uv)return false;
        const sx=d._renderSx,sy=d._renderSy,x=dir===1?sx-FOX_OX*S:sx-(FOX_SW-FOX_OX)*S,y=sy-FOX_OY*S,dw=FOX_SW*S,dh=FOX_SH*S;
        _unitGLInstances[k++]=x;_unitGLInstances[k++]=y;_unitGLInstances[k++]=dw;_unitGLInstances[k++]=dh;_unitGLInstances[k++]=uv[0];_unitGLInstances[k++]=uv[1];_unitGLInstances[k++]=uv[2];_unitGLInstances[k++]=uv[3];
      }
    }
  }

  const gl=_unitGL;if(_unitGLCanvas.width!==w)_unitGLCanvas.width=w;if(_unitGLCanvas.height!==h)_unitGLCanvas.height=h;
  gl.viewport(0,0,w,h);gl.clearColor(0,0,0,0);gl.clear(gl.COLOR_BUFFER_BIT);gl.useProgram(_unitGLProgram);gl.bindVertexArray(_unitGLVAO);gl.activeTexture(gl.TEXTURE0);gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D,_unitGLTex);gl.uniform2f(_unitGLViewportLoc,w,h);gl.bindBuffer(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER,_unitGLInstanceBuf);gl.bufferSubData(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER,0,_unitGLInstances,0,count*8);gl.drawArraysInstanced(gl.TRIANGLES,0,6,count);
  _unitGLBodyW=w;_unitGLBodyH=h;_unitGLBodyCount=count;_unitGLBodyBotHash=_renderBotHash;_unitGLBodyPlayerHash=_renderPlayerHash;_unitGLBodyTierPhaseHash=tierPhaseHash;_unitGLBodyCamX=camX;_unitGLBodyCamY=camY;_unitGLBodyZoom=zoom;_unitGLBodyCacheValid=!isMobile&&!_renderPlayerMoving;
  ctx.drawImage(_unitGLCanvas,0,0);return true;
}

// ── BOT FOX BODY LAYER CACHE ─────────────────────────────────────
// Fox body sprites are the expensive part of unit rendering. Bot animation frames only
// change every 4 RAFs, so stationary-camera frames can reuse two already-composited layers
// while HP bars, route pulses, badges and combat indicators still update every frame.
const _foxPassSprites=[],_foxPassSpriteCivs=[];
let _foxSpritePassActive=false;
function _clearFoxPassSprites(){
  for(const cid of _foxPassSpriteCivs){const v=_foxPassSprites[cid];if(v){v[0].fill(null);v[1].fill(null);v.active=false;}}
  _foxPassSpriteCivs.length=0;_foxSpritePassActive=false;
}
function _foxPassSprite(cid,civ,anim,dir,tier,suppressPromotion){
  if(!_foxSpritePassActive||(_renderByCiv[cid]?.length||0)<8)return _getFoxSprite(civ._cd,anim,dir,tier,suppressPromotion);
  let v=_foxPassSprites[cid];if(!v)v=_foxPassSprites[cid]=[new Array(FOX_ANIM_N*2).fill(null),new Array(FOX_ANIM_N*2).fill(null)];
  if(!v.active){v.active=true;_foxPassSpriteCivs.push(cid);}
  const a=v[suppressPromotion?1:0],vi=anim*2+(dir<0?1:0);
  let spr=a[vi];if(!spr)spr=a[vi]=_getFoxSprite(civ._cd,anim,dir,tier,suppressPromotion);
  return spr;
}
function _drawFoxSpriteOnly(targetCtx,d,S,tierCache){
  const civ=civs[d.civId];if(!civ)return;
  const dir=d.dir||1;
  const animIdx=(d.civId===playerCivId)?((((_simTick+(d._animSeed||0))>>2)>>>0)%FOX_ANIM_N):_BOT_PHASE_FRAMES[d._botPhaseSlot||0];
  const tier=tierCache?(tierCache[d.civId]||null):null;
  const suppressPromotion=(targetCtx===_botLayerLowCtx||targetCtx===_botLayerHighCtx);
  const spr=_foxPassSprite(d.civId,civ,animIdx,dir,tier,suppressPromotion);
  const sx=d._renderSx,sy=d._renderSy,dw=FOX_SW*S,dh=FOX_SH*S,oy=FOX_OY*S;
  targetCtx.drawImage(spr,dir===1?sx-FOX_OX*S:sx-(FOX_SW-FOX_OX)*S,sy-oy,dw,dh);
}
// Async Canvas2D army compositor. Large bot-body rebuilds are pure graphics work, so on
// browsers with OffscreenCanvas+Worker they run off the UI thread. The main thread keeps the
// previous exact layer and camera-transforms it until the newest worker frame arrives.
let _botLayerWorker=null,_botLayerWorkerReady=0,_botLayerWorkerBusy=false,_botLayerWorkerToken=0,_botLayerWorkerLatest=0;
const _botLayerWorkerSpriteIds=new Map(),_botLayerWorkerSpritePromises=new Map(),_botLayerWorkerVariantCache=[];let _botLayerWorkerNextSpriteId=1,_botLayerWorkerSpriteEpoch=1;
let _botLayerWorkerLowBitmap=null,_botLayerWorkerHighBitmap=null,_botLayerWorkerPrimePending=false,_botLayerWorkerFreeBuf=null;
const _botLayerPrimeQueue=[],_botLayerPrimeKeys=new Set();let _botLayerPrimePumpPending=false;
function _clearBotLayerWorkerBitmaps(){
  _botLayerWorkerLowBitmap?.close?.();_botLayerWorkerHighBitmap?.close?.();_botLayerWorkerLowBitmap=null;_botLayerWorkerHighBitmap=null;
}
function _resetBotLayerWorkerSpriteCache(){
  _botLayerWorkerSpriteEpoch=(_botLayerWorkerSpriteEpoch+1)>>>0||1;
  _clearBotLayerWorkerBitmaps();
  if(_botLayerWorkerReady>0&&_botLayerWorker)try{_botLayerWorker.postMessage({type:'reset'});}catch(_){}
  _botLayerWorkerSpriteIds.clear();_botLayerWorkerSpritePromises.clear();_botLayerWorkerVariantCache.length=0;_botLayerWorkerNextSpriteId=1;
  _botLayerPrimeQueue.length=0;_botLayerPrimeKeys.clear();_botLayerWorkerFreeBuf=null;_botLayerWorkerBusy=false;_botLayerWorkerPrimePending=false;_botLayerPrimePumpPending=false;
}
function _initBotLayerWorker(){
  if(_botLayerWorkerReady)return _botLayerWorkerReady>0;
  if(typeof Worker==='undefined'||typeof OffscreenCanvas==='undefined'||typeof createImageBitmap!=='function'){_botLayerWorkerReady=-1;return false;}
  try{
    const code=`let sprites=new Map(),low=null,high=null,lc=null,hc=null,W=0,H=0;\n`+
`onmessage=e=>{const d=e.data;if(d.type==='reset'){for(const sp of sprites.values())sp.close?.();sprites.clear();if(low){low.width=1;low.height=1;}if(high){high.width=1;high.height=1;}low=null;high=null;lc=null;hc=null;W=H=0;postMessage({type:'resetDone'});return;}if(d.type==='sprite'){sprites.set(d.id,d.bmp);return;}if(d.type!=='render')return;try{if(!low||W!==d.w||H!==d.h){W=d.w;H=d.h;low=new OffscreenCanvas(W,H);high=new OffscreenCanvas(W,H);lc=low.getContext('2d',{alpha:true,desynchronized:true});hc=high.getContext('2d',{alpha:true,desynchronized:true});}else{lc.clearRect(0,0,W,H);hc.clearRect(0,0,W,H);}lc.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;hc.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;const a=new Float32Array(d.buf);for(let i=0;i<a.length;i+=6){const sp=sprites.get(a[i]|0);if(!sp)continue;(a[i+5]<.5?lc:hc).drawImage(sp,a[i+1],a[i+2],a[i+3],a[i+4]);}const lb=low.transferToImageBitmap(),hb=high.transferToImageBitmap();postMessage({type:'frame',token:d.token,meta:d.meta,low:lb,high:hb,buf:d.buf},[lb,hb,d.buf]);}catch(err){postMessage({type:'error',message:String(err),token:d.token});}};`;
    const url=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([code],{type:'text/javascript'})),w=new Worker(url);URL.revokeObjectURL(url);_botLayerWorker=w;_botLayerWorkerReady=1;
    w.onmessage=e=>{const d=e.data;if(d.type==='error'){_botLayerWorkerBusy=false;return;}if(d.type!=='frame')return;_botLayerWorkerBusy=false;if(d.buf&&(!_botLayerWorkerFreeBuf||d.buf.byteLength>_botLayerWorkerFreeBuf.byteLength))_botLayerWorkerFreeBuf=d.buf;if(d.token<_botLayerWorkerLatest){d.low?.close?.();d.high?.close?.();return;}const m=d.meta||{};_botLayerWorkerLowBitmap?.close?.();_botLayerWorkerHighBitmap?.close?.();_botLayerWorkerLowBitmap=d.low;_botLayerWorkerHighBitmap=d.high;_botLayerW=m.w|0;_botLayerH=m.h|0;_botLayerCamX=m.camX;_botLayerCamY=m.camY;_botLayerZoom=m.zoom;_botLayerPhase=m.phase;_botLayerHash=m.sig;_botLayerPlayer=m.player;};
    w.onerror=()=>{_botLayerWorkerReady=-1;_botLayerWorkerBusy=false;_clearBotLayerWorkerBitmaps();try{w.terminate();}catch(_){};_botLayerWorker=null;};return true;
  }catch(_){_botLayerWorkerReady=-1;return false;}
}
function _botLayerWorkerSprite(key,spr){
  let id=_botLayerWorkerSpriteIds.get(key);if(id)return Promise.resolve(id);
  const existing=_botLayerWorkerSpritePromises.get(key);if(existing)return existing;
  id=_botLayerWorkerNextSpriteId++;_botLayerWorkerSpriteIds.set(key,id);
  const epoch=_botLayerWorkerSpriteEpoch;
  const pr=createImageBitmap(spr).then(bmp=>{
    // A reset can happen while createImageBitmap is in flight. Never let stale GPU
    // resources cross that reset boundary and silently repopulate the worker cache.
    if(epoch!==_botLayerWorkerSpriteEpoch){bmp.close?.();return 0;}
    if(_botLayerWorkerReady>0&&_botLayerWorker){_botLayerWorker.postMessage({type:'sprite',id,bmp},[bmp]);return id;}
    bmp.close?.();return 0;
  }).catch(()=>{if(epoch===_botLayerWorkerSpriteEpoch)_botLayerWorkerSpriteIds.delete(key);return 0;}).finally(()=>{if(_botLayerWorkerSpritePromises.get(key)===pr)_botLayerWorkerSpritePromises.delete(key);});
  _botLayerWorkerSpritePromises.set(key,pr);return pr;
}
function _botLayerWorkerVariantId(cid,civ,tier,anim,dir,pending,allowCreate=true){
  const sig=`${_foxSpriteVersion}|${tier?.armorTier||0}|${tier?.bootTier||0}`;
  let vc=_botLayerWorkerVariantCache[cid];if(!vc||vc.sig!==sig){vc={sig,ids:new Int32Array(FOX_ANIM_N*2)};_botLayerWorkerVariantCache[cid]=vc;}
  const vi=anim*2+(dir<0?1:0);let id=vc.ids[vi];if(id)return id;
  const key=_foxSpriteKey(civ._cd,anim,dir,tier);id=_botLayerWorkerSpriteIds.get(key)||0;
  if(id){vc.ids[vi]=id;return id;}
  if(!allowCreate)return 0;
  // Worker-only variants deliberately remain canvas-backed on the main thread: converting
  // them to ImageBitmap here and then cloning again for the worker doubles GPU work.
  const spr=_getFoxSprite(civ._cd,anim,dir,tier,true),pr=_botLayerWorkerSprite(key,spr);id=_botLayerWorkerSpriteIds.get(key)||0;vc.ids[vi]=id;
  pending?.push(pr.then(v=>{vc.ids[vi]=v||0;return v;}));return id;
}
function _queueBotLayerVariantPrime(cid,civ,tier,anim,dir){
  if(!civ)return;const sig=`${_foxSpriteVersion}|${tier?.armorTier||0}|${tier?.bootTier||0}`,vi=anim*2+(dir<0?1:0),pk=`${cid}|${sig}|${vi}`;
  const vc=_botLayerWorkerVariantCache[cid];if(vc?.sig===sig&&vc.ids[vi])return;if(_botLayerPrimeKeys.has(pk))return;
  _botLayerPrimeKeys.add(pk);_botLayerPrimeQueue.push({pk,cid,civ,tier,anim,dir});_scheduleBotLayerPrimePump();
}
function _scheduleBotLayerPrimePump(){
  if(_botLayerPrimePumpPending||!_botLayerPrimeQueue.length||_botLayerWorkerReady<0)return;_botLayerPrimePumpPending=true;
  const run=(deadline)=>{_botLayerPrimePumpPending=false;if(!_initBotLayerWorker())return;const t0=performance.now();let made=0;
    // Tiny slices keep sprite rasterization below an interaction-frame budget. requestIdleCallback
    // gets preference, but timeout fallback guarantees forward progress on browsers that starve idle.
    while(_botLayerPrimeQueue.length&&made<8&&(performance.now()-t0<3.0)&&(!deadline||deadline.timeRemaining()>0.6)){
      const q=_botLayerPrimeQueue.shift();_botLayerPrimeKeys.delete(q.pk);const pending=[];_botLayerWorkerVariantId(q.cid,q.civ,q.tier,q.anim,q.dir,pending,true);if(pending.length)Promise.allSettled(pending);made++;
    }
    if(_botLayerPrimeQueue.length)_scheduleBotLayerPrimePump();
  };
  if(typeof requestIdleCallback==='function')requestIdleCallback(run,{timeout:32});else setTimeout(()=>run(null),8);
}
function _primeBotLayerWorkerVisibleSprites(){
  if(_botLayerWorkerReady<0)return;
  for(const cid of _renderCivIds){if(cid===playerCivId)continue;const arr=_renderByCiv[cid];if(!arr?.length)continue;const civ=civs[cid];if(!civ)continue;const tier=_foxTierRenderCache[cid]||null;let mask=0;
    for(const d of arr){const dir=d.dir||1,anim=_BOT_PHASE_FRAMES[d._botPhaseSlot||0],vi=anim*2+(dir<0?1:0);if(vi<31){const bit=1<<vi;if(mask&bit)continue;mask|=bit;}_queueBotLayerVariantPrime(cid,civ,tier,anim,dir);}
  }
}
function _scheduleBotLayerWorkerPrime(){
  if(_botLayerWorkerPrimePending||_botLayerWorkerReady<0)return;_botLayerWorkerPrimePending=true;
  const run=()=>{_botLayerWorkerPrimePending=false;_primeBotLayerWorkerVisibleSprites();};
  // This pass only discovers unique visible variants; actual rasterization is separately sliced.
  setTimeout(run,0);
}
function _queueBotLayerWorkerRender(S,meta){
  if(!_initBotLayerWorker()||_botLayerWorkerBusy)return false;
  let count=0,missing=false;
  // First pass is variant-only. It avoids string/Map/queue work for every individual unit
  // when a newly visible country has not finished its worker sprite warm-up yet.
  for(const cid of _renderCivIds){
    if(cid===playerCivId)continue;const arr=_renderByCiv[cid];if(!arr?.length)continue;count+=arr.length;const civ=civs[cid];if(!civ)continue;const tier=_foxTierRenderCache[cid]||null;
    const sig=`${_foxSpriteVersion}|${tier?.armorTier||0}|${tier?.bootTier||0}`;let vc=_botLayerWorkerVariantCache[cid];if(!vc||vc.sig!==sig){vc={sig,ids:new Int32Array(FOX_ANIM_N*2)};_botLayerWorkerVariantCache[cid]=vc;}
    let needed=0;for(const d of arr){const anim=_BOT_PHASE_FRAMES[d._botPhaseSlot||0],vi=anim*2+((d.dir||1)<0?1:0);needed|=(1<<vi);}
    for(let vi=0;vi<FOX_ANIM_N*2;vi++)if((needed&(1<<vi))&&!vc.ids[vi]){missing=true;_queueBotLayerVariantPrime(cid,civ,tier,(vi/2)|0,(vi&1)?-1:1);}
  }
  if(!count||missing)return false;
  // Reuse a returned transferable record buffer when possible; otherwise allocate exactly once.
  const neededFloats=count*6;let buf=_botLayerWorkerFreeBuf;if(!buf||buf.byteLength<neededFloats*4)buf=new ArrayBuffer(neededFloats*4);_botLayerWorkerFreeBuf=null;
  const a=new Float32Array(buf,0,neededFloats);let k=0;
  for(const cid of _renderCivIds){
    if(cid===playerCivId)continue;const arr=_renderByCiv[cid];if(!arr)continue;const civ=civs[cid];if(!civ)continue;const tier=_foxTierRenderCache[cid]||null,vc=_botLayerWorkerVariantCache[cid],layer=(playerCivId>=0&&cid>playerCivId)?1:0;
    for(const d of arr){const dir=d.dir||1,anim=_BOT_PHASE_FRAMES[d._botPhaseSlot||0],id=vc.ids[anim*2+(dir<0?1:0)],sx=d._renderSx,sy=d._renderSy;a[k++]=id;a[k++]=dir===1?sx-FOX_OX*S:sx-(FOX_SW-FOX_OX)*S;a[k++]=sy-FOX_OY*S;a[k++]=FOX_SW*S;a[k++]=FOX_SH*S;a[k++]=layer;}
  }
  const token=++_botLayerWorkerToken;_botLayerWorkerLatest=token;_botLayerWorkerBusy=true;
  _botLayerWorker.postMessage({type:'render',token,w:meta.w,h:meta.h,meta,buf:a.buffer},[a.buffer]);return true;
}
function _drawCachedBotLayer(layer){
  const source=layer===_botLayerLow?(_botLayerWorkerLowBitmap||_botLayerLow):layer===_botLayerHigh?(_botLayerWorkerHighBitmap||_botLayerHigh):layer;
  if(!source?.width||!Number.isFinite(_botLayerCamX)||!Number.isFinite(_botLayerZoom)||_botLayerZoom<=0)return;
  if(_botLayerCamX===camX&&_botLayerCamY===camY&&_botLayerZoom===zoom){ctx.drawImage(source,0,0);return;}
  const scale=zoom/_botLayerZoom,dx=(_botLayerCamX-camX)*TS*zoom,dy=(_botLayerCamY-camY)*TS*zoom;
  ctx.drawImage(source,0,0,source.width,source.height,dx,dy,source.width*scale,source.height*scale);
}

const _botLayerLow=document.createElement('canvas'),_botLayerHigh=document.createElement('canvas');
const _botLayerLowCtx=_botLayerLow.getContext('2d',{alpha:true,willReadFrequently:false});
const _botLayerHighCtx=_botLayerHigh.getContext('2d',{alpha:true,willReadFrequently:false});
let _botLayerW=0,_botLayerH=0,_botLayerCamX=NaN,_botLayerCamY=NaN,_botLayerZoom=NaN;
let _botLayerPhase=-1,_botLayerHash=-1,_botLayerPlayer=-999;
function _hashVisibleBots(){
  let h=_renderBotHash;
  // Unit identity/position is already folded by the viewport collector. Only tier/civ
  // state remains here, so the cache validation cost scales with visible countries.
  for(const cid of _renderCivIds){
    if(cid===playerCivId)continue;
    const tier=_foxTierRenderCache[cid]||{armorTier:0,bootTier:0};
    h=Math.imul(h^(cid+1),16777619)>>>0;
    h=Math.imul(h^((tier.armorTier||0)*17+(tier.bootTier||0)),16777619)>>>0;
  }
  return h>>>0;
}
function _prepareBotSpriteLayers(S){
  let botCount=0;for(const cid of _renderCivIds)if(cid!==playerCivId)botCount+=(_renderByCiv[cid]?.length||0);
  // Full-screen alpha layers touch every viewport pixel twice per frame. Cached direct sprite
  // blits touch only the fox rectangles, so choose the layer path only when the visible army is
  // genuinely huge. Threshold scales with viewport area and current sprite footprint.
  const w=canvas.width,h=canvas.height;
  const spritePx=Math.max(1,FOX_SW*FOX_SH*S*S);
  const pixelBreakEven=Math.ceil((w*h*2.2)/(spritePx*0.75));
  const layerThreshold=Math.max(900,Math.min(5000,pixelBreakEven));
  if(botCount<layerThreshold)return false;
  const phase=(_botAnimTick>>2),sig=_hashVisibleBots();
  const dirty=w!==_botLayerW||h!==_botLayerH||camX!==_botLayerCamX||camY!==_botLayerCamY||zoom!==_botLayerZoom||phase!==_botLayerPhase||sig!==_botLayerHash||playerCivId!==_botLayerPlayer;
  if(!dirty){_scheduleBotLayerWorkerPrime();return true;}
  // Once an exact layer exists, never rebuild thousands of bodies synchronously during
  // camera/animation churn. Queue the newest exact frame off-thread and reuse the previous
  // layer transformed to the live camera meanwhile.
  if(_botLayerW===w&&_botLayerH===h&&Number.isFinite(_botLayerCamX)&&_initBotLayerWorker()){
    _queueBotLayerWorkerRender(S,{w,h,camX,camY,zoom,phase,sig,player:playerCivId});
    return true;
  }
  _clearBotLayerWorkerBitmaps();
  if(w!==_botLayerW||h!==_botLayerH){_botLayerLow.width=w;_botLayerLow.height=h;_botLayerHigh.width=w;_botLayerHigh.height=h;_botLayerW=w;_botLayerH=h;}
  else{_botLayerLowCtx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);_botLayerHighCtx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);}
  _botLayerLowCtx.imageSmoothingEnabled=ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled;
  _botLayerHighCtx.imageSmoothingEnabled=ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled;
  for(const cid of _renderCivIds){
    if(cid===playerCivId)continue;
    const arr=_renderByCiv[cid];if(!arr)continue;
    const tc=(playerCivId>=0&&cid>playerCivId)?_botLayerHighCtx:_botLayerLowCtx;
    for(const d of arr)_drawFoxSpriteOnly(tc,d,S,_foxTierRenderCache);
  }
  _botLayerCamX=camX;_botLayerCamY=camY;_botLayerZoom=zoom;_botLayerPhase=phase;_botLayerHash=sig;_botLayerPlayer=playerCivId;
  return true;
}
function _invalidateBotSpriteLayers(){_botLayerHash=-1;_botLayerPhase=-1;}

const _FOX_SEED_CACHE_N=512;
const _foxBounceStamp=new Float64Array(_FOX_SEED_CACHE_N),_foxBounceValue=new Float64Array(_FOX_SEED_CACHE_N);_foxBounceStamp.fill(NaN);
const _foxBlockPulseStamp=new Float64Array(_FOX_SEED_CACHE_N),_foxBlockPulseValue=new Float64Array(_FOX_SEED_CACHE_N);_foxBlockPulseStamp.fill(NaN);
function _playerFoxBounce(seed){seed=Number.isInteger(seed)?seed:0;if(seed>=0&&seed<_FOX_SEED_CACHE_N){if(_foxBounceStamp[seed]!==_simTick){_foxBounceStamp[seed]=_simTick;_foxBounceValue[seed]=Math.sin((_simTick+seed)*0.25)*1.5;}return _foxBounceValue[seed];}return Math.sin((_simTick+seed)*0.25)*1.5;}
function _playerFoxBlockPulse(seed){seed=Number.isInteger(seed)?seed:0;if(seed>=0&&seed<_FOX_SEED_CACHE_N){if(_foxBlockPulseStamp[seed]!==_simTick){_foxBlockPulseStamp[seed]=_simTick;_foxBlockPulseValue[seed]=.7+.3*Math.sin((_simTick+seed)*.15);}return _foxBlockPulseValue[seed];}return .7+.3*Math.sin((_simTick+seed)*.15);}

let _foxOrderSeq=1;
// ── SPARSE DIVISION SIMULATION ────────────────────────────────────
// Most armies are stationary and out of combat. Keep only divisions that actually need
// per-simulation-tick work in a per-civ Set instead of scanning every army object forever.function _ensureActiveDivSet(civ){
  if(!civ)return null;
  if(!civ._activeDivs){
    const a=civ._activeDivs=[];civ._activeDivCount=0;civ._activeTombs=0;
    for(const d of civ.foxes||[]){d._activePos=-1;if((d.path?.length||((d.civId===playerCivId?d.autoMove:civ._botMassFlowActive)&&d.combatCooldown>0))&&d._safeMoveDue<=0&&!d._blockedParked){d._simActive=true;d._activePos=a.length;a.push(d);civ._activeDivCount++;}else d._simActive=false;}
  }
  return civ._activeDivs;
}
function _syncDivActivity(div){
  const civ=civs[div?.civId];if(!civ||!div)return;const a=_ensureActiveDivSet(civ),moving=!!(div.path&&div.path.length);
  if((moving||((div.civId===playerCivId?div.autoMove:civ._botMassFlowActive)&&div.combatCooldown>0))&&div._safeMoveDue<=0&&!div._blockedParked){if(!div._simActive){div._simActive=true;div._activePos=a.length;a.push(div);civ._activeDivCount=(civ._activeDivCount||0)+1;}}
  else if(div._simActive){div._simActive=false;civ._activeDivCount=Math.max(0,(civ._activeDivCount||0)-1);const pos=div._activePos;if(pos>=0&&a[pos]===div){a[pos]=null;civ._activeTombs=(civ._activeTombs||0)+1;}div._activePos=-1;}
  if(civ.id!==playerCivId&&div._simWasMoving&&!moving){civ._botGroupDirty=true;civ._botTacticalDirty=true;if(div._botPlanRole==='front'||div._botPlanRole==='attack')civ._armyPlanDirty=true;if(provinces[div.provId]?.isSea)civ._botRescueDirty=true;}div._simWasMoving=moving;
}
function _removeDivActivity(div){
  _removeRenderMover(div);
  if(div?._autoCoverageState){if(div.civId===playerCivId)_markPlayerArmyPlanDirty(true);else if(civs[div.civId])civs[div.civId]._armyPlanDirty=true;}
  _releaseAutoCoverage(div);
  const civ=civs[div?.civId];if(!civ||!div)return;if(div._blockedParked)_unparkBlockedDiv(div,false);if(div._safeMoveDue>0)_cancelSafeMove(div,false,false);const a=civ._activeDivs;
  if(div._simActive){div._simActive=false;civ._activeDivCount=Math.max(0,(civ._activeDivCount||0)-1);const pos=div._activePos;if(a&&pos>=0&&a[pos]===div){a[pos]=null;civ._activeTombs=(civ._activeTombs||0)+1;}div._activePos=-1;}
}
function _tickActiveDivisions(civ,a=null){
  a=a||_ensureActiveDivSet(civ);if(!a||(civ._activeDivCount||0)===0)return;
  for(let i=0,n=a.length;i<n;i++){
    const d=a[i];if(!d||!d._simActive||d._activePos!==i)continue;
    d.update();
    // Both scheduled arrivals and ordinary/combat arrivals must hand off to the
    // same next-hop logic. Only moving/cooling units enter this sparse loop.
    if(!d.path.length&&d.hp>0){
      if(d.civId===playerCivId&&d.autoMove)_playerAutoKick(d);
      else if(civ._botMassFlowActive&&(d._botPlanRole==='attack'||d._botPlanRole==='front'||d._botPlanRole==='border'))_botMassKick(d,civ);
    }
    if(d.path.length&&!d._blockedParked&&d._safeMoveDue<=0)_tryScheduleSafeMove(d);
    const moving=!!d.path.length,waiting=(d.civId===playerCivId?d.autoMove:civ._botMassFlowActive)&&d.combatCooldown>0;
    if(!moving&&!waiting&&d._simActive){d._simActive=false;civ._activeDivCount--;a[i]=null;d._activePos=-1;civ._activeTombs=(civ._activeTombs||0)+1;if(civ.id!==playerCivId&&d._simWasMoving){civ._botGroupDirty=true;civ._botTacticalDirty=true;if(d._botPlanRole==='front'||d._botPlanRole==='attack')civ._armyPlanDirty=true;if(provinces[d.provId]?.isSea)civ._botRescueDirty=true;}}
    d._simWasMoving=moving;
  }
  if((civ._activeTombs||0)>32&&(civ._activeTombs*3>a.length)){let w=0;for(let r=0;r<a.length;r++){const d=a[r];if(d&&d._simActive){a[w]=d;d._activePos=w++;}}a.length=w;civ._activeTombs=0;}
}

// Event-driven safe movement ---------------------------------------------------
// Bot divisions traversing their OWN, uncontested land have no state that can
// change their per-tick result until arrival. Schedule that arrival once and
// expose moveProgress lazily; ownership/foreign-occupancy changes invalidate the
// schedule immediately and return the division to the exact full update path.
const _safeIncomingByProv=new Map();
function _safeIncomingAdd(div,pid){let set=_safeIncomingByProv.get(pid);if(!set){set=new Set();_safeIncomingByProv.set(pid,set);}set.add(div);}
function _safeIncomingRemove(div,pid=div?._safeMoveDest){if(pid==null||pid<0)return;const set=_safeIncomingByProv.get(pid);if(!set)return;set.delete(div);if(!set.size)_safeIncomingByProv.delete(pid);}
function _safeMoveCurrentProgress(div){return div&&div._safeMoveDue>0?Math.min(1,div._safeMoveBase+Math.max(0,_simTick-div._safeMoveStartTick)*div._safeMoveSpeed):(div?div._moveProgress:0);}
