function _deactivateForSafeMove(div){const civ=civs[div.civId],a=civ?._activeDivs;if(div._simActive&&civ){div._simActive=false;civ._activeDivCount=Math.max(0,(civ._activeDivCount||0)-1);const pos=div._activePos;if(a&&pos>=0&&a[pos]===div){a[pos]=null;civ._activeTombs=(civ._activeTombs||0)+1;}div._activePos=-1;}}
function _activateAfterSafeMove(div){const civ=civs[div.civId];if(!civ||!div.path.length)return;const a=_ensureActiveDivSet(civ);if(!div._simActive){div._simActive=true;div._activePos=a.length;a.push(div);civ._activeDivCount=(civ._activeDivCount||0)+1;}}
function _cancelSafeMove(div,commit=true,reactivate=true){
  if(!div||div._safeMoveDue<=0)return false;
  if(commit)div._moveProgress=_safeMoveCurrentProgress(div);
  _safeIncomingRemove(div,div._safeMoveDest);div._safeMoveDue=0;div._safeMoveDest=-1;div._safeMoveSpeed=0;div._safeMoveStartTick=0;div._safeMoveBase=0;
  if(reactivate&&div.path.length)_activateAfterSafeMove(div);return true;
}
function _safeMoveDestinationIsClear(div,pid){
  if(!div||pid<0||!provinces[pid])return false;
  const p=provinces[pid],owner=provOwner[pid];
  if(!p.isSea&&owner>=0&&owner!==div.civId&&!areAtWar(div.civId,owner)&&!areAllied(div.civId,owner))return false;
  if(_provDivIndexDirty)_rebuildProvDivIndex();const byCiv=_provDivIndex.get(pid);
  if(byCiv)for(const [cid,arr] of byCiv)if(cid!==div.civId&&arr.length&&areAtWar(div.civId,cid))return false;
  return true;
}
function _safeDueHeapPush(civ,due){
  const h=civ._safeDueHeap||(civ._safeDueHeap=[]);let i=h.length;h.push(due);
  while(i>0){const p=(i-1)>>1;if(h[p]<=due)break;h[i]=h[p];i=p;}h[i]=due;
}
function _safeDueHeapPop(civ){
  const h=civ._safeDueHeap;if(!h?.length)return 0;const root=h[0],last=h.pop();if(h.length){let i=0;while(true){let l=i*2+1;if(l>=h.length)break;let r=l+1,c=(r<h.length&&h[r]<h[l])?r:l;if(h[c]>=last)break;h[i]=h[c];i=c;}h[i]=last;}return root;
}
function _safeBucketAdd(civ,div){
  if(!civ||!div||div._safeMoveDue<=0)return;const due=div._safeMoveDue|0;
  const buckets=civ._safeMoveBuckets||(civ._safeMoveBuckets=new Map());let a=buckets.get(due);
  if(!a){a=[];buckets.set(due,a);_safeDueHeapPush(civ,due);}a.push(div);
  const h=civ._safeDueHeap;civ._safeMoveNextDue=h?.length?h[0]:0;
}
function _clearSafeSchedule(civ){
  if(!civ)return;const buckets=civ._safeMoveBuckets;if(buckets){for(const a of buckets.values())for(const d of a)if(d&&d._safeMoveDue>0)_cancelSafeMove(d,false,false);buckets.clear();}
  if(civ._safeDueHeap)civ._safeDueHeap.length=0;civ._safeMoveNextDue=0;civ._processingSafeMoves=false;
}

function _tryScheduleSafeMove(div){
  if(!div||div.hp<=0||!div.path.length||div.combatCooldown>0||div._safeMoveDue>0||div._blockedParked)return false;
  const pid=div.path[0];if(!_safeMoveDestinationIsClear(div,pid))return false;_setDivisionFacingBetween(div,div.provId,pid);
  const p=provinces[pid],owner=provOwner[pid],isSea=!!p?.isSea;
  const cc=civs[div.civId];
  const movBonus=cc?._moveMul??(div.civId===playerCivId?(1+(techBonus.movMul||0)):(1+(cc?.techBonus?.movMul||0)));
  const invading=!isSea&&owner>=0&&owner!==div.civId&&areAtWar(div.civId,owner);
  const warMoveMul=invading?_warMoveModifier(div.civId,owner):1;
  const speed=(invading?0.006:(isSea?0.018/5:0.018))*movBonus*warMoveMul;
  const base=Math.max(0,Math.min(0.999999,div._moveProgress||0));if(!(speed>0))return false;
  const ticks=Math.max(1,Math.ceil((1-base)/speed-1e-12));
  div._safeMoveBase=base;div._safeMoveStartTick=_simTick;div._safeMoveSpeed=speed;div._safeMoveDue=_simTick+ticks;div._safeMoveDest=pid;_safeIncomingAdd(div,pid);_deactivateForSafeMove(div);
  const civ=civs[div.civId];if(civ)_safeBucketAdd(civ,div);
  return true;
}
function _invalidateSafeIncoming(pid,enteringCiv=-1){const set=_safeIncomingByProv.get(pid);if(!set?.size)return;for(const div of [...set])if(enteringCiv<0||areAtWar(div.civId,enteringCiv))_cancelSafeMove(div,true,true);}
function _processSafeMoves(civ){
  const heap=civ?._safeDueHeap,buckets=civ?._safeMoveBuckets;
  if(!civ||!heap?.length||!buckets?.size||heap[0]>_simTick)return;
  civ._processingSafeMoves=true;
  while(heap.length&&heap[0]<=_simTick){
    const due=_safeDueHeapPop(civ),a=buckets.get(due);buckets.delete(due);if(!a)continue;
    for(let i=0;i<a.length;i++){
      const d=a[i];if(!d||d._safeMoveDue!==due)continue;
      const dest=d._safeMoveDest;
      if(d.hp<=0||d.combatCooldown>0||!d.path.length||d.path[0]!==dest||!_safeMoveDestinationIsClear(d,dest)){_cancelSafeMove(d,true,true);continue;}
      const wasSea=!!provinces[dest]?.isSea,ownerBefore=provOwner[dest];
      _safeIncomingRemove(d,dest);d._safeMoveDue=0;d._safeMoveDest=-1;d._safeMoveSpeed=0;d._moveProgress=0;d._safeMoveStartTick=0;d._safeMoveBase=0;
      d.path.shift();_syncRenderMover(d);d._vGroupId=null;const oldPid=d.provId;d.provId=dest;d._snapToProvince(dest);_indexMoveDiv(d,oldPid,dest);AudioEngine.divisionTransition(oldPid,dest,d.civId);if(d.civId!==playerCivId)civ._botGroupDirty=true;
      if(!wasSea&&ownerBefore<0)civClaimProvince(dest,d.civId);
      else if(!wasSea&&ownerBefore!==d.civId&&areAtWar(d.civId,ownerBefore)){
        const defenders=allDivsInProv(dest,ownerBefore);if(defenders.length===0)civClaimProvince(dest,d.civId);
      }
      if(d.civId===playerCivId&&d.autoMove&&d.path.length===0)_playerAutoKick(d);
      else if(d.civId!==playerCivId&&d.path.length===0&&civ._botMassFlowActive&&(d._botPlanRole==='attack'||d._botPlanRole==='front'||d._botPlanRole==='border'))_botMassKick(d,civ);
      else if(d.path.length){if(!_tryScheduleSafeMove(d))_activateAfterSafeMove(d);}
      
    }
  }
  civ._processingSafeMoves=false;civ._safeMoveNextDue=heap.length?heap[0]:0;
}


const _blockedIncomingByProv=new Map();
let _blockedPlayerCount=0;
function _blockedIncomingRemove(div,pid=div?._blockedDest){if(pid==null||pid<0)return;const set=_blockedIncomingByProv.get(pid);if(!set)return;set.delete(div);if(!set.size)_blockedIncomingByProv.delete(pid);}
function _blockedCurrentProgress(div){return div&&div._blockedParked?Math.min(0.45,div._blockedBase+Math.max(0,_simTick-div._blockedStartTick)*0.012):(div?div._moveProgress:0);}
function _parkBlockedDiv(div,pid,baseProgress=div?._moveProgress||0){
  if(!div||div._blockedParked||!div.path.length)return false;div._blockedParked=true;div._blockedDest=pid;div._blockedBase=Math.min(0.45,Math.max(0,baseProgress));div._blockedStartTick=_simTick;
  let set=_blockedIncomingByProv.get(pid);if(!set){set=new Set();_blockedIncomingByProv.set(pid,set);}set.add(div);if(div.civId===playerCivId)_blockedPlayerCount++;_deactivateForSafeMove(div);return true;
}
function _unparkBlockedDiv(div,reactivate=true){if(!div||!div._blockedParked)return false;div._moveProgress=_blockedCurrentProgress(div);_blockedIncomingRemove(div,div._blockedDest);if(div.civId===playerCivId&&_blockedPlayerCount>0)_blockedPlayerCount--;div._blockedParked=false;div._blockedDest=-1;div._blockedBase=0;div._blockedStartTick=0;if(reactivate&&div.path.length)_activateAfterSafeMove(div);return true;}
function _wakeBlockedIncoming(pid){const set=_blockedIncomingByProv.get(pid);if(!set?.size)return;for(const div of [...set])_unparkBlockedDiv(div,true);}
function _wakeAllBlockedDivs(){if(!_blockedIncomingByProv.size)return;for(const set of [..._blockedIncomingByProv.values()])for(const div of [...set])_unparkBlockedDiv(div,true);_blockedIncomingByProv.clear();}


// Stable fox facing -----------------------------------------------------------
// Facing persists across province arrivals. Near-vertical route segments keep the
// prior facing instead of arbitrarily resolving a zero/tiny dx as "left".
function _setDivisionFacingBetween(div,fromPid,toPid){
  if(!div)return 1;
  if(div._faceFrom===fromPid&&div._faceTo===toPid)return div.dir||1;
  div._faceFrom=fromPid;div._faceTo=toPid;
  const a=provinces[fromPid],b=provinces[toPid];
  if(!a||!b)return div.dir||1;
  const dx=b.centerTx-a.centerTx,dy=b.centerTy-a.centerTy;
  const deadX=Math.max(0.18,Math.abs(dy)*0.06);
  if(Math.abs(dx)>deadX)div.dir=dx>0?1:-1;
  else if(div.dir!==1&&div.dir!==-1)div.dir=1;
  return div.dir||1;
}
function _syncDivisionFacingToPath(div){
  if(!div||!div.path?.length)return div?.dir||1;
  return _setDivisionFacingBetween(div,div.provId,div.path[0]);
}


function _warEnemyCivInProvince(pid,civId){
  if(warPairs.size===0)return -1;
  if(_provDivIndexDirty)_rebuildProvDivIndex();
  const byCiv=_provDivIndex.get(pid);if(!byCiv||byCiv.size===0)return -1;
  for(const [cid,arr] of byCiv)if(cid!==civId&&arr.length&&areAtWar(civId,cid))return cid;
  return -1;
}

// ── SPARSE PLAYER RENDER-MOVER INDEX ──────────────────────────────
// Render-only bookkeeping. Simulation, pathfinding and ownership still use the canonical
// civ.foxes / Division.path state. This prevents a few moving armies from forcing a scan
// across tens of thousands of stationary player divisions every frame.
function _ensureRenderMoverList(civ){
  if(!civ)return [];
  if(!civ._renderMovers){
    civ._renderMovers=[];civ._renderMoverTombs=0;civ._renderMoverOrderDirty=false;
    for(const d of civ.foxes||[]){
      d._renderMoverPos=-1;
      if(d.path?.length){d._renderMoverPos=civ._renderMovers.length;civ._renderMovers.push(d);}
    }
  }
  return civ._renderMovers;
}
function _addRenderMover(div){
  const civ=civs[div?.civId];if(!civ||!div||!div.path?.length||div._renderMoverPos>=0)return;
  const a=_ensureRenderMoverList(civ);
  if(div._renderMoverPos>=0)return; // lazy initialization may already have inserted it
  div._renderMoverPos=a.length;a.push(div);civ._renderMoverOrderDirty=true;
}
function _removeRenderMover(div){
  const civ=civs[div?.civId];if(!civ||!div)return;
  const a=civ._renderMovers,pos=div._renderMoverPos;
  if(a&&pos>=0&&pos<a.length&&a[pos]===div){a[pos]=null;civ._renderMoverTombs=(civ._renderMoverTombs||0)+1;}
  div._renderMoverPos=-1;
}
function _syncRenderMover(div){if(!div)return;if(div.path?.length)_addRenderMover(div);else _removeRenderMover(div);}
function _clearCivRenderMovers(civ){
  if(!civ)return;
  const a=civ._renderMovers;if(a)for(const d of a)if(d)d._renderMoverPos=-1;
  civ._renderMovers=[];civ._renderMoverTombs=0;civ._renderMoverOrderDirty=false;
}
function _orderedRenderMovers(civ){
  const a=_ensureRenderMoverList(civ),t=civ._renderMoverTombs||0;
  if(t){
    let w=0;
    for(let r=0;r<a.length;r++){const d=a[r];if(d&&d.path?.length&&d.civId===civ.id)a[w++]=d;}
    a.length=w;civ._renderMoverTombs=0;civ._renderMoverOrderDirty=true;
  }
  if(civ._renderMoverOrderDirty){
    a.sort((x,y)=>x._foxOrder-y._foxOrder);
    for(let i=0;i<a.length;i++)a[i]._renderMoverPos=i;
    civ._renderMoverOrderDirty=false;
  }
  return a;
}

class Division{
  constructor(civId,provId){
    this.id=_divId++;
    this._foxOrder=_foxOrderSeq++; // preserves civ.foxes insertion order for sparse health processing
    this.civId=civId;
    this.provId=provId;
    this.size=1;
    this.hp=100;
    this.maxHp=100;
    this.divCount=1;   // legacy save compatibility; live grouping is visual-only
    this._path=[];
    // Player visual animation is driven by the global simulation tick plus this stable phase,
    // so stationary armies no longer need a gameplay update merely to keep animating.
    this._animSeed=Math.floor(Math.random()*400);
    this.frame=this._animSeed;
    this._botPhaseSlot = Math.floor(Math.random()*8); // 0-7 stagger slot
    this._pdiPid=-1;this._pdiCiv=-1;this._pdiPos=-1; // incremental province-index slot
    this._simActive=false;this._activePos=-1; // active-movement array membership
    this._renderMoverPos=-1; // sparse render-movement list membership
    this.dir=1;
    this._combatUntil=0;
    this._combatOpponent=-1; // current cooldown is only valid while this war pair still exists
    this._moveProgress=0;this._safeMoveDue=0;this._safeMoveDest=-1;this._safeMoveBase=0;this._safeMoveStartTick=0;this._safeMoveSpeed=0;this._blockedParked=false;this._blockedDest=-1;this._blockedBase=0;this._blockedStartTick=0;
    this._retargetIn=0;
    this.autoMove=(civId===playerCivId&&playerAutoMoveUniversal); // universal player battle-plan automation
    this.borderMode='';       // '', 'own', or 'selected'
    this.borderTargets=[];    // civ IDs when borderMode==='selected'
    this._planTargetPid=-1;   // current coordinator-assigned slot/front province
    this._autoRepathAt=0;     // legacy/backward-compatible timer
    this._botPlanRole='';     // AI only: 'border', 'front', 'attack', or 'rescue'
    this._botPlanTargetPid=-1; // AI only: current national-border/front slot
    // Visual grouping (player only) — cosmetic only, never merges actual stats
    this._vGroupId      = null;  // shared ID for co-located visual groups
    this._hiddenInGroup = false; // true = co-located non-leader, skip draw
    this._vGroupCount   = 1;     // how many in this visual group (set by _assignProvSlots)
    this._slotIdx    = 0;  // assigned by _assignProvSlots each frame
    this._provStackN = 0;  // stationary player divs in this province (badge)
    this._snapToProvince(provId);
  }
  get path(){return this._path;}
  set path(v){_invalidateProvBotSlot(this.provId);if(this._blockedParked)_unparkBlockedDiv(this,false);if(this._safeMoveDue>0)_cancelSafeMove(this,true,false);this._path=Array.isArray(v)?v:[];if(this._path.length)_syncDivisionFacingToPath(this);_syncDivActivity(this);_syncRenderMover(this);if(this._path.length)_tryScheduleSafeMove(this);}
  get moveProgress(){return this._safeMoveDue>0?_safeMoveCurrentProgress(this):(this._blockedParked?_blockedCurrentProgress(this):this._moveProgress);}
  set moveProgress(v){
    // Resetting progress after assigning a path must NEVER strand the division.
    // The event-driven path setter may already have scheduled/deactivated this unit;
    // cancel that event without committing its old progress, restore activity, then
    // reschedule from the requested progress. This keeps legacy `path=...; moveProgress=0`
    // call sites correct without reintroducing per-tick movement scans.
    const nv=Number.isFinite(v)?v:0,hasPath=!!this._path.length;
    if(this._safeMoveDue>0)_cancelSafeMove(this,false,false);
    if(this._blockedParked)_unparkBlockedDiv(this,false);
    this._moveProgress=nv;
    if(hasPath){_syncDivActivity(this);_tryScheduleSafeMove(this);}
    
  }
  get combatCooldown(){return this._combatUntil>_simTick?this._combatUntil-_simTick:0;}
  set combatCooldown(v){
    this._combatUntil=v>0?_simTick+v:0;
    // A timer must never carry a fighting division out of combat in the background.
    if(v>0&&this._safeMoveDue>0)_cancelSafeMove(this,true,false);
    _syncDivActivity(this);
  }
  _snapToProvince(pid){
    const p=provinces[pid];
    if(p){
      // Place at province center — drawDivisions assigns the real
      // stacked-grid offset each frame so hitTest stays accurate.
      this.tileX=p.centerTx+0.5;
      this.tileY=p.centerTy+0.5;
    }
  }
  get selected(){return selectedDivs.has(this.id);}
  sx(){return(this.tileX-camX)*TS*zoom;}
  sy(){return(this.tileY-camY)*TS*zoom;}
  // Screen coords of the province center this div currently occupies
  homeSx(){
    const p=provinces[this.provId];
    return p?(p.centerTx+0.5-camX)*TS*zoom:this.sx();
  }
  homeSy(){
    const p=provinces[this.provId];
    return p?(p.centerTy+0.5-camY)*TS*zoom:this.sy();
  }
  hitTest(mx,my){
    if(this._hiddenInGroup) return false;
    const sz=Math.max(52,Math.min(110,zoom*85+22));
    const S=sz/68;
    const cx=this.sx()+2*S;
    const cy=this.sy()-4*S;
    const hw=36*S, hh=22*S;
    const dx=(mx-cx)/hw, dy=(my-cy)/hh;
    return dx*dx+dy*dy<1;
  }
  hitTestSelect(mx,my){
    if(this._hiddenInGroup) return false;
    const sz=Math.max(52,Math.min(110,zoom*85+22));
    const S=sz/68;
    const cx=this.sx()+2*S;
    const cy=this.sy()-4*S;
    const hw=18*S, hh=11*S;
    const dx=(mx-cx)/hw, dy=(my-cy)/hh;
    return dx*dx+dy*dy<1;
  }

  // Is the next province in our path blocked by active combat?
  // Result is cached per render frame to avoid redundant computation.
  _nextIsBlocked(){
    if(this.path.length===0) return false;
    // Return cached result if already computed this frame
    if(this._blockedFrame === time) return this._blockedCache;
    const _cacheBlocked = (v) => { this._blockedFrame=time; this._blockedCache=v; return v; };
    const nextPid=this.path[0];
    const nextOwner=provOwner[nextPid];
    const nextIsSea=provinces[nextPid]&&provinces[nextPid].isSea;
    // Blocked by peace treaty (but allied territory and sea zones are passable)
    if(!nextIsSea && nextOwner>=0 && nextOwner!==this.civId && !areAtWar(this.civId,nextOwner) && !areAllied(this.civId,nextOwner)) return true;
    // Any hostile occupant in the next land province blocks visually and physically.
    if(!nextIsSea&&_warEnemyCivInProvince(nextPid,this.civId)>=0)return _cacheBlocked(true);
    // Sea zones remain open; fleets enter the same province and combatCooldown then locks
    // both sides in place until the naval fight is resolved.
    return _cacheBlocked(false);
  }

  update(){
    // Once a division has actually engaged, combatCooldown is the event-driven movement
    // lock. This replaces the former per-unit world/contact scan every simulation tick.
    if(this.combatCooldown>0){
      // A peace/special-war cleanup must release ships immediately. The old cooldown was
      // pair-blind, so surviving units could stare at a vanished annihilation opponent in water.
      if(this._combatOpponent>=0&&!areAtWar(this.civId,this._combatOpponent)){this.combatCooldown=0;this._combatOpponent=-1;}
      else return;
    }
    // Player AUTO MOVE / BORDER paths are assigned centrally by tickPlayerArmyPlans().
    // This prevents every army independently choosing the same nearest front province.
    if(this.path.length===0){ this.moveProgress=0; return; }

    const nextPid=this.path[0];
    _setDivisionFacingBetween(this,this.provId,nextPid);
    const np=provinces[nextPid];
    if(!np){ this.path.shift();_syncRenderMover(this); return; }

    // Hard block: can't enter foreign territory at peace (but allies and sea zones are OK)
    const nextOwner=provOwner[nextPid];
    const nextIsSea=provinces[nextPid]&&provinces[nextPid].isSea;

    // Ultra-hot fast path: an own, non-sea destination with no foreign divisions in it
    // cannot be peace-blocked, invasion-slowed, defender-blocked, or head-on blocked.
    // Avoid all war/alliance/index scans for this overwhelmingly common movement case.
    if(!nextIsSea&&nextOwner===this.civId){
      if(_provDivIndexDirty)_rebuildProvDivIndex();
      const occupants=_provDivIndex.get(nextPid);
      if(!occupants||(occupants.size===1&&occupants.has(this.civId))){
        const cc=civs[this.civId],movBonus=cc?._moveMul??(this.civId===playerCivId?(1+(techBonus.movMul||0)):(1+(cc?.techBonus?.movMul||0)));
        this._moveProgress=Math.min(1,this._moveProgress+0.018*movBonus);
        if(this._moveProgress<1){_tryScheduleSafeMove(this);return;}
        this._moveProgress=0;this.path.shift();_syncRenderMover(this);this._vGroupId=null;const oldPid=this.provId;this.provId=nextPid;this._snapToProvince(nextPid);_indexMoveDiv(this,oldPid,nextPid);if(this.civId!==playerCivId)civs[this.civId]._botGroupDirty=true;return;
      }
    }
    if(!nextIsSea && nextOwner>=0 && nextOwner!==this.civId && !areAtWar(this.civId,nextOwner) && !areAllied(this.civId,nextOwner)){
      this.path=[];
      this.moveProgress=0;
      if(this.autoMove) this._autoRepathAt=0;
      return;
    }

    // Any hostile unit physically occupying the next LAND province blocks the route,
    // regardless of who owns that province or which direction the hostile unit is facing.
    // Park once; the province index wakes us only when occupancy changes. This is both the
    // requested all-directions combat rule and the key >2k-unit hot-loop optimization.
    if(!nextIsSea&&_warEnemyCivInProvince(nextPid,this.civId)>=0){
      const p=Math.min(this.moveProgress+0.012,0.45);this._moveProgress=p;_parkBlockedDiv(this,nextPid,p);return;
    }
    if(this._nextIsBlocked()){
      const p=Math.min(this.moveProgress+0.012,0.45);this._moveProgress=p;_parkBlockedDiv(this,nextPid,p);return;
    }

    // Advance progress toward next province.
    // Invading enemy territory (active war) is 3× slower than own/unclaimed/allied land.
    // nextOwner was already read above for the hard-block check — reuse it.
    const invading = !nextIsSea && nextOwner>=0 && nextOwner!==this.civId && areAtWar(this.civId,nextOwner);
    const cc=civs[this.civId];
    const movBonus=cc?._moveMul??(this.civId===playerCivId?(1+(techBonus.movMul||0)):(1+(cc?.techBonus?.movMul||0)));
    const warMoveMul=invading?_warMoveModifier(this.civId,nextOwner):1;
    const MOVE_SPEED = (invading ? 0.006 : (nextIsSea ? 0.018/5 : 0.018)) * movBonus * warMoveMul;
    this._moveProgress=Math.min(1, this._moveProgress+MOVE_SPEED);
    if(this.moveProgress<1&&_tryScheduleSafeMove(this))return;

    if(this._moveProgress>=1){
      // Actually step into the province
      this._moveProgress=0;
      this.path.shift();_syncRenderMover(this);
      this._vGroupId = null; // stepping away dissolves the visual group membership
      const oldPid=this.provId;
      this.provId=nextPid;
      this._snapToProvince(nextPid);
      _indexMoveDiv(this,oldPid,nextPid);
      AudioEngine.divisionTransition(oldPid,nextPid,this.civId);
      if(this.civId!==playerCivId)civs[this.civId]._botGroupDirty=true;

      // Claim province (never claim sea zones — they are always neutral)
      const owner=provOwner[nextPid];
      if(!nextIsSea && owner<0) civClaimProvince(nextPid,this.civId);
      else if(!nextIsSea && owner!==this.civId && areAtWar(this.civId,owner)){
        const defenders=allDivsInProv(nextPid,owner);
        if(defenders.length===0) civClaimProvince(nextPid,this.civId);
      }
    }
  }
  // draw() now only pushes data into the batched queues passed from drawDivisions.
  // All actual ctx calls live in the batched passes there.
  draw(S, badgeQueue, selRings, hpBars, marchDots, anchors, civTierCache, drawSprite=true){
    if(this._hiddenInGroup)return;
    // Screen coordinates were computed once by the viewport collector.
    const sx=this._renderSx,sy=this._renderSy;
    const civ=civs[this.civId];if(!civ)return;
    const dir=this.dir||1;
    const bounce=(this.civId===playerCivId)?_playerFoxBounce(this._animSeed||0):0;
    const isPlayer=this.civId===playerCivId;

    if(this._renderSelected)selRings.push(sx+S*8,sy+S*10,S*34,S*9);

    if(drawSprite)_drawFoxSpriteOnly(ctx,this,S,civTierCache);

    let showBadge=false,badgeNum=1,badgeColor='#f0c060',textColor='#f0d070';
    if(isPlayer){
      if(this._vGroupId!=null&&this._vGroupCount>1){showBadge=true;badgeNum=this._vGroupCount;badgeColor='#a0d8ff';textColor='#c8eeff';}
      else if(this._vGroupId==null&&this._provStackN>1){showBadge=true;badgeNum=this._provStackN;badgeColor='#80d0ff';textColor='#c0eeff';}
    }else{const visualN=this._provStackN||1,dc=this.divCount||1;if(visualN>1||dc>1||this.size>1){showBadge=true;badgeNum=visualN>1?visualN:(dc>1?dc:this.size);}}
    if(showBadge)badgeQueue.push(sx+dir*(-16)*S,sy+(2+bounce)*S,badgeNum,badgeColor,textColor);

    if(this.path.length>0){const hcx=sx+(dir===1?28:-28)*S,hcy=sy+(-14+bounce)*S;marchDots.push(hcx,hcy-11*1.85*S,3.5*S,2*S);}
    if(this.hp<this.maxHp){const bw=40*S,bh=4*S;hpBars.push(sx-18*S,sy+(-36+bounce)*S,bw,bh,Math.max(0,this.hp/this.maxHp));}
    if(provinces[this.provId]?.isSea)anchors.push(sx+(dir===1?22:-22)*S,sy+(-28+bounce)*S);
  }

}

// ── FOX LEG HELPER (used by Division.draw) ───────────────────────────────────
function _foxLeg(ctx, bx, phase, bounce){
  const lb=Math.abs(phase)*1.5, px=phase*2;
  const saved=ctx.fillStyle;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(bx-2,2+bounce); ctx.lineTo(bx-2.5,7+bounce); ctx.lineTo(bx+2.5,7+bounce); ctx.lineTo(bx+2,2+bounce); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(bx-2.5,7+bounce); ctx.lineTo(bx-3+px,13+lb); ctx.lineTo(bx+3+px,13+lb); ctx.lineTo(bx+2.5,7+bounce); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle='#1a1a1a'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(bx+px,14+lb,2.5,1.8,0,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle=saved;
}


function spawnFox(civId,provId){
  const civ=civs[civId]; if(!civ) return;
  const count = civId===playerCivId ? civ.foxes.length : civFoxDivCount(civ);
  if(count>=civFoxCap(civ)) return;
  const d=new Division(civId,provId);civ.foxes.push(d);_indexAddDiv(d);
  if(civId===playerCivId&&playerAutoMoveUniversal&&gameReady)_markPlayerArmyPlanDirty(true);
}

// ── WAR SYSTEM ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const warPairs=new Set(); // integer keys: min(a,b)*10000 + max(a,b)
let _warAdjDirty=true;
const _warAdj=[]; // civId -> reusable enemy id[]; rebuilt once per war-graph mutation
function _markWarAdjDirty(){_warAdjDirty=true;_markPlayerArmyPlanDirty(true);}
function _ensureWarAdj(){
  if(!_warAdjDirty&&_warAdj.length>=civs.length)return _warAdj;
  _warAdj.length=civs.length;for(let i=0;i<civs.length;i++){let a=_warAdj[i];if(!a)a=_warAdj[i]=[];else a.length=0;}
  for(const key of warPairs){const a=(key/10000)|0,b=key%10000;if(a>=0&&b>=0&&a<civs.length&&b<civs.length){_warAdj[a].push(b);_warAdj[b].push(a);}}
  _warAdjDirty=false;return _warAdj;
}
// Integer war key: min*10000+max (safe for civ counts < 10000)
function warKey(a,b){ return a<b ? a*10000+b : b*10000+a; }
function areAtWar(a,b){ return a!==b && warPairs.has(warKey(a,b)); }

// ── ALLIANCE SYSTEM ────────────────────────────────────────────────
// alliances: Array of {id, name, leaderId, memberIds: Set<number>, color}
let alliances = [];
let _nextAllianceId = 0;
const ALLIANCE_JOIN_COST = 200; // coins required to join an existing alliance
let _allianceStructVersion=1,_allianceIndexVersion=0;
let _civAllianceIndex=new Int32Array(0);
function _touchAllianceMembership(){_allianceStructVersion++;_allianceMembershipDirty=true;if(typeof _touchRouteTopology==='function')_touchRouteTopology();_markPlayerArmyPlanDirty(true);}
let _allianceMembershipDirty=true;
function _ensureAllianceMembershipIndex(){
  if(!_allianceMembershipDirty&&_allianceIndexVersion===_allianceStructVersion&&_civAllianceIndex.length>=civs.length)return;
  if(_civAllianceIndex.length<civs.length)_civAllianceIndex=new Int32Array(Math.max(civs.length,_civAllianceIndex.length*2||256));
  _civAllianceIndex.fill(-1);
  for(let ai=0;ai<alliances.length;ai++)for(const cid of alliances[ai].memberIds)if(cid>=0&&cid<civs.length)_civAllianceIndex[cid]=ai;
  _allianceMembershipDirty=false;_allianceIndexVersion=_allianceStructVersion;
}
function getAllianceOfCiv(civId){
  if(civId<0||civId>=civs.length)return null;_ensureAllianceMembershipIndex();
  const ai=_civAllianceIndex[civId];return ai>=0?alliances[ai]||null:null;
}
function areAllied(a, b){
  if(a === b||a<0||b<0) return false;
  _ensureAllianceMembershipIndex();
  return a<_civAllianceIndex.length&&b<_civAllianceIndex.length&&_civAllianceIndex[a]>=0&&_civAllianceIndex[a]===_civAllianceIndex[b];
}
function allianceColor(idx){
  const palette=['#60c0e0','#e0a060','#80d080','#e08080','#c080e0','#d0d060','#60c0c0'];
  return palette[idx % palette.length];
}

function createAlliance(leaderId, name){
  const existing = getAllianceOfCiv(leaderId);
  if(existing) return null; // already in one
  const al = {id: _nextAllianceId++, name: name.trim() || 'New Alliance', leaderId, memberIds: new Set([leaderId]), color: allianceColor(alliances.length)};
  alliances.push(al);
  _touchAllianceMembership();
  _allianceLabelDirty = true; _allianceBorderDirty = true; _alOverlayDirty = true;
  if(leaderId===playerCivId)AudioEngine.alliance();
  return al;
}
function joinAlliance(allianceId, civId){
  const al = alliances.find(a => a.id === allianceId);
  if(!al) return false;
  if(getAllianceOfCiv(civId)) return false; // already in one
  const civ = civs[civId];
  if(!civ) return false;
  if(civ.res.coins < ALLIANCE_JOIN_COST) return false;
  civ.res.coins -= ALLIANCE_JOIN_COST;
  al.memberIds.add(civId);
  _touchAllianceMembership();
  _allianceLabelDirty = true; _allianceBorderDirty = true; _alOverlayDirty = true;
  if(civId===playerCivId||al.leaderId===playerCivId)AudioEngine.alliance();
  return true;
}
function leaveAlliance(civId){
  const al = getAllianceOfCiv(civId);
  if(!al) return;
  const playerRelevant=(civId===playerCivId||al.leaderId===playerCivId);
  al.memberIds.delete(civId);
  _touchAllianceMembership();
  _allianceLabelDirty = true; _allianceBorderDirty = true; _alOverlayDirty = true;
  if(al.memberIds.size === 0){
    alliances.splice(alliances.indexOf(al), 1);
    return;
  }
  // If leader left, promote first remaining member
  if(al.leaderId === civId){
    al.leaderId = al.memberIds.values().next().value;
  }
  if(playerRelevant)AudioEngine.allianceBreak();
}
function promoteAllianceLeader(newLeaderId){
  const al = getAllianceOfCiv(newLeaderId);
  if(!al) return;
  if(al.leaderId !== playerCivId) return; // only current leader can promote
  al.leaderId = newLeaderId;
}
function disbandAlliance(allianceId){
  const idx = alliances.findIndex(a => a.id === allianceId);
  if(idx >= 0){const playerRelevant=alliances[idx]?.memberIds?.has(playerCivId);alliances.splice(idx, 1);_touchAllianceMembership();_allianceLabelDirty=true;_allianceBorderDirty=true;_alOverlayDirty=true;if(playerRelevant)AudioEngine.allianceBreak();}
}
// Invite a nation into your alliance — free (leader extends the invitation)
function inviteToAlliance(allianceId, civId){
  const al = alliances.find(a => a.id === allianceId);
  if(!al) return false;
  if(getAllianceOfCiv(civId)) return false; // already in an alliance
  const civ = civs[civId];
  if(!civ || !civ.ownedProvs.size) return false;
  al.memberIds.add(civId);
  _touchAllianceMembership();
  _allianceLabelDirty = true; _allianceBorderDirty = true; _alOverlayDirty = true;
  if(al.leaderId===playerCivId)AudioEngine.alliance();
  return true;
}
// Kick a member from alliance (leader only)
function kickFromAlliance(targetCivId){
  if(playerCivId < 0) return;
  const al = getAllianceOfCiv(playerCivId);
  if(!al || al.leaderId !== playerCivId) return;
  if(targetCivId === playerCivId) return; // can't kick self — use leave
  al.memberIds.delete(targetCivId);
  _touchAllianceMembership();
  if(al.memberIds.size === 0){
    alliances.splice(alliances.indexOf(al), 1);
  }
  _allianceLabelDirty = true; _allianceBorderDirty = true; _alOverlayDirty = true;
  AudioEngine.allianceBreak();
}
// Betray: kick member AND declare war on them
function betrayAndAttack(targetCivId){
  if(playerCivId < 0) return;
  const al = getAllianceOfCiv(playerCivId);
  if(!al) return;
  // Remove the target from the alliance first so declareWar doesn't block
  al.memberIds.delete(targetCivId);
  _touchAllianceMembership();
  if(al.memberIds.size === 0){
    alliances.splice(alliances.indexOf(al), 1);
  }
  _allianceLabelDirty = true; _allianceBorderDirty = true; _alOverlayDirty = true;
  // Now declare war (propagates to both alliances)
  declareWar(playerCivId, targetCivId);
}

// ── VIEW MODE ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
let viewMode = 'nations'; // 'nations' | 'alliances'

// ── ALLIANCE LABEL CACHE ──────────────────────────────────────────
// HOI4-style spread labels for alliance map overlay
let _allianceLabelCache = null;
let _allianceLabelDirty = true;
const _allianceLabelImgCache = new Map();
const _ALLIANCE_LABEL_CACHE_MAX=240;
function _trimAllianceLabelImgCache(){while(_allianceLabelImgCache.size>_ALLIANCE_LABEL_CACHE_MAX)_allianceLabelImgCache.delete(_allianceLabelImgCache.keys().next().value);}
let _allianceLabelImgZoom = -1;

// ── ALLIANCE BORDER CACHE ─────────────────────────────────────────
// Segments where adjacent tiles belong to DIFFERENT alliance groups.
// Group = allianceId of the tile's owning civ, or -1 if no alliance.
// Rebuilt whenever alliance membership changes.
let _allianceBorderSegs = null;
let _allianceBorderDirty = true;
let _allianceBorderSegVersion=0;
let _allianceBorderPathChunks=null;
let _alBdrPath2D=null, _alBdrCamX=-999, _alBdrCamY=-999, _alBdrZoom=-1;

const WAR_TYPES=Object.freeze({normal:{label:'Normal War',speed:1,attack:1,defense:1},conquest:{label:'Conquest War',speed:1.30,attack:1,defense:.80},raid:{label:'Raid War',speed:1.70,attack:.80,defense:1},annihilation:{label:'Annihilation War',speed:1,attack:1.50,defense:.30}});
function _warTypeForPair(a,b){return warRecords.get(warKey(a,b))?.type||'normal';}
function _warSideModifier(civId,otherId,kind){
  const rec=warRecords.get(warKey(civId,otherId));if(!rec)return 1;
  const wt=WAR_TYPES[rec.type]||WAR_TYPES.normal,side=rec.attackerSide||[];
  // Alliance battles can call this several times per combat pair. Membership is immutable
  // for the lifetime of a war record, so avoid a linear Array.includes() scan on every hit.
  let atk=rec._attackerSet;if(!atk||rec._attackerSetSrc!==side){atk=rec._attackerSet=new Set(side);rec._attackerSetSrc=side;}
  if(!atk.has(civId))return 1;return wt[kind]||1;
}
function _warMoveModifier(civId,nextOwner){return nextOwner>=0&&areAtWar(civId,nextOwner)?_warSideModifier(civId,nextOwner,'speed'):1;}

function declareWar(attackerId, defenderId, warType='normal'){
  if(attackerId===defenderId) return;
  // BLOCK: you cannot declare war on your own alliance member
  if(areAllied(attackerId, defenderId)) return;
  if(areAtWar(attackerId,defenderId)) return;

  warType=WAR_TYPES[warType]?warType:'normal';
  // ── Collect all pairs to add (alliance propagation) ───────────────
  // Gather attacker-side: attacker + all their alliance members
  // Gather defender-side: defender + all their alliance members
  const atkAlliance = getAllianceOfCiv(attackerId);
  const defAlliance = getAllianceOfCiv(defenderId);

  const attackerSide = _expandWarSide(atkAlliance ? [...atkAlliance.memberIds] : [attackerId]);
  const defenderSide = _expandWarSide(defAlliance ? [...defAlliance.memberIds] : [defenderId]);

  // One declaration-wide pre-war snapshot is shared by every propagated pair. Pair-local
  // snapshots are insufficient once alliance members capitulate at different times: the
  // last surviving pair may no longer contain every province whose ownership changed.
  const declarationId=((_warDeclarationSerial++)>>>0)||1;
  const declarationOwners={};
  for(const cid of [...attackerSide,...defenderSide]){
    const c=civs[cid];if(!c)continue;
    for(const pid of c.ownedProvs)if(provinces[pid]&&!provinces[pid].isSea)declarationOwners[pid]=cid;
  }

  warDeclarations.set(declarationId,{
    id:declarationId,type:warType,declaredBy:attackerId,attackerSide:[...attackerSide],defenderSide:[...defenderSide],
    originalOwners:{...declarationOwners},invaded:new Set(),started:{day:gameDay,month:gameMonth,year:gameYear}
  });

  // Collect new war pairs to add (avoid adding within same side)
  const newPairs = [];
  for(const a of attackerSide){
    for(const d of defenderSide){
      if(a === d) continue;
      if(!areAtWar(a, d)) newPairs.push([a, d]);
    }
  }

  if(newPairs.length && (attackerSide.includes(playerCivId)||defenderSide.includes(playerCivId))){AudioEngine.war();if(warType==='annihilation')AudioEngine.smite();}

  // Commit all new war pairs. Only participants gain/lose legal movement edges;
  // invalidating every country's route-component cache made large war histories progressively slower.
  if(newPairs.length)_touchRouteWarParticipants(newPairs.flat());
  for(const [a, d] of newPairs){
    warPairs.add(warKey(a, d));_markWarAdjDirty();_botWarCacheDirty=true;_markBotStrategyCivDirty(a);_markBotStrategyCivDirty(d);
    const rec=_ensureWarRecord(a,d);rec.type=warType;rec.declaredBy=attackerId;rec.attackerSide=[...attackerSide];rec.defenderSide=[...defenderSide];
    rec.declarationId=declarationId;rec.declarationOwners={...declarationOwners};
    const att=civs[a], def=civs[d];
    if(att && def){
      showWarNotification(att.name+' declared '+WAR_TYPES[warType].label+' on '+def.name+'!', att.color);
      if(a!==playerCivId) botLaunchAttack(att, d);
      if(d!==playerCivId) botLaunchAttack(def, a);
    }
  }
}


function makePeace(a,b){_wakeAllBlockedDivs();
  const key=warKey(a,b), playerPeace=(a===playerCivId||b===playerCivId)&&warPairs.has(key);
  warPairs.delete(key);_markWarAdjDirty();_touchRouteWarParticipants([a,b]);_botWarCacheDirty=true;_markBotStrategyCivDirty(a);_markBotStrategyCivDirty(b);
  warRecords.delete(key);_cleanupUnusedWarDeclarations();
  if(playerPeace)AudioEngine.peace();
  if(playerAutoMoveUniversal)setTimeout(_refreshUniversalAutoMovePlan,0);
  if(typeof _tryStartQueuedPeaceConferences==='function') setTimeout(_tryStartQueuedPeaceConferences,0);
}

// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// SIMPLE BATCH PEACE SETTLEMENTS / CAPITULATION / NEW SOVEREIGN STATES
// Rules:
//   1) Capitulated countries are queued while the player keeps fighting.
//   2) No settlement UI opens until ALL of the player's active wars are over.
//   3) Defeated countries are settled ONE BY ONE.
//   4) No war-score economy, no occupation claims, no reparations, no pass turns.
//   5) Settlement is simply: select land -> choose recipient -> give land.
// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
const warRecords = new Map(); // warKey -> exact ownership snapshots used to restore pre-war borders
const warDeclarations = new Map(); // declarationId -> declaration-wide immutable borders + annihilation devastation
let _warDeclarationSerial=1;
let peaceConference = null;
let pendingPeaceSettlements = []; // queued player victories waiting for every player war to finish
let _peacePrevSpeed = 1;
let _peaceConferenceSerial = 1;
let _peaceBatchTotal = 0;
let _peaceBatchCompleted = 0;
let _peaceRecipientStickyId = -1;
let peaceBrush = 1;
let _peaceHoverPid = -1;

function _decodeWarKey(key){
  return [Math.floor(key/10000), key%10000];
}

function _expandWarSide(seedIds){
  const out=new Set(seedIds);
  let changed=true;
  while(changed){
    changed=false;
    for(const id of [...out]){
      const al=getAllianceOfCiv(id);
      if(al) for(const m of al.memberIds) if(!out.has(m)){out.add(m);changed=true;}
    }
  }
  return [...out];
}

function _ensureWarRecord(a,b){
  const key=warKey(a,b);
  let rec=warRecords.get(key);
  if(rec) return rec;
  const ca=civs[a], cb=civs[b];
  rec={
    key,a,b,
    startA:ca?[...ca.ownedProvs]:[],
    startB:cb?[...cb.ownedProvs]:[],
    startOwners:{},
    contrib:{[a]:10,[b]:10},
    started:{day:gameDay,month:gameMonth,year:gameYear},type:'normal',declaredBy:a,attackerSide:[a],defenderSide:[b]
  };
  for(const pid of rec.startA) rec.startOwners[pid]=a;
  for(const pid of rec.startB) rec.startOwners[pid]=b;
  warRecords.set(key,rec);
  return rec;
}

function _trackAnnihilationCapture(prevOwner,newOwner,pid){
  const rec=warRecords.get(warKey(prevOwner,newOwner));if(!rec||rec.type!=='annihilation')return;
  let decl=rec.declarationId!=null?warDeclarations.get(rec.declarationId):null;
  if(!decl){
    decl={id:rec.declarationId??null,type:'annihilation',declaredBy:rec.declaredBy,attackerSide:[...(rec.attackerSide||[rec.a])],defenderSide:[...(rec.defenderSide||[rec.b])],originalOwners:{...(rec.declarationOwners||rec.startOwners||{})},invaded:new Set(),started:rec.started||null};
    if(decl.id!=null)warDeclarations.set(decl.id,decl);
  }
  // Every province that changes hands across this declaration is remembered exactly once.
  // Recapture does not erase devastation history: annihilation damage is about territory
  // that was invaded at any point, not merely the final occupation at capitulation.
  decl.invaded.add(pid);
}
function _cleanupUnusedWarDeclarations(){
  const live=new Set();for(const rec of warRecords.values())if(rec?.declarationId!=null)live.add(rec.declarationId);
  for(const id of warDeclarations.keys())if(!live.has(id))warDeclarations.delete(id);
}

function _peaceProvinceCost(pid){
  // Kept for bot-only automatic peace weighting and backward compatibility.
  const p=provinces[pid]; if(!p||p.isSea) return 0;
  const b=p.bld||{};
  const industry=(b.farm||0)+(b.sawmill||0)+(b.mine||0)+(b.market||0)+(b.barracks||0)*1.4+(b.fort||0)*1.2+(b.tower||0);
  return Math.max(8,Math.round(7+Math.sqrt(Math.max(1,p.tiles.length))*0.55+industry*4));
}

function _recordWarContribution(winnerId, loserId, pid=null, bonus=0){
  // Contribution is no longer exposed to the player, but bot-only wars still use it
  // to choose a sensible primary victor when they resolve automatically.
  if(winnerId<0||loserId<0||winnerId===loserId) return;
  const key=warKey(winnerId,loserId);
  if(!warPairs.has(key)) return;
  const rec=_ensureWarRecord(winnerId,loserId);
  const amount=(pid!=null?_peaceProvinceCost(pid):0)+bonus;
  rec.contrib[winnerId]=(rec.contrib[winnerId]||0)+Math.max(1,amount);
}

const _emptyWarEnemies=[];
function _warEnemiesFor(civId){
  if(civId<0||civId>=civs.length)return _emptyWarEnemies;
  const a=_ensureWarAdj()[civId];return a||_emptyWarEnemies;
}

function _playerHasActiveWars(){
  if(playerCivId<0) return false;
  return _warEnemiesFor(playerCivId).some(id=>civs[id]&&civs[id].ownedProvs.size>0);
}

function _warContributionAgainst(victorId,defeatedId){
  const rec=warRecords.get(warKey(victorId,defeatedId));
  return rec?.contrib?.[victorId]||0;
}

function _defeatedWarStates(defeatedId){
  const set=new Set();
  for(const rec of warRecords.values()){
    if(rec.a===defeatedId) for(const pid of rec.startA||[]) if(provinces[pid]&&!provinces[pid].isSea) set.add(pid);
    if(rec.b===defeatedId) for(const pid of rec.startB||[]) if(provinces[pid]&&!provinces[pid].isSea) set.add(pid);
  }
  if(set.size===0){
    const civ=civs[defeatedId];
    if(civ) for(const p of provinces) if(!p.isSea && p.countryId===civ.countryId) set.add(p.id);
  }
  return [...set];
}

function _primaryVictor(defeatedId,victors){
  let best=victors[0]??-1,bestScore=-Infinity;
  for(const id of victors){
    let score=_warContributionAgainst(id,defeatedId);
    for(const pid of _defeatedWarStates(defeatedId)) if(provOwner[pid]===id) score+=_peaceProvinceCost(pid)*2;
    if(score>bestScore){bestScore=score;best=id;}
  }
  return best;
}

function _cleanupWarRecords(records){
  const affected=[],keys=new Set();let changed=false;
  for(const rec of records||[]){if(!rec)continue;keys.add(warKey(rec.a,rec.b));}
  for(const key of keys){
    const rec=warRecords.get(key);
    const pair=rec?[rec.a,rec.b]:_decodeWarKey(key),a=pair[0],b=pair[1];
    if(!warPairs.has(key)&&!warRecords.has(key))continue;
    warPairs.delete(key);warRecords.delete(key);_markWarAdjDirty();affected.push(a,b);changed=true;
    _botWarCacheDirty=true;_markBotStrategyCivDirty(a);_markBotStrategyCivDirty(b);
  }
  if(changed){_touchRouteWarParticipants(affected);_wakeAllBlockedDivs();}
  _cleanupUnusedWarDeclarations();
  if(changed&&playerAutoMoveUniversal)setTimeout(_refreshUniversalAutoMovePlan,0);
  return changed;
}
function _cleanupWarForDefeated(defeatedId){
  const records=[];
  for(const rec of warRecords.values())if(rec&&(rec.a===defeatedId||rec.b===defeatedId))records.push(rec);
  _cleanupWarRecords(records);
  // Do NOT scan every division after every peace. Illegal next steps are rejected lazily
  // by Division.update(), while repatriation handles units physically left in foreign land.
}

const _repatriateScratch=[];
function _repatriatePeaceDivisions(){
  if(_provDivIndexDirty)_rebuildProvDivIndex();
  _repatriateScratch.length=0;
  // Iterate occupied province buckets, not every army in every country. In the common
  // case nearly all troops are already home and never enter the move list.
  for(const [pid,byCiv] of _provDivIndex){
    const owner=provOwner[pid];if(owner<0)continue; // sea/unowned needs no repatriation
    for(const [cid,arr] of byCiv){
      if(cid===owner||!arr.length||areAtWar(owner,cid)||areAllied(owner,cid))continue;
      const civ=civs[cid];if(!civ?.ownedProvs.size)continue;
      const fallback=civ.ownedProvs.values().next().value;
      for(const d of arr)_repatriateScratch.push(d,fallback);
    }
  }
  for(let i=0;i<_repatriateScratch.length;i+=2){
    const d=_repatriateScratch[i],fallback=_repatriateScratch[i+1],oldPid=d.provId;
    d.path=[];d.moveProgress=0;d.provId=fallback;d._snapToProvince(fallback);_indexMoveDiv(d,oldPid,fallback);
  }
}

function _removeCivFromAlliance(civId){
  const al=getAllianceOfCiv(civId);
  if(!al) return;
  al.memberIds.delete(civId);_touchAllianceMembership();
  if(al.memberIds.size===0) alliances.splice(alliances.indexOf(al),1);
  else if(al.leaderId===civId) al.leaderId=al.memberIds.values().next().value;
  _allianceLabelDirty=true;_allianceBorderDirty=true;_alOverlayDirty=true;
}

function _autoResolveDefeat(defeatedId,victors){
  const defeated=civs[defeatedId]; if(!defeated) return;
  const primary=_primaryVictor(defeatedId,victors);
  const states=_defeatedWarStates(defeatedId);
  for(const pid of states){
    const owner=provOwner[pid];
    if(!victors.includes(owner) && primary>=0) civClaimProvince(pid,primary);
  }
  _indexClearCivDivs(defeated);
  _cleanupWarForDefeated(defeatedId);
  if(defeated.ownedProvs.size===0){_removeCivFromAlliance(defeatedId);}
  _repatriatePeaceDivisions();
  if(defeatedId===playerCivId)AudioEngine.capitulation(false);else if(victors.includes(playerCivId))AudioEngine.capitulation(true);
  showWarNotification(defeated.name+' capitulated!',primary>=0?civs[primary].color:'#f0a030');
}

function _pcProvinceOriginalCiv(pid){
  const countryId=provinces[pid]?.countryId;
  if(countryId==null||countryId<0) return -1;
  const c=civs.find(x=>x&&x.countryId===countryId&&!x.customNation);
  return c?c.id:-1;
}

function _peaceOriginalOwnerMap(defeatedId){
  const map=new Map();
  for(const rec of warRecords.values()){
    if(rec.a!==defeatedId&&rec.b!==defeatedId) continue;
    for(const [pidStr,owner] of Object.entries(rec.startOwners||{})){
      const pid=+pidStr;if(provinces[pid]&&!provinces[pid].isSea&&!map.has(pid))map.set(pid,+owner);
    }
    if(!(rec.startOwners&&Object.keys(rec.startOwners).length)){
      if(rec.a===defeatedId) for(const pid of (rec.startA||[])) if(!map.has(pid))map.set(pid,defeatedId);
      if(rec.b===defeatedId) for(const pid of (rec.startB||[])) if(!map.has(pid))map.set(pid,defeatedId);
    }
  }
  for(const pid of _defeatedWarStates(defeatedId)){
    if(map.has(pid)) continue;
    const core=_pcProvinceOriginalCiv(pid);
    map.set(pid,core>=0?core:defeatedId);
  }
  return map;
}

function _makePeaceSettlementSnapshot(defeatedId,victors){
  const eligible=_defeatedWarStates(defeatedId).filter(pid=>provinces[pid]&&!provinces[pid].isSea);
  const originalOwners=_peaceOriginalOwnerMap(defeatedId);
  return {
    defeatedId,
    victors:[...new Set(victors)].filter(id=>civs[id]),
    eligible:[...eligible],
    originalOwners:Object.fromEntries([...originalOwners.entries()].map(([pid,owner])=>[String(pid),owner]))
  };
}

function _queuePlayerPeaceSettlement(defeatedId,victors){
  if(pendingPeaceSettlements.some(x=>x.defeatedId===defeatedId) || peaceConference?.defeatedId===defeatedId) return;
  const snap=_makePeaceSettlementSnapshot(defeatedId,victors);
  if(!snap.eligible.length) return;
  pendingPeaceSettlements.push(snap);
}

function _serializePendingPeaceSettlements(){
  return pendingPeaceSettlements.map(x=>({
    defeatedId:x.defeatedId,
    victors:[...(x.victors||[])],
    eligible:[...(x.eligible||[])],
    originalOwners:{...(x.originalOwners||{})}
  }));
}

function _restorePendingPeaceSettlements(rows){
  pendingPeaceSettlements=[];
  for(const x of (rows||[])){
    if(!x||!Number.isInteger(x.defeatedId)||!civs[x.defeatedId]) continue;
    pendingPeaceSettlements.push({
      defeatedId:x.defeatedId,
      victors:Array.isArray(x.victors)?x.victors.filter(id=>Number.isInteger(id)&&civs[id]):[],
      eligible:Array.isArray(x.eligible)?x.eligible.filter(pid=>Number.isInteger(pid)&&provinces[pid]&&!provinces[pid].isSea):[],
      originalOwners:{...(x.originalOwners||{})}
    });
  }
}

function _tryStartQueuedPeaceConferences(){
  if(peaceConference||!gameReady||playerCivId<0||!pendingPeaceSettlements.length) return false;
  // Core rule: never interrupt the player while ANY of their wars are still active.
  if(_playerHasActiveWars()) return false;
  if(_peaceBatchTotal<=0){
    _peaceBatchTotal=pendingPeaceSettlements.length;
    _peaceBatchCompleted=0;
    _peaceRecipientStickyId=playerCivId;
    _peacePrevSpeed=gameSpeed||_preEscSpeed||1;
  }
  const next=pendingPeaceSettlements.shift();
  if(!next){_peaceBatchTotal=0;_peaceBatchCompleted=0;return false;}
  startPeaceConference(next);
  return true;
}

function _rollbackCivTechSteps(civId,steps=2){
  const civ=civs[civId];if(!civ)return;  const set=civId===playerCivId?playerTechs:(civ.techs||(civ.techs=new Set()));
  // Economic tree: remove exactly the top N researched tiers.
  let econMax=0;for(let i=0;i<_ECON_TIERS.length;i++)if(_ECON_TIERS[i].some(id=>set.has(id)))econMax=i+1;
  const econKeep=Math.max(0,econMax-steps);for(let i=econKeep;i<_ECON_TIERS.length;i++)for(const id of _ECON_TIERS[i])set.delete(id);
  // Military tree is six parallel branches. Roll each branch back independently by N tiers
  // instead of treating the row list as one long sequence (which used to erase whole branches).
  for(const prefix of ['arm','gun','spc','bot','air','wmd']){
    let max=0;for(const id of set){const m=id.match(new RegExp('^'+prefix+'(\\d+)$'));if(m)max=Math.max(max,+m[1]);}
    const keep=Math.max(0,max-steps);for(const id of [...set]){const m=id.match(new RegExp('^'+prefix+'(\\d+)$'));if(m&&+m[1]>keep)set.delete(id);}
  }
  if(civId===playerCivId){civ.techs=new Set(playerTechs);_recomputeTechBonuses();}
  else _recomputeBotTechBonus(civ);
}
function _damageBuildingsInProvinces(targets){
  // Annihilation devastates infrastructure without deleting a country's entire economy:
  // every existing building in actually invaded territory loses ONE level, never all levels.
  let changed=0;for(const pid of targets||[]){const p=provinces[pid];if(!p||p.isSea)continue;let had=false;for(const k of Object.keys(p.bld||{})){const lv=p.bld[k]||0;if(lv>0){had=true;p.bld[k]=Math.max(0,lv-1);}}if(had)changed++;}
  if(changed)_markEconomyAggregatesDirty();return changed;
}
function _destroyCivBuildings(civId,records=null){
  const civ=civs[civId];if(!civ)return 0;
  const targets=new Set();
  if(Array.isArray(records)&&records.length){for(const rec of records){if(rec.a===civId)for(const pid of (rec.startA||[]))targets.add(pid);if(rec.b===civId)for(const pid of (rec.startB||[]))targets.add(pid);}}
  else for(const pid of civ.ownedProvs)targets.add(pid);
  return _damageBuildingsInProvinces(targets);
}
function _restoreWarBordersForRecords(records){
  if(!records?.length)return;
  const prevSuppress=_suppressWarContribution;
  _beginRouteOwnershipBatch();_suppressWarContribution=true;
  try{for(const rec of records)for(const [pidStr,owner] of Object.entries(rec.startOwners||{})){const pid=+pidStr;if(provinces[pid]&&!provinces[pid].isSea&&civs[owner])civClaimProvince(pid,owner);}}
  finally{_suppressWarContribution=prevSuppress;_endRouteOwnershipBatch();}
}
function _restoreWarBordersForPair(rec){if(rec)_restoreWarBordersForRecords([rec]);}
function _specialDeclarationRecords(seed){
  if(!seed)return [];
  if(seed.declarationId!=null)return [...warRecords.values()].filter(r=>r&&r.type===seed.type&&r.declarationId===seed.declarationId);
  return [...warRecords.values()].filter(r=>r&&r.type===seed.type&&r.declaredBy===seed.declaredBy&&r.started?.day===seed.started?.day&&r.started?.month===seed.started?.month&&r.started?.year===seed.started?.year);
}
function _restoreSpecialWarForDefeated(defeatedId,seed,records,enemies){
  const owners=seed?.declarationOwners&&Object.keys(seed.declarationOwners).length?seed.declarationOwners:null;
  const enemySet=new Set(enemies||[]),prevSuppress=_suppressWarContribution;
  _beginRouteOwnershipBatch();_suppressWarContribution=true;
  try{
    if(owners){
      // Restore only borders involving the capitulating nation. This prevents one member
      // of an alliance war from rewinding territory that another still-active war changed.
      for(const [pidStr,originalOwnerRaw] of Object.entries(owners)){
        const pid=+pidStr,originalOwner=+originalOwnerRaw;if(!provinces[pid]||provinces[pid].isSea||!civs[originalOwner])continue;
        const cur=provOwner[pid];
        if(originalOwner===defeatedId){if(cur===defeatedId||enemySet.has(cur))civClaimProvince(pid,defeatedId);}
        else if(cur===defeatedId&&enemySet.has(originalOwner))civClaimProvince(pid,originalOwner);
      }
    }else{
      // Backward-compatible saves created before declaration-wide snapshots existed.
      const scoped=[];
      for(const rec of records){
        const copy={...rec,startOwners:{}};
        for(const [pidStr,ownerRaw] of Object.entries(rec.startOwners||{})){
          const pid=+pidStr,owner=+ownerRaw,cur=provOwner[pid];
          if(owner===defeatedId?(cur===defeatedId||enemySet.has(cur)):(cur===defeatedId&&enemySet.has(owner)))copy.startOwners[pidStr]=owner;
        }
        scoped.push(copy);
      }
      _restoreWarBordersForRecords(scoped);
    }
  }finally{_suppressWarContribution=prevSuppress;_endRouteOwnershipBatch();}
}
function _applySpecialWarVictory(defeatedId,enemies){
  const pairRecords=enemies.map(id=>warRecords.get(warKey(id,defeatedId))).filter(Boolean);
  const special=pairRecords.find(r=>r&&(r.type==='raid'||r.type==='annihilation'));
  if(!special)return false;
  // A capitulation can belong to several simultaneous wars. Resolve ONLY the special
  // declaration that triggered this branch; unrelated normal/raid/alliance wars stay alive.
  const declarationRecords=_specialDeclarationRecords(special);
  const specialRecords=(declarationRecords.length?declarationRecords:pairRecords.filter(r=>r.type===special.type))
    .filter(r=>r&&(r.a===defeatedId||r.b===defeatedId));
  const specialEnemies=[...new Set(specialRecords.map(r=>r.a===defeatedId?r.b:r.a))].filter(id=>civs[id]);
  if(!specialEnemies.length)return false;
  const declaration=special.declarationId!=null?warDeclarations.get(special.declarationId):null;
  const victor=special.declaredBy>=0&&specialEnemies.includes(special.declaredBy)?special.declaredBy:_primaryVictor(defeatedId,specialEnemies);
  const defeated=civs[defeatedId],winner=civs[victor];if(!defeated||!winner)return false;
  if(special.type==='raid'){
    for(const k of ['wood','stone','food','coins']){winner.res[k]=(winner.res[k]||0)+(defeated.res[k]||0);defeated.res[k]=0;}
    _restoreSpecialWarForDefeated(defeatedId,special,specialRecords,specialEnemies);
    showWarNotification(`${winner.name} completed a raid on ${defeated.name} · all resources seized · borders restored`,winner.color);
  }else{
    // ANNIHILATION IS A DESTRUCTIVE RAID, NOT ANNEXATION. Track every province that
    // actually changed hands during this declaration, devastate the defeated side's
    // invaded territory, then restore its exact declaration-time borders.
    const originalOwners=declaration?.originalOwners||special.declarationOwners||{};
    const invaded=new Set(declaration?.invaded||[]);
    // Backward-compatible/failsafe reconstruction: if an older save did not record
    // capture history, any defeated pre-war province currently held by an enemy was invaded.
    for(const [pidStr,ownerRaw] of Object.entries(originalOwners)){const pid=+pidStr,owner=+ownerRaw;if(owner===defeatedId&&provOwner[pid]!==defeatedId)invaded.add(pid);}
    // Only devastation inside the capitulating country's original territory belongs to
    // this victory. This prevents simultaneous alliance wars from destroying partners' cities.
    const devastated=new Set();for(const pid of invaded)if(+originalOwners[pid]===defeatedId)devastated.add(pid);
    const buildingsDamaged=_damageBuildingsInProvinces(devastated);
    // Annihilation NEVER removes city markers. Cities are permanent map geography;
    // only infrastructure/technology is devastated and borders are restored.
    const citiesDestroyed=0;
    _rollbackCivTechSteps(defeatedId,2);
    // Use the exact same proven border rollback lifecycle as Raid War. Annihilation differs
    // only by its destructive effects above; it must NEVER have a second annexation/revert
    // algorithm of its own.
    _restoreSpecialWarForDefeated(defeatedId,special,specialRecords,specialEnemies);
    _markCapitalsDirty();
    if(winner.id===playerCivId||defeated.id===playerCivId)AudioEngine.smite();
    showWarNotification(`${winner.name} WON · ${defeated.name} annihilated · MAP REVERTED · ${buildingsDamaged} built-up state${buildingsDamaged===1?'':'s'} damaged · resources retained · technology −2 tiers`,winner.color);
  }
  _cleanupWarRecords(specialRecords);_repatriatePeaceDivisions();return true;
}

function checkWarConclusions(candidateIds=null){
  if(peaceConference) return;
  let changed=false;
  const n=candidateIds?candidateIds.length:civs.length;
  for(let ci=0;ci<n;ci++){
    const defeated=civs[candidateIds?candidateIds[ci]:ci];
    if(!defeated||defeated.ownedProvs.size>0) continue;
    const enemies=_warEnemiesFor(defeated.id).filter(id=>civs[id]?.ownedProvs.size>0);
    if(!enemies.length) continue;
    _indexClearCivDivs(defeated);
    if(_applySpecialWarVictory(defeated.id,enemies)){changed=true;continue;}
    if(playerCivId>=0&&enemies.includes(playerCivId)){
      // Snapshot BEFORE deleting war records; they contain the exact pre-war borders.
      _queuePlayerPeaceSettlement(defeated.id,enemies);
      _cleanupWarForDefeated(defeated.id);
      _repatriatePeaceDivisions();
      const left=_warEnemiesFor(playerCivId).filter(id=>civs[id]?.ownedProvs.size>0).length;
      showWarNotification(
        left>0
          ? `${defeated.name} capitulated · ${left} war${left===1?'':'s'} still active · settlement queued`
          : `${defeated.name} capitulated · all your wars are over · opening settlements`,
        civs[playerCivId]?.color||'#f0a030'
      );
      changed=true;
    } else {
      _autoResolveDefeat(defeated.id,enemies);
      changed=true;
    }
  }
  if(changed||pendingPeaceSettlements.length) _tryStartQueuedPeaceConferences();
}

function _makeCustomCiv(name,color,sourceCivId=-1){
  const id=civs.length;
  const src=civs[sourceCivId];
  const safeColor=/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(color||'')?color:'#4f9ecf';
  const civ={
    id,countryId:-1000-id,name:(name||('New Nation '+(id+1))).trim(),color:safeColor,
    terrAbgr:hexToAbgr(lighten(safeColor,.20)),borderAbgr:hexToAbgr(lighten(safeColor,.35)),
    res:civStartRes(1),ownedProvs:new Set(),territory:new LazyTerritorySet(),foxes:[],spawnTick:0,isPlayer:false,_moveMul:1,
    customNation:true,
    _cd:{base:safeColor,baseD:darken(safeColor,.22),helm:darken(safeColor,.38),helmD:darken(darken(safeColor,.38),.12),lite:lighten(safeColor,.50)}
  };
  const srcTechs=sourceCivId===playerCivId?playerTechs:src?.techs;
  civ.techs=new Set(srcTechs?[...srcTechs]:_buildTechSet(3,3));
  civs.push(civ);
  _recomputeBotTechBonus(civ);
  botPhase[id]=Math.floor(Math.random()*120);
  return civ;
}

function _restoreDynamicCivFromSave(cd){
  while(civs.length<=cd.id){
    const id=civs.length;
    const fallback={id,name:'Restored State '+(id+1),color:'#4f9ecf',countryId:-1000-id,customNation:true,botTechs:[]};
    const data=id===cd.id?cd:fallback;
    _makeCustomCiv(data.name,data.color,-1);
  }
  const civ=civs[cd.id];
  if(!civ) return null;
  if(cd.name) civ.name=cd.name;
  if(cd.color&&/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(cd.color)){
    civ.color=cd.color;civ.terrAbgr=hexToAbgr(lighten(cd.color,.20));civ.borderAbgr=hexToAbgr(lighten(cd.color,.35));
    civ._cd={base:cd.color,baseD:darken(cd.color,.22),helm:darken(cd.color,.38),helmD:darken(darken(cd.color,.38),.12),lite:lighten(cd.color,.50)};
  }
  civ.countryId=cd.countryId??civ.countryId;
  civ.tnoCanonicalName=cd.tnoCanonicalName||civ.tnoCanonicalName||'';
  civ.customNation=!!cd.customNation;
  return civ;
}

function _pcLiberatableOriginalNations(){
  const pc=peaceConference;
  if(!pc||!_originalMapOwnerIds||_originalMapOwnerIds.length!==provinces.length)return[];
  const groups=new Map();
  for(const pid of pc.eligible){
    if(!provinces[pid]||provinces[pid].isSea)continue;
    const originalId=_originalMapOwnerIds[pid];
    if(originalId<0||originalId===pc.defeatedId||!civs[originalId])continue;
    // "Inside the defeated nation" means the defeated state owned this province
    // when this war began; only land it STILL holds in the settlement is freeable.
    if(pc.originalOwners.get(pid)!==pc.defeatedId||provOwner[pid]!==pc.defeatedId)continue;
    let row=groups.get(originalId);
    if(!row){row={id:originalId,pids:[]};groups.set(originalId,row);}
    row.pids.push(pid);
  }
  return [...groups.values()].sort((a,b)=>{
    const d=b.pids.length-a.pids.length;
    return d||((civs[a.id]?.name||'').localeCompare(civs[b.id]?.name||''));
  });
}
function _pcFreeOriginalNation(originalId){
  const pc=peaceConference,civ=civs[originalId];
  if(!pc||!civ)return false;
  const row=_pcLiberatableOriginalNations().find(x=>x.id===originalId);
  if(!row||!row.pids.length)return false;
  _suppressWarContribution=true;
  try{
    for(const pid of row.pids){civClaimProvince(pid,originalId);pc.settled.add(pid);pc.selected.delete(pid);}
  }finally{_suppressWarContribution=false;}
  _pcLog(`FREED ${civ.name}: ${row.pids.length} original state${row.pids.length===1?'':'s'} restored`);
  _repatriatePeaceDivisions();
  renderPeaceConference();
  return true;
}
function _renderPeaceLiberationList(){
  const box=document.getElementById('pc-liberate-list');if(!box)return;
  box.innerHTML='';
  const rows=_pcLiberatableOriginalNations();
  if(!rows.length){
    const empty=document.createElement('div');empty.className='pc-liberate-empty';
    empty.textContent='No original-map nations are still occupied by this defeated country.';
    box.appendChild(empty);return;
  }
  for(const row of rows){
    const civ=civs[row.id];if(!civ)continue;
    const el=document.createElement('div');el.className='pc-liberate-row';
    const dot=document.createElement('span');dot.className='pc-liberate-dot';dot.style.background=civ.color||'#888';
    const info=document.createElement('div');info.style.minWidth='0';
    const name=document.createElement('div');name.className='pc-liberate-name';name.textContent=civ.name;
    const count=document.createElement('div');count.className='pc-liberate-count';count.textContent=`${row.pids.length} ORIGINAL STATE${row.pids.length===1?'':'S'} OCCUPIED`;
    info.append(name,count);
    const btn=document.createElement('button');btn.type='button';btn.className='pc-liberate-btn';btn.textContent='FREE';
    btn.title=`Restore ${row.pids.length} starting-map state${row.pids.length===1?'':'s'} to ${civ.name}`;
    btn.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.stopPropagation();_pcFreeOriginalNation(row.id);});
    el.append(dot,info,btn);box.appendChild(el);
  }
}

function _refreshPeaceRecipientOptions(preferredId=null){
  const pc=peaceConference;
  const rec=document.getElementById('pc-recipient');
  if(!rec||!pc)return;
  const current=(_peaceRecipientStickyId>=0)?_peaceRecipientStickyId:(preferredId!=null?+preferredId:parseInt(rec.value));
  rec.innerHTML='';
  const seen=new Set();
  let firstValue=null;

  const addGroup=(label,ids,decorate)=>{
    const clean=[];
    for(const rawId of ids){
      const id=+rawId,c=civs[id];
      if(!c||seen.has(id))continue;
      seen.add(id);clean.push(id);
    }
    if(!clean.length)return;
    const group=document.createElement('optgroup');group.label=label;
    for(const id of clean){
      const c=civs[id];
      const opt=document.createElement('option');opt.value=String(id);
      const land=c.ownedProvs?.size||0;
      opt.textContent=decorate?decorate(c,id,land):`${c.name}${land?'': ' · no land'}`;
      group.appendChild(opt);
      if(firstValue==null)firstValue=id;
    }
    rec.appendChild(group);
  };

  if(playerCivId>=0&&civs[playerCivId]) addGroup('YOU',[playerCivId],c=>`${c.name} (YOU)`);
  const currentStates=civs.filter(c=>c&&c.id!==playerCivId&&!c.customNation).sort((a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name)).map(c=>c.id);
  const newStates=civs.filter(c=>c&&c.id!==playerCivId&&c.customNation).sort((a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name)).map(c=>c.id);
  addGroup('CURRENT STATES',currentStates,(c,id,land)=>`${c.name}${id===pc.defeatedId?' (defeated)':''}${land?'':' · no land'}`);
  addGroup('NEW STATES (CREATED)',newStates,(c,id,land)=>`${c.name}${land?'':' · no land'}`);

  const desired=Number.isInteger(current)&&seen.has(current)?current:firstValue;
  if(desired!=null){rec.value=String(desired);if(_peaceRecipientStickyId<0)_peaceRecipientStickyId=desired;}
}
function startPeaceConference(settlementOrDefeatedId,victors=[]){
  AudioEngine.peace();
  let settlement;
  if(settlementOrDefeatedId&&typeof settlementOrDefeatedId==='object') settlement=settlementOrDefeatedId;
  else settlement=_makePeaceSettlementSnapshot(+settlementOrDefeatedId,victors);
  const defeatedId=settlement.defeatedId;
  const defeated=civs[defeatedId]; if(!defeated) return;
  const eligible=[...(settlement.eligible||[])].filter(pid=>provinces[pid]&&!provinces[pid].isSea);
  if(!eligible.length){
    _peaceBatchCompleted++;
    setTimeout(_tryStartQueuedPeaceConferences,0);
    return;
  }
  const originalOwners=new Map(Object.entries(settlement.originalOwners||{}).map(([pid,owner])=>[+pid,+owner]));
  setGameSpeed(0);
  selectedDivs.clear();updateMilBar();closePanel();

  // Only NOW, after all player wars are finished, restore this country's exact pre-war borders.
  // There is deliberately no occupation layer in the settlement UI.
  _suppressWarContribution=true;
  try{
    for(const pid of eligible){
      const owner=originalOwners.get(pid);
      if(owner!=null&&owner>=0&&civs[owner]) civClaimProvince(pid,owner);
    }
  } finally {_suppressWarContribution=false;}

  peaceConference={
    id:_peaceConferenceSerial++,
    defeatedId,
    victors:[...new Set(settlement.victors||[])],
    eligible:new Set(eligible),
    settled:new Set(),
    selected:new Set(),
    originalOwners,
    log:[]
  };
  document.body.classList.add('pc-active');
  const root=document.getElementById('peaceConference');root.classList.add('active');root.setAttribute('aria-hidden','false');
  _refreshPeaceRecipientOptions(_peaceRecipientStickyId>=0?_peaceRecipientStickyId:playerCivId);
  document.getElementById('pc-new-name').value='';
  document.getElementById('pc-state-search').value='';
  peaceBrush=1;_peaceHoverPid=-1;
  const pbs=document.getElementById('pc-brush-slider');if(pbs)pbs.value='1';
  const pbv=document.getElementById('pc-brush-val');if(pbv)pbv.textContent='1';
  renderPeaceConference();
  showWarNotification(`${defeated.name} · choose who gets its land`,civs[playerCivId]?.color||'#f0a030');
}

function _pcSelected(){
  if(!peaceConference)return[];
  // Assigned land can be selected again so mistakes are easy to fix/reassign.
  return [...peaceConference.selected].filter(pid=>peaceConference.eligible.has(pid));
}
function _pcLog(msg){if(!peaceConference)return;peaceConference.log.unshift(msg);peaceConference.log=peaceConference.log.slice(0,5);}

function _pcTransferSelected(recipientId,label='Given'){
  AudioEngine.peaceAction();
  const pids=_pcSelected();if(!pids.length||!civs[recipientId])return false;
  for(const pid of pids){civClaimProvince(pid,recipientId);peaceConference.settled.add(pid);}
  peaceConference.selected.clear();
  _pcLog(`${pids.length} state${pids.length!==1?'s':''} → ${civs[recipientId].name}`);
  renderPeaceConference();return true;
}

function _pcRelease(){
  AudioEngine.peaceAction();
  if(!peaceConference)return;
  const pids=_pcSelected();if(!pids.length)return;
  const name=document.getElementById('pc-new-name').value.trim()||(`Free ${provinces[pids[0]]?.name||'State'}`);
  const color=document.getElementById('pc-new-color').value||'#4f9ecf';
  const n=_makeCustomCiv(name,color,peaceConference.defeatedId);
  n.res=civStartRes(pids.length);
  for(const pid of pids){civClaimProvince(pid,n.id);peaceConference.settled.add(pid);}
  peaceConference.selected.clear();
  _pcLog(`${pids.length} state${pids.length!==1?'s':''} → NEW STATE: ${n.name}`);
  _civLabelDirty=true;_allianceLabelDirty=true;_allianceBorderDirty=true;_alOverlayDirty=true;
  AudioEngine.revolt();
  _refreshPeaceRecipientOptions(_peaceRecipientStickyId);
  renderPeaceConference();
}

function _pcTakeAllResources(){
  AudioEngine.peaceAction();
  const pc=peaceConference,defeated=pc?civs[pc.defeatedId]:null,player=civs[playerCivId];if(!pc||!defeated||!player)return;
  let total=0;for(const k of ['wood','stone','food','coins']){const v=Math.max(0,+defeated.res[k]||0);player.res[k]=(player.res[k]||0)+v;defeated.res[k]=0;total+=v;}
  _pcLog(total?`ALL RESOURCES seized from ${defeated.name}`:`${defeated.name} has no resources left`);renderPeaceConference();
}
function _pcForceLeaveAlliance(){
  AudioEngine.peaceAction();
  const pc=peaceConference,defeated=pc?civs[pc.defeatedId]:null;if(!pc||!defeated)return;const al=getAllianceOfCiv(defeated.id);
  if(!al){_pcLog(`${defeated.name} is already outside any alliance`);renderPeaceConference();return;}
  const oldName=al.name;_removeCivFromAlliance(defeated.id);_pcLog(`${defeated.name} forced out of ${oldName}`);renderPeaceConference();
}
function _pcForceJoinMyAlliance(){
  AudioEngine.peaceAction();
  const pc=peaceConference,defeated=pc?civs[pc.defeatedId]:null,playerAl=getAllianceOfCiv(playerCivId);if(!pc||!defeated)return;
  if(!playerAl){_pcLog('You are not currently in an alliance');renderPeaceConference();return;}
  const cur=getAllianceOfCiv(defeated.id);if(cur===playerAl){_pcLog(`${defeated.name} is already in ${playerAl.name}`);renderPeaceConference();return;}
  if(cur)_removeCivFromAlliance(defeated.id);
  // Peace terms override ordinary invitation/leader restrictions by design.
  playerAl.memberIds.add(defeated.id);_touchAllianceMembership();
  _allianceLabelDirty=true;_allianceBorderDirty=true;_alOverlayDirty=true;
  pc.forceJoinAllianceId=playerAl.id;_pcLog(`${defeated.name} forced to join ${playerAl.name}`);renderPeaceConference();
}

function _hidePeaceConference(){
  document.body.classList.remove('pc-active');
  const root=document.getElementById('peaceConference');
  root.classList.remove('active');root.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
  // Tear down the transient treaty compositor explicitly. Mobile browsers can otherwise
  // retain its final cities/border bitmap as a frozen promoted layer until another resize.
  _peaceHoverPid=-1;
  if(typeof _resetLayerTransform==='function'){_resetLayerTransform(mapCanvas);_resetLayerTransform(labelCanvas);_resetLayerTransform(fxCanvas);}
  else{mapCanvas.style.transform='';labelCanvas.style.transform='';fxCanvas.style.transform='';}
  // Clear every transient/promoted overlay. Mobile Chromium/WebKit can preserve the last
  // composited bitmap even after the treaty DOM closes unless the backing canvas changes.
  mapCtx.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);mapCtx.clearRect(0,0,mapCanvas.width,mapCanvas.height);
  labelCtx.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);labelCtx.clearRect(0,0,labelCanvas.width,labelCanvas.height);
  fxCtx.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);fxCtx.clearRect(0,0,fxCanvas.width,fxCanvas.height);
  _fxHadContent=false;_labelLayerValid=false;_labelLayerKey='';_labelLayerContentKey='';
  _labelLayerCamX=_labelLayerCamY=_labelLayerZoom=NaN;_lastExactLabelCameraRender=0;
  _mapLayerKey='';_mapLayerViewKey='';_mapLayerContentKey='';
  _mapLayerCamX=_mapLayerCamY=_mapLayerZoom=NaN;
  _baseLayerValid=false;_mapLayerValid=false;
}

function _pcFinish(){
  const pc=peaceConference;if(!pc)return;
  const defeated=civs[pc.defeatedId];
  const unassigned=[...pc.eligible].filter(pid=>!pc.settled.has(pid)).length;
  if(defeated&&defeated.ownedProvs.size===0) _removeCivFromAlliance(defeated.id);
  _repatriatePeaceDivisions();
  _hidePeaceConference();
  peaceConference=null;
  _peaceBatchCompleted++;
  _terrBorderDirty=true;_civLabelDirty=true;_allianceLabelDirty=true;_allianceBorderDirty=true;_alOverlayDirty=true;

  if(pendingPeaceSettlements.length){
    showWarNotification(`Settlement saved${unassigned?` · ${unassigned} unassigned state${unassigned===1?'':'s'} stayed on original borders`:''} · next country`,'#e0b050');
    setTimeout(_tryStartQueuedPeaceConferences,0);
  }else{
    const done=_peaceBatchCompleted;
    showWarNotification(`Peace complete · ${done} defeated countr${done===1?'y':'ies'} settled`,'#e0b050');
    _peaceBatchTotal=0;_peaceBatchCompleted=0;
    setGameSpeed(_peacePrevSpeed||1);
    if(playerAutoMoveUniversal)setTimeout(_refreshUniversalAutoMovePlan,0);
    setTimeout(checkWarConclusions,0);
  }
}

function _provinceBrush(pid,size,allowedFn=null){
  if(pid<0||!provinces[pid]||provinces[pid].isSea)return[];
  const max=Math.max(1,Math.min(5,+size||1));
  const visited=new Set([pid]);let frontier=[pid];
  for(let depth=1;depth<max;depth++){
    const next=[];
    for(const cur of frontier){
      for(let _ai=_adjBegin(cur),_ae=_adjEnd(cur);_ai<_ae;_ai++){const n=_adjList[_ai];
        if(visited.has(n)||!provinces[n]||provinces[n].isSea)continue;
        visited.add(n);next.push(n);
      }
    }
    if(!next.length)break;frontier=next;
  }
  const out=[...visited];
  return allowedFn?out.filter(allowedFn):out;
}
function _peaceBrushProvs(pid){
  const pc=peaceConference;if(!pc)return[];
  return _provinceBrush(pid,peaceBrush,p=>pc.eligible.has(p));
}
function _pcAnnexAllAndNext(){
  AudioEngine.peaceAction();
  const pc=peaceConference;if(!pc||playerCivId<0||!civs[playerCivId])return;
  AudioEngine.annex();
  for(const pid of pc.eligible){civClaimProvince(pid,playerCivId);pc.settled.add(pid);}
  pc.selected.clear();_pcLog(`ALL ${pc.eligible.size} states → ${civs[playerCivId].name}`);
  _pcFinish();
}
function _pcAnnexAllWarsAndEnd(){
  AudioEngine.peaceAction();
  const pc=peaceConference;if(!pc||playerCivId<0||!civs[playerCivId])return;
  AudioEngine.annex();
  const all=[{defeatedId:pc.defeatedId,eligible:[...pc.eligible]},...pendingPeaceSettlements.map(x=>({defeatedId:x.defeatedId,eligible:[...(x.eligible||[])]}))];
  _suppressWarContribution=true;
  try{
    for(const row of all){
      for(const pid of row.eligible)if(provinces[pid]&&!provinces[pid].isSea)civClaimProvince(pid,playerCivId);
      const d=civs[row.defeatedId];if(d&&d.ownedProvs.size===0)_removeCivFromAlliance(d.id);
    }
  }finally{_suppressWarContribution=false;}
  pendingPeaceSettlements=[];
  _repatriatePeaceDivisions();
  _hidePeaceConference();peaceConference=null;_peaceHoverPid=-1;
  const count=all.length;_peaceBatchCompleted+=count;
  _terrBorderDirty=true;_civLabelDirty=true;_allianceLabelDirty=true;_allianceBorderDirty=true;_alOverlayDirty=true;
  showWarNotification(`Peace complete · annexed all land from ${count} defeated countr${count===1?'y':'ies'}`,civs[playerCivId].color);
  _peaceBatchTotal=0;_peaceBatchCompleted=0;setGameSpeed(_peacePrevSpeed||1);if(playerAutoMoveUniversal)setTimeout(_refreshUniversalAutoMovePlan,0);setTimeout(checkWarConclusions,0);
}

function renderPeaceConference(){
  const pc=peaceConference;if(!pc)return;
  const defeated=civs[pc.defeatedId];
  const batchIndex=Math.min(_peaceBatchTotal,_peaceBatchCompleted+1);
  const remainingCountries=pendingPeaceSettlements.length;
  const selectedCount=_pcSelected().length;
  const unassigned=[...pc.eligible].filter(pid=>!pc.settled.has(pid)).length;

  document.getElementById('pc-title').textContent=`${defeated?.name||'Defeated Country'} — Land Settlement`;
  document.getElementById('pc-sub').textContent=`Country ${batchIndex} of ${Math.max(1,_peaceBatchTotal)} · ${selectedCount} selected · ${unassigned} not assigned yet. Unassigned land simply stays on the original border.`;
  document.getElementById('pc-banner').textContent=`ALL YOUR WARS ARE FINISHED · SETTLEMENT ${batchIndex}/${Math.max(1,_peaceBatchTotal)} · choose who gets the land`;

  _renderPeaceLiberationList();

  const q=(document.getElementById('pc-state-search').value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  const list=document.getElementById('pc-states');list.innerHTML='';
  const rows=[...pc.eligible].sort((a,b)=>{
    const sa=pc.settled.has(a),sb=pc.settled.has(b);if(sa!==sb)return sa?1:-1;return (provinces[a]?.name||'').localeCompare(provinces[b]?.name||'');
  });
  for(const pid of rows){
    const p=provinces[pid];if(!p)continue;
    const owner=provOwner[pid]>=0?civs[provOwner[pid]]:null;
    const hay=`${p.name} ${owner?.name||''}`.toLowerCase();if(q&&!hay.includes(q))continue;
    const assigned=pc.settled.has(pid),selected=pc.selected.has(pid);
    const row=document.createElement('div');row.className='pc-state'+(selected?' selected':'')+(assigned?' settled':'');row.dataset.pid=pid;
    row.innerHTML=`<div class="pc-check">${selected?'✓':assigned?'•':''}</div><div><div class="pc-state-name">${p.name}</div><div class="pc-state-meta">${assigned?`GIVEN TO: ${owner?.name||'Unclaimed'}`:`ORIGINAL: ${owner?.name||'Unclaimed'} · not assigned`}</div></div>`;
    row.addEventListener('click',()=>{if(pc.selected.has(pid))pc.selected.delete(pid);else pc.selected.add(pid);renderPeaceConference();});
    list.appendChild(row);
  }

  const recipientId=parseInt(document.getElementById('pc-recipient').value);
  const recipient=civs[recipientId];
  const give=document.getElementById('pc-annex');
  give.disabled=!selectedCount||!recipient;
  give.textContent=selectedCount&&recipient?`GIVE ${selectedCount} → ${recipient.name.toUpperCase()}`:'GIVE SELECTED LAND';
  document.getElementById('pc-release').disabled=!selectedCount;

  const takeBtn=document.getElementById('pc-take-resources'),leaveBtn=document.getElementById('pc-force-leave'),joinBtn=document.getElementById('pc-force-join');
  const defeatedAlliance=defeated?getAllianceOfCiv(defeated.id):null,playerAlliance=getAllianceOfCiv(playerCivId);
  const resourceTotal=defeated?['wood','stone','food','coins'].reduce((n,k)=>n+(+defeated.res[k]||0),0):0;
  if(takeBtn){takeBtn.disabled=resourceTotal<=0;takeBtn.textContent=resourceTotal>0?'TAKE ALL RESOURCES':'RESOURCES TAKEN';}
  if(leaveBtn){leaveBtn.disabled=!defeatedAlliance;leaveBtn.textContent=defeatedAlliance?`FORCE LEAVE ${defeatedAlliance.name.toUpperCase()}`:'NO ALLIANCE TO LEAVE';}
  if(joinBtn){joinBtn.disabled=!playerAlliance||defeatedAlliance===playerAlliance;joinBtn.textContent=!playerAlliance?'YOU HAVE NO ALLIANCE':(defeatedAlliance===playerAlliance?`IN ${playerAlliance.name.toUpperCase()}`:`FORCE JOIN ${playerAlliance.name.toUpperCase()}`);}

  const log=document.getElementById('pc-log');
  log.innerHTML=pc.log.map(x=>`• ${x}`).join('<br>');log.classList.toggle('has',pc.log.length>0);
  const finish=document.getElementById('pc-finish');
  finish.textContent=remainingCountries?`✓ DONE · NEXT COUNTRY (${remainingCountries} LEFT)`:'✓ DONE · FINISH PEACE';
}

function peaceToggleProvince(pid){
  const pc=peaceConference;if(!pc||!pc.eligible.has(pid))return;
  const brush=_peaceBrushProvs(pid);if(!brush.length)return;
  const allSelected=brush.every(p=>pc.selected.has(p));
  for(const p of brush){if(allSelected)pc.selected.delete(p);else pc.selected.add(p);}
  AudioEngine.paintStroke();
  renderPeaceConference();
}

function drawPeaceConferenceOverlay(){
  const pc=peaceConference;if(!pc)return;
  const tpx=TS*zoom,sw=canvas.width,sh=canvas.height;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,190,35,.28)';
  for(const pid of pc.selected){
    const p=provinces[pid];if(!p)continue;
