    for(const ti of p.tiles){const tx2=ti%WORLD_W,ty2=(ti/WORLD_W)|0;const sx=(tx2-camX)*tpx,sy=(ty2-camY)*tpx;if(sx<-tpx||sy<-tpx||sx>sw+tpx||sy>sh+tpx)continue;ctx.fillRect(sx,sy,tpx,tpx);}
  }
  const hover=_peaceBrushProvs(_peaceHoverPid);
  if(hover.length){
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.14)';
    for(const pid of hover){const p=provinces[pid];if(!p)continue;for(const ti of p.tiles){const tx2=ti%WORLD_W,ty2=(ti/WORLD_W)|0;const sx=(tx2-camX)*tpx,sy=(ty2-camY)*tpx;if(sx<-tpx||sy<-tpx||sx>sw+tpx||sy>sh+tpx)continue;ctx.fillRect(sx,sy,tpx,tpx);}}
  }
  for(const pid of pc.eligible){
    let segs=_provOutlineCache.get(pid);if(!segs)segs=_buildProvOutline(pid);const path=new Path2D();
    for(let i=0;i<segs.length;i+=4){const x1=(segs[i]-camX)*tpx,y1=(segs[i+1]-camY)*tpx,x2=(segs[i+2]-camX)*tpx,y2=(segs[i+3]-camY)*tpx;if((x1<-3&&x2<-3)||(x1>sw+3&&x2>sw+3)||(y1<-3&&y2<-3)||(y1>sh+3&&y2>sh+3))continue;path.moveTo(x1,y1);path.lineTo(x2,y2);}
    ctx.strokeStyle=pc.selected.has(pid)?'#ffe170':(pc.settled.has(pid)?'rgba(120,210,120,.7)':'rgba(240,160,40,.65)');
    ctx.lineWidth=pc.selected.has(pid)?2.6:1.25;ctx.stroke(path);
  }
  ctx.restore();
}

function tickTreatyEconomy(){ /* intentionally empty: simple peace has no treaty economy */ }

// ── WAR NOTIFICATION BANNER ───────────────────────────────────────
let _warNoteTimer=0;
let _warNoteMsg='', _warNoteColor='#e04040',_fxHadContent=false;
function showWarNotification(msg,color){
  _warNoteMsg=msg; _warNoteColor=color||'#e04040'; _warNoteTimer=240;
}
function drawWarNotification(){
  if(_warNoteTimer<=0) return;
  _warNoteTimer--;
  const alpha=Math.min(1,_warNoteTimer/30);
  const cx=canvas.width/2, y=68;
  const fs=15;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha=alpha;
  ctx.font=`700 ${fs}px 'Cinzel',serif`;
  ctx.textAlign='center'; ctx.textBaseline='middle';
  const tw=ctx.measureText(_warNoteMsg).width;
  const bw=tw+28, bh=30;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(cx-bw/2,y-bh/2,bw,bh,6);
  ctx.fillStyle='rgba(8,2,2,0.92)'; ctx.fill();
  ctx.strokeStyle=_warNoteColor; ctx.lineWidth=1.5; ctx.stroke();
  ctx.fillStyle=_warNoteColor;
  ctx.fillText('⚔ '+_warNoteMsg,cx,y);
  ctx.restore();
}

// ── COMBAT TICK ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Returns all divisions of civId in province pid
// Province→divisions spatial index — rebuilt once per tickCombat call.
// Maps pid → Map<civId, div[]>. Gives O(1) lookups for allDivsInProv.
const _provDivIndex = new Map(); // pid → Map<civId, div[]>
const _contestedProvIds = new Set(); // only provinces currently containing 2+ civs
let   _provDivIndexDirty = true; // true only after a bulk/legacy mutation
const _provBotSlotCache=new Map(),_provPlayerSlotCache=new Map();
const _PLAYER_SLOT_CACHE_MAX=isMobile?2048:4096;
let _playerSlotCacheCivId=-999;
function _invalidateProvBotSlot(pid){if(pid>=0){_provBotSlotCache.delete(pid);_provPlayerSlotCache.delete(pid);}}


// Incremental province→division index. Each division stores its exact bucket slot so
// moving/spawning/dying is O(1) (swap-remove) rather than forcing an all-armies scan.
function _indexAddDiv(div){
  if(_provDivIndexDirty||!div)return;
  const pid=div.provId,cid=div.civId;_invalidateProvBotSlot(pid);
  let civMap=_provDivIndex.get(pid);const wasEmpty=!civMap;if(!civMap){civMap=new Map();_provDivIndex.set(pid,civMap);}
  let arr=civMap.get(cid);if(!arr){arr=[];civMap.set(cid,arr);_invalidateSafeIncoming(pid,cid);}
  div._pdiPid=pid;div._pdiCiv=cid;div._pdiPos=arr.length;arr.push(div);
  if(wasEmpty&&typeof _occupiedRenderAdd==='function')_occupiedRenderAdd(pid);
  if(civMap.size>1)_contestedProvIds.add(pid);
}
function _indexRemoveDiv(div){
  if(_provDivIndexDirty||!div)return;
  const pid=div._pdiPid,cid=div._pdiCiv;_invalidateProvBotSlot(pid);
  const civMap=_provDivIndex.get(pid),arr=civMap?.get(cid);
  if(!arr){_provDivIndexDirty=true;return;}
  let pos=div._pdiPos;
  if(pos<0||pos>=arr.length||arr[pos]!==div){pos=arr.indexOf(div);if(pos<0){_provDivIndexDirty=true;return;}}
  const last=arr[arr.length-1];arr.pop();
  if(pos<arr.length){arr[pos]=last;last._pdiPos=pos;}
  if(!arr.length){civMap.delete(cid);_wakeBlockedIncoming(pid);if(!civMap.size){_provDivIndex.delete(pid);if(typeof _occupiedRenderRemove==='function')_occupiedRenderRemove(pid);}}
  if(!civMap||civMap.size<2)_contestedProvIds.delete(pid);else _contestedProvIds.add(pid);
  div._pdiPid=-1;div._pdiCiv=-1;div._pdiPos=-1;
}
function _indexMoveDiv(div,oldPid,newPid){
  if(_provDivIndexDirty||oldPid===newPid)return;
  
  _indexRemoveDiv(div);if(!_provDivIndexDirty)_indexAddDiv(div);
  
  
}
function _indexClearCivDivs(civ){
  if(!civ)return;
  for(const d of civ.foxes){_removeDivActivity(d);if(!_provDivIndexDirty)_indexRemoveDiv(d);}
  _clearSafeSchedule(civ);civ.foxes.length=0;if(civ._activeDivs){for(const d of civ._activeDivs)if(d){d._simActive=false;d._activePos=-1;}civ._activeDivs.length=0;civ._activeDivCount=0;civ._activeTombs=0;}civ._botGroupDirty=false;
}
function _rebuildProvDivIndex(){
  
  _provDivIndex.clear();_contestedProvIds.clear();_provBotSlotCache.clear();_provPlayerSlotCache.clear();
  if(typeof _markOccupiedRenderBucketsDirty==='function')_markOccupiedRenderBucketsDirty();
  _provDivIndexDirty=false;
  for(const civ of civs){
    for(const div of civ.foxes){
      let civMap=_provDivIndex.get(div.provId);if(!civMap){civMap=new Map();_provDivIndex.set(div.provId,civMap);}
      let arr=civMap.get(civ.id);if(!arr){arr=[];civMap.set(civ.id,arr);}
      div._pdiPid=div.provId;div._pdiCiv=civ.id;div._pdiPos=arr.length;arr.push(div);
      if(civMap.size>1)_contestedProvIds.add(div.provId);
    }
  }
}

function allDivsInProv(pid, civId){
  if(_provDivIndexDirty) _rebuildProvDivIndex();
  return _provDivIndex.get(pid)?.get(civId) || [];
}

// All divisions in a province across all civs (used in tickCombat Scenario 2)
function allDivsInProvAllCivs(pid){
  if(_provDivIndexDirty) _rebuildProvDivIndex();
  return _provDivIndex.get(pid); // returns Map<civId,div[]> or undefined
}

const _combatOpponentHint=new Map();
const _combatAttackGroups=new Map();
const _combatAttackPool=[];
const _combatReadyPool=[];
const _combatDeadSet=new Set();
const _combatDeadCivs=new Set();
const _combatPairScratch=[];

let _combatPoolCursor=0;
function _combatScratch(){let a=_combatReadyPool[_combatPoolCursor];if(!a)a=_combatReadyPool[_combatPoolCursor]=[];_combatPoolCursor++;a.length=0;return a;}
function _combatReady(src){const out=_combatScratch();for(const d of src)if(d.hp>0&&d.combatCooldown===0)out.push(d);return out;}
function tickCombat(){
  // Peace is overwhelmingly the common case. Exit before touching combat-only scratch
  // structures or resolving bot difficulty; the next real combat tick resets all scratch
  // state before use, so clearing it every peaceful simulation tick was pure overhead.
  if(!gameReady)return;
  if(warPairs.size===0){_warConclusionCandidates.clear();return;}
  _combatOpponentHint.clear();_combatPoolCursor=0;_combatDeadSet.clear();_combatDeadCivs.clear();
  // Difficulty is constant for the whole combat tick; reuse the already cached profile
  // instead of re-entering the lookup for every attack/defence damage calculation.
  const combatDiff=_botDiff();
  if(_provDivIndexDirty) _rebuildProvDivIndex();

  function combinedDmg(divs){
    let base=0;for(let i=0;i<divs.length;i++){const d=divs[i];base+=Math.round((1.6+Math.random()*1.2)*d.size);}
    const rep=divs[0];if(!rep)return base;
    const enemyId=_combatOpponentHint.get(rep.civId),warAtk=enemyId==null?1:_warSideModifier(rep.civId,enemyId,'attack');
    if(rep.civId===playerCivId&&techBonus.atkMul)return Math.round(base*(1+techBonus.atkMul)*warAtk);
    const botB=civs[rep.civId]?.techBonus,diffAtk=combatDiff.atk||1;
    if(botB&&botB.atkMul)return Math.round(base*(1+botB.atkMul)*diffAtk*warAtk);
    return Math.round(base*diffAtk*warAtk);
  }
  function applyDmgToGroup(dmg,defenders,fortMul,attackerId=-1){
    // Sort only the READY combatants, never copy/sort the full province bucket.
    defenders.sort((a,b)=>(a.hp/a.maxHp)-(b.hp/b.maxHp));
    const rep=defenders[0],isPlayerDef=rep&&rep.civId===playerCivId,botB=(!isPlayerDef&&rep)?civs[rep.civId]?.techBonus:null;
    const warDef=(rep&&attackerId>=0)?_warSideModifier(rep.civId,attackerId,'defense'):1;
    const diffDef=combatDiff.def||1,effFortMul=isPlayerDef?fortMul*(1+(techBonus.defMul||0))*warDef:botB?fortMul*(1+(botB.defMul||0))*diffDef*warDef:fortMul*diffDef*warDef;
    const playerReduce=Math.min(.75,techBonus.dmgReduce||0),botReduce=botB?Math.min(.75,botB.dmgReduce||0):0;
    const effDmg=isPlayerDef?dmg*(1-playerReduce):botB?dmg*(1-botReduce):dmg;let rem=effDmg;
    for(let i=0;i<defenders.length&&rem>0;i++){
      const def=defenders[i],take=Math.min(def.hp,rem/effFortMul);def.hp-=take;
      if(take>0){_syncDamagedDiv(def);if(attackerId>=0)def._lastDamager=attackerId;if(def.hp<=0)_combatDeadSet.add(def);}
      rem-=take*effFortMul;
    }
  }
  function resolvePair(attackCid,attackers,defCid,defenders,targetPid,fortMul,cooldown){
    const aReady=_combatReady(attackers),dReady=_combatReady(defenders);if(!aReady.length||!dReady.length)return false;
    _combatOpponentHint.set(attackCid,defCid);_combatOpponentHint.set(defCid,attackCid);
    const aDmg=combinedDmg(aReady),dDmg=combinedDmg(dReady);applyDmgToGroup(aDmg,dReady,fortMul,attackCid);applyDmgToGroup(dDmg,aReady,1,defCid);
    for(const d of aReady){d._combatOpponent=defCid;d.combatCooldown=cooldown;}for(const d of dReady){d._combatOpponent=attackCid;d.combatCooldown=cooldown;}
    const pp=provinces[targetPid];if(pp){burst(tcx(pp.centerTx),tcy(pp.centerTy),pp.isSea?'#40a0ff':'#ff6020');AudioEngine.battle(pp.centerTx,pp.centerTy,!!pp.isSea);}return true;
  }

  // LAND CONTACT: process only event-driven parked attackers. The previous implementation
  // rescanned every division in every wartime country on every simulation tick.
  for(const [targetPid,incoming] of _blockedIncomingByProv){
    if(!incoming?.size)continue;const byCiv=_provDivIndex.get(targetPid);if(!byCiv||!byCiv.size)continue;
    _combatAttackGroups.clear();let poolUsed=0;
    for(const d of incoming){
      if(!d||d.hp<=0||!d._blockedParked||d._blockedDest!==targetPid)continue;
      let a=_combatAttackGroups.get(d.civId);if(!a){a=_combatAttackPool[poolUsed]||(_combatAttackPool[poolUsed]=[]);poolUsed++;a.length=0;_combatAttackGroups.set(d.civId,a);}a.push(d);
    }
    if(!_combatAttackGroups.size)continue;
    const fortMul=1+(provinces[targetPid]?.bld?.fort||0)/6;
    for(const [attackCid,attackers] of _combatAttackGroups){
      // Rotate the first defender by combat cycle so a unit surrounded from north/south/etc.
      // cannot permanently starve one hostile side just because Map insertion order is stable.
      const defs=_combatScratch();for(const [defCid,defenders] of byCiv)if(defCid!==attackCid&&defenders.length&&areAtWar(attackCid,defCid))defs.push(defCid,defenders);
      const n=defs.length>>1;if(!n)continue;const start=((Math.floor(_simTick/30)+attackCid+targetPid)%n+n)%n;
      for(let k=0;k<n;k++){const q=((start+k)%n)<<1;if(resolvePair(attackCid,attackers,defs[q],defs[q+1],targetPid,fortMul,30))break;}
    }
  }

  // SAME-PROVINCE CONTACT (including sea): contested set is maintained incrementally.
  for(const pid of _contestedProvIds){
    const byCiv=_provDivIndex.get(pid);if(!byCiv||byCiv.size<2)continue;
    const civIds=_combatScratch();for(const cid of byCiv.keys())civIds.push(cid);
    const cooldown=provinces[pid]?.isSea?35:30;
    _combatPairScratch.length=0;
    for(let i=0;i<civIds.length;i++)for(let j=i+1;j<civIds.length;j++){const a=civIds[i],b=civIds[j];if(areAtWar(a,b))_combatPairScratch.push(a,b);}
    const pairN=_combatPairScratch.length>>1;if(!pairN)continue;
    // Pair order rotates every cooldown window. This guarantees multi-direction/multi-side
    // engagements all receive combat turns instead of the first pair monopolising cooldowns.
    const start=((Math.floor(_simTick/cooldown)+pid)%pairN+pairN)%pairN;
    for(let k=0;k<pairN;k++){const q=((start+k)%pairN)<<1,a=_combatPairScratch[q],b=_combatPairScratch[q+1];resolvePair(a,byCiv.get(a),b,byCiv.get(b),pid,1,cooldown);}
  }

  // Remove only divisions that actually died this tick. No all-armies death scan.
  for(const d of _combatDeadSet){
    if(!d||d.hp>0)continue;const civ=civs[d.civId];if(!civ)continue;_combatDeadCivs.add(civ.id);
    _untrackDamagedDiv(d);_removeDivActivity(d);_indexRemoveDiv(d);if(d._lastDamager>=0)_recordWarContribution(d._lastDamager,civ.id,null,12);selectedDivs.delete(d.id);
    const pid=d.provId;
    const lp=provinces[pid];if(lp){if(lp.isSea)AudioEngine.navalSink(lp.centerTx,lp.centerTy);else AudioEngine.unitLoss(lp.centerTx,lp.centerTy,d.civId===playerCivId);}
    if(!provinces[pid]?.isSea&&provOwner[pid]===civ.id){const byCiv=_provDivIndex.get(pid);if(byCiv)for(const [otherId,arr] of byCiv){if(otherId!==civ.id&&arr.length&&areAtWar(civ.id,otherId)){civClaimProvince(pid,otherId);break;}}}
  }
  for(const cid of _combatDeadCivs){const civ=civs[cid];if(!civ)continue;let w=0;for(let r=0;r<civ.foxes.length;r++){const d=civ.foxes[r];if(d.hp>0)civ.foxes[w++]=d;}civ.foxes.length=w;}

  if(_warConclusionCandidates.size){_warConclusionScratch.length=0;for(const id of _warConclusionCandidates)_warConclusionScratch.push(id);_warConclusionCandidates.clear();checkWarConclusions(_warConclusionScratch);}
}

// ── BOT MILITARY AI ───────────────────────────────────────────────
const BOT_DIFFICULTY_PROFILES=Object.freeze({
  easy:  {cap:1.00,spawn:1.00,think:1.00,war:1.00,atk:1.00,def:1.00},
  normal:{cap:1.25,spawn:0.82,think:0.82,war:1.80,atk:1.07,def:1.04},
  hard:  {cap:1.55,spawn:0.66,think:0.66,war:3.00,atk:1.15,def:1.08},
  brutal:{cap:1.90,spawn:0.52,think:0.52,war:4.80,atk:1.25,def:1.13}
});
let botDifficulty='easy';
let _botDiffCacheKey='',_botDiffCache=BOT_DIFFICULTY_PROFILES.easy;
function _botDiff(){if(_botDiffCacheKey!==botDifficulty){_botDiffCacheKey=botDifficulty;_botDiffCache=BOT_DIFFICULTY_PROFILES[botDifficulty]||BOT_DIFFICULTY_PROFILES.easy;}return _botDiffCache;}
function botDivCap(civ){const own=civ.ownedProvs.size,key=botDifficulty;if(civ._botCapOwn===own&&civ._botCapDiff===key)return civ._botCapCache;const f=_botDiff().cap,cap=Math.max(1,Math.min(Math.round(12*f),Math.ceil((3+Math.floor(own/4))*f)));civ._botCapOwn=own;civ._botCapDiff=key;civ._botCapCache=cap;return cap;}

function _botOwnRouteComponents(civ){
  const routeVersion=_routeLegalStamp(civ.id),territoryVersion=civ._territoryVersion||1;
  if(civ._routeOwnCompRouteVersion===routeVersion&&civ._routeOwnCompTerritoryVersion===territoryVersion&&civ._routeOwnCompSet)return civ._routeOwnCompSet;
  const comps=civ._routeOwnCompSet||new Set();comps.clear();
  for(const pid of civ.ownedProvs){const c=_routeComponentId(pid,civ.id,true);if(c>=0)comps.add(c);}
  civ._routeOwnCompSet=comps;civ._routeOwnCompRouteVersion=routeVersion;civ._routeOwnCompTerritoryVersion=territoryVersion;return comps;
}
function _botReachStamp(civ,enemy){return _routeLegalStamp(civ.id)+':' +(civ._territoryVersion||1)+':' +(enemy?enemy._territoryVersion||1:0);}
function _botSetEnemyReach(civ,enemy,reachable){
  const cache=civ._routeEnemyReachCache||(civ._routeEnemyReachCache=new Map());
  cache.set(enemy.id,{stamp:_botReachStamp(civ,enemy),reachable:!!reachable});
}
function _botHasReachableEnemyTerritory(civ,enemy){
  if(!civ||!enemy||!civ.ownedProvs.size||!enemy.ownedProvs.size)return false;
  const cache=civ._routeEnemyReachCache||(civ._routeEnemyReachCache=new Map()),stamp=_botReachStamp(civ,enemy),cached=cache.get(enemy.id);
  if(cached&&cached.stamp===stamp)return cached.reachable;
  const comps=_botOwnRouteComponents(civ);
  for(const pid of enemy.ownedProvs){const c=_routeComponentId(pid,civ.id,true);if(c>=0&&comps.has(c)){cache.set(enemy.id,{stamp,reachable:true});return true;}}
  cache.set(enemy.id,{stamp,reachable:false});return false;
}
function _botNearestEnemyTarget(civ,enemyCivId){
  const enemy=civs[enemyCivId];if(!_botHasReachableEnemyTerritory(civ,enemy))return -1;
  _ensureBotPathBuffers();const stamp=_botNextPathStamp();let qh=0,qt=0;
  for(const pid of civ.ownedProvs){if(pid<0||pid>=provinces.length||_botPathSeen[pid]===stamp)continue;_botPathSeen[pid]=stamp;_botPathQueue[qt++]=pid;}
  while(qh<qt){const cur=_botPathQueue[qh++];for(let ai=_adjBegin(cur),ae=_adjEnd(cur);ai<ae;ai++){
    const n=_adjList[ai];if(_botPathSeen[n]===stamp)continue;
    if(provOwner[n]===enemyCivId)return n;
    if(!_routeProvinceLegal(n,civ.id,true))continue;
    _botPathSeen[n]=stamp;_botPathQueue[qt++]=n;
  }}
  _botSetEnemyReach(civ,enemy,false);return -1;
}
function _botFrontTargetsForEnemy(civ,enemyCivId){
  const enemy=civs[enemyCivId],ownVersion=civ._territoryVersion||1,enemyVersion=enemy?enemy._territoryVersion||1:0;
  const cache=civ._botFrontTargetCache||(civ._botFrontTargetCache=new Map());
  const row=cache.get(enemyCivId);if(row&&row.ownVersion===ownVersion&&row.enemyVersion===enemyVersion)return row.targets;
  const targets=[];
  for(const pid of civ.ownedProvs){for(let ai=_adjBegin(pid),ae=_adjEnd(pid);ai<ae;ai++){const n=_adjList[ai];if(provOwner[n]===enemyCivId)targets.push(n);}}
  cache.set(enemyCivId,{ownVersion,enemyVersion,targets});return targets;
}
function botLaunchAttack(civ, enemyCivId){
  const enemy=civs[enemyCivId]; if(!enemy||!enemy.ownedProvs.size) return;

  // Reject disconnected/impossible wars BEFORE scanning this country's entire
  // border. Once cached, repeated bot-vs-bot attempts are constant-time until
  // ownership/war/alliance topology actually changes.
  if(!_botHasReachableEnemyTerritory(civ,enemy))return;

  const cachedTargets=_botFrontTargetsForEnemy(civ,enemyCivId);
  let targets=cachedTargets;

  // Overseas/non-adjacent but legally reachable war: one multi-source BFS finds
  // the nearest enemy province. Impossible cases were already rejected above.
  if(targets.length===0){const target=_botNearestEnemyTarget(civ,enemyCivId);if(target<0)return;targets=[target];}

  for(const div of civ.foxes){
    if(div.path.length>0) continue;

    if(provOwner[div.provId]===enemyCivId){
      if(allDivsInProv(div.provId,enemyCivId).length===0)civClaimProvince(div.provId,civ.id);
    }

    let tgt=null;
    if(targets.length===1){if(targets[0]!==div.provId)tgt=targets[0];}
    else{
      const r=Math.floor(Math.random()*targets.length);
      for(let i=0;i<targets.length;i++){const t=targets[(r+i)%targets.length];if(t!==div.provId){tgt=t;break;}}
    }
    if(tgt===null) continue;

    const path=_botStrictPath(div.provId,tgt,civ.id);
    if(path&&path.length>1) div.path=path.slice(1);
  }
}

function botSpawnDivisions(civ,atWar=false,spawnDelay=0){
  if(civ.id===playerCivId) return;
  const cap=botDivCap(civ);
  if(civ.foxes.length>=cap) return;
  if(!spawnDelay)spawnDelay=Math.max(24,Math.round((atWar?80:350)*_botDiff().spawn));
  if(++civ.spawnTick < spawnDelay) return;
  civ.spawnTick=0;
  const tier=DIV_TIERS[0],r=civ.res;
  if(r.food<tier.cost.food||r.wood<tier.cost.wood||r.stone<tier.cost.stone||r.coins<tier.cost.coins) return;
  r.food-=tier.cost.food;r.wood-=tier.cost.wood;r.stone-=tier.cost.stone;r.coins-=tier.cost.coins;
  const ownCount=civ.ownedProvs.size;if(!ownCount)return;
  // Preserve the old one-random-draw uniform fallback without allocating an Array of
  // every owned province on each spawn attempt. Large empires can have hundreds of
  // provinces, so this removes recurring GC pressure while keeping spawn semantics.
  let pick=(Math.random()*ownCount)|0,spawnPid=-1,i=0;
  for(const pid of civ.ownedProvs){if(i++===pick){spawnPid=pid;break;}}
  outer:for(const pid of civ.ownedProvs){for(let ai=_adjBegin(pid),ae=_adjEnd(pid);ai<ae;ai++){const n=_adjList[ai];if(provOwner[n]>=0&&provOwner[n]!==civ.id){spawnPid=pid;break outer;}}}
  const d=new Division(civ.id,spawnPid);d.size=tier.size;civ.foxes.push(d);civ._botGroupDirty=true;_indexAddDiv(d);
}

// ── BOT VISUAL GROUPING ONLY ──────────────────────────────────────
// Bots never merge Division objects. Co-located units are collapsed only by the
// renderer, so every unit keeps its own HP, ID, movement state and AI choice.
function botAutoGroup(civ,atWar=false){
  if(civ)civ._botGroupDirty=false;
}

function _botNormalizeWarStacks(civ){
  const base=Math.max(1,DIV_TIERS[0]?.size||1),add=[];let changed=false;
  for(const d of [...civ.foxes]){
    if(!d||d.size<=base+1e-9)continue;
    const ratio=Math.max(0.01,Math.min(1,d.hp/Math.max(1,d.maxHp))),parts=[];let remaining=d.size;
    while(remaining>base+1e-9){parts.push(base);remaining-=base;}if(remaining>1e-9)parts.push(remaining);
    if(parts.length<=1)continue;changed=true;
    d.path=[];d.moveProgress=0;d.size=parts[0];d.hp=ratio*d.maxHp;d._botPlanRole='';d._botPlanTargetPid=-1;
    for(let i=1;i<parts.length;i++){
      const nd=new Division(civ.id,d.provId);nd.size=parts[i];nd.hp=ratio*nd.maxHp;nd._botPlanRole='';nd._botPlanTargetPid=-1;add.push(nd);
    }
  }
  if(add.length){for(const d of add){civ.foxes.push(d);_indexAddDiv(d);}civ._botTacticalDirty=true;civ._armyPlanDirty=true;}
  return changed;
}

// ── BOT BORDER / FRONTLINE PLANNER ─────────────────────────────────
// Bots now use the same strategic idea as the player's AUTO MOVE + BORDER tools:
//   • peacetime: automatically guard their entire national land border
//   • wartime: all armies advance across reachable enemy fronts
//   • never trespass neutral countries while routing
// National-border mode intentionally includes unclaimed land edges, matching the
// player's "Guard Own Country Border" command. Sea/coast edges are excluded here.
function botBorderProvs(civ,warEnemies){
  const borders=_getBotLandBorders(civ);if(!warEnemies)return borders;if(!warEnemies.length)return [];
  const n=civs.length;if(_botThreatMarks.length<n)_botThreatMarks=new Uint32Array(Math.max(n,_botThreatMarks.length*2||256));
  let stamp=(++_botThreatStamp)>>>0;if(stamp===0){_botThreatMarks.fill(0);_botThreatStamp=stamp=1;}
  for(const id of warEnemies)if(id>=0&&id<n)_botThreatMarks[id]=stamp;
  const result=[];
  for(const pid of borders){for(let ai=_adjBegin(pid),ae=_adjEnd(pid);ai<ae;ai++){const o=provOwner[_adjList[ai]];if(o>=0&&_botThreatMarks[o]===stamp){result.push(pid);break;}}}
  return result;
}

let _botPathPrev=new Int32Array(0),_botPathQueue=new Int32Array(0),_botPathSeen=new Uint32Array(0),_botPathStamp=0;
function _ensureBotPathBuffers(){
  const n=provinces.length;if(_botPathPrev.length===n)return;
  _botPathPrev=new Int32Array(n);_botPathQueue=new Int32Array(n);_botPathSeen=new Uint32Array(n);_botPathStamp=0;
}
function _botNextPathStamp(){let s=(++_botPathStamp)>>>0;if(s===0){_botPathSeen.fill(0);_botPathStamp=s=1;}return s;}
function _botStrictPath(startPid,endPid,civId){
  if(startPid===endPid)return [startPid];_ensureBotPathBuffers();
  function bfs(allowSea){
    if(!_routeCanReach(startPid,endPid,civId,allowSea))return null;
    const stamp=_botNextPathStamp();let qh=0,qt=0;_botPathQueue[qt++]=startPid;_botPathSeen[startPid]=stamp;_botPathPrev[startPid]=-1;
    while(qh<qt){const cur=_botPathQueue[qh++];for(let ai=_adjBegin(cur),ae=_adjEnd(cur);ai<ae;ai++){
      const v=_adjList[ai];if(_botPathSeen[v]===stamp||!_routeProvinceLegal(v,civId,allowSea))continue;
      _botPathSeen[v]=stamp;_botPathPrev[v]=cur;
      if(v===endPid){let len=1,x=v;while(x!==startPid){x=_botPathPrev[x];if(x<0)return null;len++;}const path=new Array(len);x=v;for(let i=len-1;i>=0;i--){path[i]=x;if(i)x=_botPathPrev[x];}return path;}
      _botPathQueue[qt++]=v;
    }}return null;
  }
  // Component checks make repeated impossible bot invasions effectively O(1).
  return bfs(false)||bfs(true);
}
function _botNearestOwnedLand(startPid,civId){
  _ensureBotPathBuffers();const stamp=_botNextPathStamp();let qh=0,qt=0;_botPathQueue[qt++]=startPid;_botPathSeen[startPid]=stamp;
  while(qh<qt){const cur=_botPathQueue[qh++];for(let ai=_adjBegin(cur),ae=_adjEnd(cur);ai<ae;ai++){const v=_adjList[ai];if(_botPathSeen[v]===stamp)continue;_botPathSeen[v]=stamp;const p=provinces[v];if(p&&!p.isSea&&provOwner[v]===civId)return v;_botPathQueue[qt++]=v;}}
  return null;
}

function _botMinDistToTargets(div,targets){
  let best=1e12;
  for(const pid of targets) best=Math.min(best,_provPlanDist(div.provId,pid));
  return best;
}

// Pick stable, maximally-spaced guard anchors when there are fewer divisions than
// border provinces. This avoids the old nearest-first clustering that left huge gaps.
function _evenCoverageTargets(lines,count){
  const clean=lines.filter(a=>a&&a.length);if(count<=0||!clean.length)return[];
  const total=clean.reduce((n,a)=>n+a.length,0);if(count>=total)return clean.flat();
  const alloc=new Int32Array(clean.length);
  // If possible, every disconnected land-border component gets at least one guard.
  let used=0;if(count>=clean.length){for(let i=0;i<clean.length;i++){alloc[i]=1;used++;}}
  else{
    const order=clean.map((a,i)=>({i,n:a.length})).sort((a,b)=>b.n-a.n||a.i-b.i);
    for(let k=0;k<count;k++){alloc[order[k].i]=1;used++;}
  }
  // Remaining slots go to the component with the worst current spacing ratio.
  while(used<count){let bi=-1,bs=-1;for(let i=0;i<clean.length;i++){if(alloc[i]>=clean[i].length)continue;const score=clean[i].length/(alloc[i]+1);if(score>bs){bs=score;bi=i;}}if(bi<0)break;alloc[bi]++;used++;}
  const out=[];
  for(let li=0;li<clean.length;li++){
    const line=clean[li],need=alloc[li];if(!need)continue;if(need>=line.length){out.push(...line);continue;}
    // Farthest-point sampling on province centres spreads units across the shape,
    // regardless of BFS ordering around irregular borders. Deterministic tie-break by pid.
    const first=line.reduce((a,b)=>a<b?a:b),chosen=[first],chosenSet=new Set(chosen);
    // Incremental nearest-anchor distances preserve the exact farthest-point result
    // without rechecking every previously chosen guard on every candidate: O(N*K),
    // not O(N*K²), which matters for large borders on the potato-PC target.
    const nearest=new Float64Array(line.length);
    for(let i=0;i<line.length;i++)nearest[i]=line[i]===first?-1:_provPlanDist(line[i],first);
    while(chosen.length<need){let bestPid=-1,bestD=-1,bestIdx=-1;for(let i=0;i<line.length;i++){const pid=line[i],md=nearest[i];if(md<0||chosenSet.has(pid))continue;if(md>bestD||(md===bestD&&(bestPid<0||pid<bestPid))){bestD=md;bestPid=pid;bestIdx=i;}}if(bestPid<0)break;chosen.push(bestPid);chosenSet.add(bestPid);nearest[bestIdx]=-1;for(let i=0;i<line.length;i++){if(nearest[i]<0)continue;const d=_provPlanDist(line[i],bestPid);if(d<nearest[i])nearest[i]=d;}}
    out.push(...chosen);
  }
  return out;
}

// Balanced assignment across disconnected border/front components AND across
// provinces inside each component. Existing valid assignments are preserved so
// the whole army does not reshuffle every planning pulse.
function _botAssignBalancedPlan(civ,divs,targets,role){
  if(!divs.length)return;
